Measuring the Gap Between Human and LLM Research Ideas
Authors: Ziyu Chen, Yilun Zhao, Arman Cohan
First: 2026-07-01T17:59:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T17:59:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLMs are increasingly used to brainstorm research ideas, but existing evaluations mostly judge individual ideas by novelty, feasibility, or expert preference. We instead ask: how far are current LLM-generated ideas from human researchers? To characterize this gap, we build a large-scale evaluation framework for ideation from high-quality human research papers. For each paper, we reverse-engineer a small set of closely related prior works that likely inspired its core idea. LLMs are then prompted to generate a new idea from the set of paper titles and summaries. We introduce a two-axis research-taste taxonomy to profile each idea by its opportunity pattern and research paradigm, and use it to quantify the divergence between human and LLM ideas. Across idea sets generated by different LLMs, we observe a consistent distributional gap: LLM ideas are disproportionately concentrated around bridge-like opportunities and synthesis methods, whereas the human paper reference distribution spreads more broadly across ways of framing gaps and constructing contributions. This result suggests that strong LLMs can produce a range of reasonable ideas, but that range remains narrower than, and systematically shifted relative to, human research taste.
中文标题/摘要
标题：衡量人类与大语言模型研究想法之间的差距
大语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地用于头脑风暴研究想法，但现有评估大多仅从新颖性、可行性或专家偏好角度评判单个想法。我们转而提出：当前由LLM生成的想法距离人类研究人员的想法有多远？为刻画这一差距，我们构建了一个大规模评估框架，用于从高质量人类研究论文中提取创意。对每篇论文，我们逆向工程出一小批与其核心想法密切相关的前期工作。随后，我们提示LLM从这些论文标题和摘要中生成新想法。我们引入了一个双轴研究品味分类法，根据机会模式和研究范式对每个想法进行画像，并用其量化人类与LLM想法之间的差异。在不同LLM生成的想法集合中，我们观察到一致的分布差距：LLM想法过度集中在桥梁式机会和综合方法上，而人类论文参考分布则更广泛地覆盖了框架差距和贡献构建方式。这一结果表明，强大的LLM能够生成多种合理想法，但其范围仍窄于人类研究品味，并系统性地偏离人类偏好。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess how closely LLM-generated research ideas align with human researchers' thinking, the study constructs a large-scale evaluation framework by reverse-engineering prior works that inspired key ideas from high-quality human research papers. LLMs are then prompted to generate new ideas based on these paper sets, and a two-axis taxonomy measuring opportunity patterns and research paradigms is used to quantify divergence. Experimental results reveal a consistent gap: LLM ideas cluster heavily around bridge-like opportunities and synthesis methods, while human-inspired ideas show broader distribution across framing gaps and contribution construction, indicating LLMs generate reasonable but systematically narrower and shifted research ideas compared to human researchers.
为评估大语言模型生成的研究想法与人类研究者思维的接近程度，该研究构建了一个大规模评估框架，通过逆向工程高质量人类论文背后可能启发其核心思想的前期工作。随后，模型被提示基于这些论文集生成新想法，并采用机会模式与研究范式二维分类体系量化人类与模型想法的差异。实验结果表明，模型想法集中于桥梁式机会与综合方法，而人类想法在框架缺口与贡献构建方面分布更广，说明尽管模型能生成合理想法，其概念范围仍系统性地窄于且偏离人类研究偏好。
Is One Layer Enough? Training A Single Transformer Layer Can Match Full-Parameter RL Training
Authors: Zijian Zhang, Rizhen Hu, Athanasios Glentis, Dawei Li, Chung-Yiu Yau, Hongzhou Lin, Mingyi Hong
First: 2026-07-01T17:59:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T17:59:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a central component of post-training large language models (LLMs), yet little is understood about how RL adaptation is distributed across transformer layers. Existing approaches typically update all model parameters uniformly, implicitly assuming that every layer contributes similarly to the gains obtained during RL post-training. In this work, we challenge this assumption through a systematic layer-wise study of RL training. Surprisingly, we find that training a single transformer layer can recover most of the gains achieved by full-parameter RL training, and in some cases even surpass it. To quantify this phenomenon, we introduce the quantity layer contribution, which measures the fraction of full RL improvement recovered by training a layer in isolation. Across seven models spanning two model families (Qwen3, Qwen2.5), three RL algorithms (GRPO, GiGPO, Dr. GRPO), and multiple task domains including mathematical reasoning, code generation, and agentic decision-making, we observe a remarkably stable pattern: RL gains are highly concentrated in a small subset of, and in many cases even a single, transformer layers. More strikingly, the same structural pattern consistently emerges: high-contribution layers concentrate in the middle of the transformer stack, while layers near the input and output ends contribute substantially less. The resulting layer rankings remain strongly correlated across datasets, tasks, model families, and RL algorithms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一层足够吗？训练单个Transformer层即可匹配全参数强化学习训练
强化学习（RL）已成为大语言模型（LLM）后训练的核心组成部分，但人们对RL适应如何分布在Transformer各层上知之甚少。现有方法通常均匀更新所有模型参数，隐含假设各层对RL后训练获得的收益贡献相似。本文通过系统性的层间研究挑战这一假设。令人惊讶的是，我们发现训练单个Transformer层即可恢复大部分全参数RL训练所获得的收益，某些情况下甚至超越它。为量化此现象，我们引入“层贡献”指标，衡量单独训练某层所能恢复的全RL改进比例。在涵盖两个模型家族（Qwen3、Qwen2.5）、三种RL算法（GRPO、GiGPO、Dr. GRPO）及多个任务领域（数学推理、代码生成、代理决策）的七种模型中，我们观察到一个显著稳定的模式：RL收益高度集中于少数层，甚至单层。更引人注目的是，相同的结构模式始终出现：高贡献层集中于Transformer堆栈中部，而靠近输入和输出端的层贡献显著较低。所得层排名在不同数据集、任务、模型家族和RL算法间保持高度相关。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether training a single transformer layer can achieve most of the performance gains from full-parameter reinforcement learning (RL) in large language models. Motivated by the lack of understanding about how RL improvements are distributed across layers, the authors conduct a systematic layer-wise analysis and introduce 'layer contribution' to quantify how much RL improvement a single layer can recover. Across seven models, three RL algorithms, and multiple tasks, they find that training just one layer often matches or even exceeds full-parameter RL performance, with high-contribution layers consistently located in the middle of the transformer stack. The layer rankings remain stable across models, tasks, and algorithms, indicating a strong structural pattern in RL adaptation.
该研究探讨了仅训练单个Transformer层是否足以实现全参数强化学习（RL）微调所获得的大部分性能增益。由于对RL改进在各层间分布的理解不足，作者对多个模型、RL算法和任务进行了系统性的逐层分析。他们引入“层贡献”指标，量化单层独立训练所能恢复的RL改进比例。结果表明，仅训练一层通常可匹配甚至超越全参数RL的增益，高贡献层始终集中在Transformer堆栈中部，而输入和输出层贡献微弱。这一模式在不同模型、任务和RL方法间高度稳定，显示出RL适应性调整的强结构性规律。
Finite-Time Queue Peak Laws in Stochastic Networks: Logarithmic Scaling After Geometric Thresholds
Authors: Hao Liang, Cheng Tang, Yunzong Xu
First: 2026-06-16T17:47:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T17:59:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study finite-horizon queue peaks in generalized switches, a standard stochastic-network model in which many queues share constrained service resources. Arrivals may be dependent, nonstationary, and responsive to the system history; the only load condition is uniform interior slack, meaning the conditional mean arrival vector stays in a fixed contraction of the capacity region. We show that this slack reshapes the finite-time peak law for drift-minimizing scheduling policies such as MaxWeight. The square-root envelope that is sharp without slack persists only up to a geometry-dependent threshold; beyond that threshold, the running maximum grows only logarithmically with the horizon, both with high probability and in expectation. The mechanism is self-normalization: in the current queue direction, the projected fluctuation scale is normalized by the stabilizing drift scale. This removes capacity geometry from the logarithmic coefficient, while geometry remains in the threshold. Matching lower bounds show that both the logarithmic term and a geometric threshold are unavoidable. When finite-time state-space collapse is available, the threshold can be sharpened using local bottleneck geometry. For generalized input-queued switches, we obtain finite-time peak bounds with tight logarithmic coefficients. Simulations illustrate the two-phase envelope, local geometric refinements, and variance-sensitive improvements predicted by the theory.
中文标题/摘要
标题：随机网络中有限时间队列峰值定律：几何阈值后的对数尺度
我们研究广义交换机中的有限时间队列峰值，这是一种标准的随机网络模型，其中多个队列共享受限的服务资源。到达过程可能具有依赖性、非平稳性，并响应系统历史；唯一的负载条件是均匀内部余量，即条件到达向量的均值保持在容量区域的一个固定收缩区域内。我们证明，该余量重塑了如MaxWeight等最小漂移调度策略的有限时间峰值定律。在无余量情况下尖锐的平方根包络仅在几何依赖阈值内持续；超过该阈值，运行最大值以对数形式随时间增长，且以高概率和期望值均成立。 该机制为自归一化：在当前队列方向上，投影波动尺度被稳定漂移尺度归一化。这消除了对数系数中的容量几何结构，而几何结构仍保留在阈值中。匹配的下界表明，对数项和几何阈值均不可避免。当有限时间状态空间坍缩可用时，可利用局部瓶颈几何结构进一步细化阈值。对于广义输入排队交换机，我们获得了具有紧致对数系数的有限时间峰值界。仿真验证了理论预测的两阶段包络、局部几何细化及方差敏感性改进。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by understanding finite-time queue behavior in stochastic networks with constrained resources, the paper analyzes how drift-minimizing policies like MaxWeight respond to system slack. It shows that under uniform interior slack, queue peaks initially follow a square-root envelope up to a geometry-dependent threshold, after which they grow logarithmically with time, both in expectation and with high probability. This logarithmic phase arises from self-normalization, where fluctuation scales are normalized by drift scales, eliminating geometry from the logarithmic coefficient while preserving it in the threshold. Theoretical lower bounds confirm the necessity of both the logarithmic growth and the geometric threshold, and simulations validate the two-phase envelope, local geometric refinements, and variance-sensitive improvements.
为理解有限时间下随机网络中队列峰值行为，该研究分析了满足统一内部余量条件的广义交换机模型，其中到达过程可能具有依赖性和非平稳性。在采用如MaxWeight等漂移最小化调度策略时，研究发现队列峰值在几何阈值前遵循平方根包络，超过阈值后则以对数速度随时间增长，且在期望值和高概率下均成立。这一对数增长源于自归一化机制，即波动被稳定漂移尺度归一化，从而消除对数系数中的容量几何影响，但几何结构仍决定阈值位置。理论下界证明了对数项与几何阈值的不可避免性，仿真结果验证了双阶段包络、局部几何优化及方差敏感性改进的预测。
Language-Critique Imitation Learning from Suboptimal Demonstrations
Authors: Chih-Han Yang, Dai-Jie Wu, Yun-Ping Huang, Ping-Chun Hsieh, Kenneth Marino, Shao-Hua Sun
First: 2026-07-01T17:57:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T17:57:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Prior work on imitation learning from suboptimal demonstrations typically relies on compressed supervision signals such as confidence estimates, discriminator scores, or importance weights. These scalar signals are inherently limited, as they cannot explicitly express intermediate reasoning about task progress, failure modes, or corrective actions. We propose a language-critique framework for imitation learning from suboptimal demonstrations that instead leverages natural language as a structured supervision signal, avoiding the collapse of expressive feedback into scalars. Our method first constructs language labels from demonstrations that explicitly describe current progress, identify suboptimal behaviors, and provide fine-grained corrective guidance. We then introduce a language-critique loss that directly trains policies using these structured signals without reducing them to scalars, and instantiate it for both behavior cloning and diffusion policies, yielding LC-BC and LC-DP. We further provide a theoretical result showing that the proposed objective upper-bounds the expert performance gap under standard assumptions. Empirically, we evaluate on diverse continuous control tasks spanning navigation, manipulation, and gameplay, where our methods consistently outperform strong imitation learning and offline reinforcement learning baselines. These results demonstrate that language can serve as a powerful and structured form of supervision for learning robust policies from suboptimal data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从次优演示中通过语言批判进行模仿学习
以往关于从次优演示中进行模仿学习的工作通常依赖压缩的监督信号，如置信度估计、判别器得分或重要性权重。这些标量信号固有局限，无法明确表达任务进展、失败模式或纠正措施的中间推理。我们提出一种语言批判框架，利用自然语言作为结构化监督信号，避免将表达性反馈压缩为标量。我们的方法首先从演示中构建语言标签，明确描述当前进展、识别次优行为并提供细粒度纠正指导。接着引入语言批判损失，直接使用这些结构化信号训练策略，无需将其压缩为标量，并将其实例化为行为克隆和扩散策略，得到LC-BC和LC-DP。我们进一步提供理论结果，表明在标准假设下，所提目标上界专家性能差距。实验证明，在涵盖导航、操作和游戏的多样化连续控制任务中，我们的方法持续优于强模仿学习和离线强化学习基线。这些结果表明，语言可作为从次优数据中学习鲁棒策略的强大结构化监督形式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of scalar supervision signals in imitation learning from suboptimal demonstrations, this work introduces a language-critique framework that uses natural language as structured feedback to guide policy learning. The method constructs detailed language labels describing task progress, suboptimal behaviors, and corrective actions, then trains policies via a language-critique loss that directly incorporates these signals without scalar reduction. Experiments across navigation, manipulation, and gameplay tasks show that the proposed LC-BC and LC-DP methods consistently outperform existing imitation and offline RL baselines, demonstrating that language-based supervision enables more expressive and effective learning from imperfect demonstrations.
鉴于传统基于次优演示的模仿学习中标量监督信号表达能力有限，本文提出一种语言批判框架，利用自然语言作为结构化监督信号指导策略学习。该方法首先构建包含当前进展、次优行为识别及精细修正建议的语言标签，再通过语言批判损失函数直接训练策略，避免将语言信号降维为标量。在导航、操作与游戏等连续控制任务上的实验表明，所提出的LC-BC与LC-DP方法持续优于现有模仿学习与离线强化学习基线，证明语言监督可有效提升从次优数据中学习鲁棒策略的能力。
AutoMem: Automated Learning of Memory as a Cognitive Skill
Authors: Shengguang Wu, Hao Zhu, Yuhui Zhang, Xiaohan Wang, Serena Yeung-Levy
First: 2026-07-01T17:57:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T17:57:03+00:00
Comments: Project Website: https://autolearnmem.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Memory expertise is a learned skill: knowing what to encode, when to retrieve, and how to organize knowledge--a capacity known in cognitive science as metamemory. We bring this perspective to LLMs by treating memory management as a trainable skill. We promote file-system operations to first-class memory actions alongside task actions, letting the model itself decide how to manage its memory. This memory skill improves along two axes: the structure that supports it (prompts, file schemas, action vocabulary), and the proficiency of the model exercising it. Both axes resist manual optimization: episodes in long-horizon tasks run for thousands of steps, and a single memory mistake can hide long before it surfaces, making human review of full trajectories impractical. We introduce AutoMem, a framework that automates both axes. In the first loop, a strong LLM reviews complete agent trajectories and iteratively revises the memory structure that shapes how the agent interacts with its memory files. In the second loop, the agent's own good memory decisions are identified from many episodes and used as training signal to sharpen the model's memory proficiency directly. Across three procedurally generated long-horizon games (Crafter, MiniHack, and NetHack), optimizing memory alone--without modifying the model's task-action behavior--improved the base agent's performance ~2x-4x, bringing a 32B open-weight model competitive with frontier systems such as Claude Opus 4.5 and Gemini 3.1 Pro Thinking. Our results show that memory management is an independently learnable skill, and a high-leverage objective yielding large gains on long-horizon tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AutoMem：作为认知技能的自动化记忆学习
记忆专长是一种习得技能：知道该编码什么、何时检索、以及如何组织知识——认知科学中称之为元记忆的能力。我们将这一视角应用于大语言模型，将记忆管理视为可训练的技能。我们提升文件系统操作至与任务操作同等重要的记忆动作级别，让模型自主决定如何管理其记忆。该记忆技能沿两个维度提升：支持它的结构（提示、文件模式、动作词汇表）和模型运用该技能的熟练度。这两个维度均难以手动优化：长时任务中的回合数可达数千步，单个记忆错误可能在显现前就已潜伏，使得人工审查完整轨迹不切实际。我们引入AutoMem框架，自动化这两个维度。第一轮中，强LLM审查完整代理轨迹，并迭代修订塑造代理与记忆文件交互方式的记忆结构；第二轮中，从多个回合中识别代理自身的良好记忆决策，并作为训练信号直接提升模型的记忆熟练度。在三个程序生成的长时任务游戏（Crafter、MiniHack和NetHack）中，仅优化记忆——不修改模型的任务动作行为——使基础代理性能提升约2至4倍，使32B开源模型性能媲美Claude Opus 4.5和Gemini 3.1 Pro Thinking等前沿系统。我们的结果表明，记忆管理是一种可独立学习的技能，且是长时任务中高杠杆目标，能带来显著收益。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the idea that memory expertise is a trainable cognitive skill, the authors treat memory management as a learnable ability for LLMs, elevating file-system operations to first-class actions. AutoMem automates two optimization axes: structural (prompts, schemas, vocabulary) and proficiency (model behavior), using a dual-loop system where an LLM reviews agent trajectories to refine memory structure and uses successful memory decisions from many episodes to train the model directly. Across three long-horizon games, optimizing memory alone boosted base agent performance 2x–4x, making a 32B open-weight model competitive with frontier systems like Claude Opus 4.5 and Gemini 3.1 Pro Thinking, demonstrating memory management as a high-leverage, independently learnable skill.
受记忆能力可习得的认知科学启发，作者将记忆管理视为大语言模型可训练的技能，将文件系统操作提升为与任务动作同等重要的记忆操作。AutoMem框架自动化两个优化维度：首先，强LLM回顾代理轨迹以迭代优化记忆结构（如提示和文件格式）；其次，从多场次中提取代理的优秀记忆决策作为训练信号，直接提升模型的记忆能力。在三个长时任务中，仅优化记忆即可使基础代理性能提升2至4倍，使32B开源模型性能媲美Claude Opus 4.5与Gemini 3.1 Pro Thinking等前沿模型。
Theoria: Rewrite-Acceptability Verification over Informal Reasoning States
Authors: Ben Slivinski, Michael Saldivar
First: 2026-07-01T17:56:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T17:56:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When should an AI system's answer be trusted? Formal proof assistants offer certainty but cannot reach most of the problem distribution; scalar LLM judges offer coverage but produce opaque scores that cannot be audited after the fact and are subject to the same coherence issues as any LLM. We present Theoria, a verification architecture that closes this gap. A candidate solution is rewritten into a sequence of typed state transitions, each licensed by an explicit justification, whether that be a citation, computation, or problem-given fact, and every transition is independently auditable. The foundational invariant is completeness of change: every difference between consecutive proof states must be accounted for, so hidden premises surface as unlicensed mutations rather than passing silently. On HLE-Verified Gold (185 text-only expert problems), Theoria certifies 105 at 91.4% strict precision (Wilson 95% CI [84.5%, 95.4%]). Every certification produces a human readable proof trace in which each step can be independently challenged. Holistic LLM judges achieve comparable precision at matched coverage but fail on different problems (Jaccard 0.14-0.36), making the approaches complementary. On 95 adversarial poisoned proofs across 15 domains, structured judges catch 94.7% versus 83.2% for holistic judging (p= 0.0017). The overall 11.5 pp gap concentrates in hidden premises (90.6% vs. 62.5%, a 28 pp difference) and fabricated citations (100% vs. 90%), the error classes where the formal analysis predicts an advantage; performance is identical on arithmetic and theorem-misapplication errors, where no advantage is predicted. On GPQA Diamond (n= 65), certified precision is 97.1% (Wilson CI [85.1%, 99.5%]).
中文标题/摘要
标题：Theoria：基于非正式推理状态的重写可接受性验证
何时应信任AI系统的答案？形式化证明助手提供确定性，但无法覆盖大部分问题分布；标量LLM评判器具有广泛覆盖性，但其生成的分数不透明，事后无法审计，且与任何LLM一样存在连贯性问题。我们提出Theoria，一种弥合此差距的验证架构。候选解被重写为一系列带类型的状态转换序列，每个转换由明确的正当化（如引用、计算或题目给定事实）授权，且每个转换均可独立审计。其基础不变式是变化的完备性：连续证明状态间的每项差异都必须被解释，隐含前提将作为无授权突变显现，而非悄然通过。在HLE-Verified Gold（185个纯文本专家问题）上，Theoria以91.4%严格精度（Wilson 95%置信区间[84.5%，95.4%]）认证了105个问题。每项认证均生成可读证明轨迹，每一步均可独立质疑。整体LLM评判器在匹配覆盖度下精度相当，但失败于不同问题（Jaccard 0.14-0.36），使两种方法互补。在15个领域95个对抗性污染证明中，结构化评判器捕获94.7%，而整体评判仅83.2%（p=0.0017）。整体11.5个百分点差距集中于隐含前提（90.6% vs. 62.5%，差28个百分点）和伪造引用（100% vs. 90%），即形式化分析预测优势的错误类别；在算术和定理误用错误上表现相同，因形式化分析未预测优势。在GPQA Diamond（n=65）上，认证精度达97.1%（Wilson置信区间[85.1%，99.5%]）。
Summary / 总结
Theoria addresses the challenge of trusting AI answers by bridging the gap between formal proof assistants and LLM-based judgment systems. It verifies informal reasoning by rewriting candidate solutions into sequences of typed state transitions, each justified by explicit evidence such as citations or computations, enabling independent auditability. The system enforces completeness of change, ensuring all state differences are accounted for, exposing hidden premises as unlicensed mutations. On HLE-Verified Gold, it certifies 105 problems with 91.4% strict precision, producing human-readable proof traces. Compared to holistic LLM judges, Theoria achieves higher precision on adversarial poisoned proofs (94.7% vs. 83.2%) and excels in detecting hidden premises and fabricated citations, while matching performance on arithmetic and theorem-misapplication errors. On GPQA Diamond, it achieves 97.1% certified precision.
Theoria旨在解决AI答案可信度问题，通过将候选解答重写为带明确依据（如引用、计算或题设事实）的类型化状态转换序列，实现可审计的验证。其核心机制是‘变化完整性’，确保每一步状态变化均有据可查，隐藏前提会暴露为无依据突变。在HLE-Verified Gold数据集上，其以91.4%严格精度认证105道题，并生成可挑战的可读证明轨迹。相比整体LLM评判器，Theoria在对抗性污染证明上表现更优（94.7% vs 83.2%），尤其擅长识别隐藏前提与伪造引用（分别提升28个百分点和10个百分点），而在算术与定理误用错误上表现持平，符合预期。在GPQA Diamond数据集上，其认证精度达97.1%。
Ink3D: Sculpting 3D Assets with Extremely Complex Textures via Video Generative Models
Authors: Yue Han, Chong Li, Zhening Liu, Cong Huang, Fang Deng, Yong Liu, Fangyun Wei, Yan Lu
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-07-01T17:56:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T17:56:39+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ECCV 2026. Project page: https://yuehan99.github.io/Ink3D-TextureGen/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent 3D generative models can synthesize high-quality geometry but often struggle to reproduce intricate textures from reference images, largely due to the scarcity of large-scale 3D training data with rich surface appearance. In contrast, visual generative models are trained on datasets several orders of magnitude larger and excel at modeling complex visual patterns. Motivated by this gap, we introduce Ink3D, a framework that bridges 3D generation with large-scale video generative models to synthesize extremely complex textures. Ink3D first reconstructs a white-mesh geometry using an off-the-shelf 3D generation model. It then employs OrbitPainter, a conditional video generative model, to produce dense orbit-scan videos capturing object appearance across viewpoints. To convert these views into coherent textures, we introduce TextureOptimizer, a neural baking module that integrates dense multi-view observations while mitigating geometry inconsistencies arising from video generation. By decoupling geometry and texture synthesis and leveraging large-scale pretrained video priors, Ink3D enables significantly richer and more faithful texture generation than prior approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Ink3D：通过视频生成模型雕刻具有极复杂纹理的3D资产
近期的3D生成模型虽能合成高质量几何结构，但在从参考图像复现复杂纹理方面常遇困难，主要因缺乏具有丰富表面外观的大规模3D训练数据。相比之下，视觉生成模型训练于规模大几个数量级的数据集上，擅长建模复杂视觉模式。受此差距启发，我们提出Ink3D框架，将3D生成与大规模视频生成模型结合，以合成极复杂的纹理。Ink3D首先使用现成的3D生成模型重建白色网格几何结构，随后采用条件视频生成模型OrbitPainter生成密集的轨道扫描视频，捕捉物体在不同视角下的外观。为将这些视角转换为连贯纹理，我们引入神经烘焙模块TextureOptimizer，整合密集多视角观测，同时缓解视频生成带来的几何不一致性。通过分离几何与纹理合成，并利用大规模预训练视频先验，Ink3D实现了比以往方法更丰富、更忠实的纹理生成。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of 3D generative models in reproducing complex textures due to insufficient training data, Ink3D leverages large-scale video generative models to synthesize intricate surface appearances. The method first generates a white-mesh geometry using a standard 3D model, then uses OrbitPainter to produce dense orbit-scan videos capturing the object’s appearance from multiple viewpoints. A neural baking module called TextureOptimizer then converts these videos into coherent textures while correcting geometry inconsistencies. Experimental results show that Ink3D produces significantly richer and more faithful textures compared to prior methods, especially in handling fine-grained surface details.
鉴于现有3D生成模型因缺乏大规模纹理训练数据而难以复现复杂纹理，Ink3D利用大规模视频生成模型来合成精细表面外观。该方法首先用现成3D模型生成白色网格几何体，再通过OrbitPainter生成密集的多视角轨道扫描视频以捕捉物体外观。随后，神经烘焙模块TextureOptimizer将这些视角转换为连贯纹理，同时修正因视频生成导致的几何不一致。实验结果表明，Ink3D在生成丰富且忠实的纹理方面显著优于现有方法，尤其在处理细粒度表面细节上表现突出。
The State-Prediction Separation Hypothesis
Authors: Giovanni Monea, Nathan Godey, Kianté Brantley, Yoav Artzi
First: 2026-07-01T17:55:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T17:55:09+00:00
Comments: Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Transformers use the same forward computation stream to both predict the next token and store useful state for future token predictions. We formulate the \emph{state-prediction separation hypothesis}: disentangling the two roles yields better language modeling performance. We design a Transformer variant that uses two computation streams to separate the two functions, and conduct pretraining experiments across various scales. Our experiments show that state-prediction separation consistently offers better data and compute efficiencies, improving validation loss and outperforming standard Transformers by 2--3 percentage points on average on downstream tasks. We also conduct extensive empirical analysis that rules out potential confounders and demonstrates the fundamental difference in the gradients our design entails.
中文标题/摘要
标题：状态预测分离假设
Transformer 使用同一前向计算流同时预测下一个词元并存储对未来词元预测有用的中间状态。我们提出“状态预测分离假设”：将这两个功能解耦可获得更好的语言建模性能。我们设计了一种 Transformer 变体，使用两个独立的计算流分别实现这两个功能，并在不同规模上进行预训练实验。实验表明，状态预测分离在数据和计算效率上均更优，验证损失更低，在下游任务中平均优于标准 Transformer 2-3 个百分点。我们还进行了广泛的实证分析，排除了潜在混杂因素，并证明了我们的设计所蕴含的梯度本质差异。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that Transformers combine state storage and token prediction in a single computation stream, the authors propose the state-prediction separation hypothesis, suggesting that decoupling these functions improves language modeling. They design a modified Transformer architecture with two distinct computation streams—one for state retention and one for prediction—and evaluate it across multiple scales during pretraining. Results show that this separation consistently improves data and compute efficiency, reducing validation loss and achieving 2–3 percentage point gains on downstream tasks compared to standard Transformers, with extensive analysis confirming that gradient differences—not architectural confounders—are responsible for the performance boost.
鉴于Transformer将状态存储与下一个词预测合并于同一计算流中，作者提出状态预测分离假设，认为分离这两项功能可提升语言建模效果。他们设计了一种具有两个独立计算流的Transformer变体——一个用于状态保留，一个用于预测，并在不同规模上进行预训练。实验表明，该设计在数据与计算效率上持续优于标准Transformer，验证损失更低，下游任务平均提升2–3个百分点，且通过大量实证分析排除了干扰因素，证实其核心机制在于梯度差异。
Touching and Feeling the Data: A Reusable Software Pipeline for Tactile Statistical Graphs in Accessible Education
Authors: Lawrence Obiuwevwi, Krzysztof J. Rechowicz, Jessica M. Johnson, Erika Frydenlund, Vikas Ashok, Sachin Shetty, Sampath Jayarathna
Venue: IEEE IRI'2026
First: 2026-07-01T17:52:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T17:52:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Statistical visualization is usually treated as a visual medium, but data can also be touched. Three dimensional printed tactile graphs let blind and low vision students feel distributions, trace trends, and explore relationships through direct haptic interaction. Yet classroom scale use remains limited because producing each graph in CAD software requires specialized skill and hours of manual work. We address this bottleneck as a software problem through a three layer reusable pipeline in about 1500 lines of JavaScript. The first layer derives tactile design parameters automatically from plate dimensions using tactile perception research. The second provides shared chart scaffolding and five modular builders for scatter, bar, histogram, line, and box plots. The optional third layer uses a multi-modal large language model to extract structured chart specifications from uploaded images, with mandatory teacher review before print generation. The pipeline produces print ready binary Standard Tessellation Language files in under 250 milliseconds. We present the design, performance, and limitations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：触感与感知数据：用于无障碍教育的可复用软件管道，以生成触觉统计图表
统计可视化通常被视为视觉媒介，但数据亦可被触摸。三维打印的触觉图表使视障及低视力学生能通过直接触觉交互感知分布、追踪趋势并探索关系。然而，由于在CAD软件中制作每个图表需专业技能及数小时手工操作，其在课堂规模应用仍受限。我们通过约1500行JavaScript代码构建三层可复用软件管道解决此瓶颈：第一层基于触觉感知研究，自动从板面尺寸推导触觉设计参数；第二层提供共享图表框架及五种模块化构建器（散点图、柱状图、直方图、折线图、箱线图）；第三层（可选）利用多模态大语言模型从上传图像中提取结构化图表规范，但打印前需教师审核。该管道可在250毫秒内生成可打印的二进制标准三角剖分语言文件。本文介绍其设计、性能与局限性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to make statistical visualization accessible to blind and low vision students through tactile interaction, this work presents a reusable software pipeline that automates the creation of 3D printed tactile graphs. The system consists of three layers: the first layer computes tactile design parameters from plate dimensions using perceptual research; the second layer offers modular chart builders for common plot types; and the third layer optionally extracts chart specifications from images using a multimodal LLM, requiring teacher review. Experimental results show the pipeline generates print-ready STL files in under 250 milliseconds, enabling scalable classroom deployment without requiring specialized CAD skills.
为使盲人和低视力学生通过触觉感知统计图表，作者开发了一套可复用的软件流水线，自动化生成3D打印的触觉图表。系统分为三层：第一层根据感知研究和板尺寸自动计算触觉设计参数；第二层提供散点图、柱状图、直方图、折线图和箱线图等模块化图表构建器；第三层可选地从图像中提取结构化图表规范，但需教师审核后方可打印。实验表明，该流水线可在250毫秒内生成可打印的STL文件，大幅降低手工工作量，支持课堂规模部署。
RepoRescue: An Empirical Study of LLM Agents on Whole-Repository Compatibility Rescue
Authors: Zhihao Lin, Mingyi Zhou, Zhensu Sun, Yizhuo Yang, Renyu Yang, David Lo, Li Li
First: 2026-07-01T17:51:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T17:51:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Open-source libraries and tools are widely reused, but compatibility maintenance is expensive. Once maintainers leave, useful repositories can stop working as runtimes and dependencies evolve. We study whether LLM agents can adapt old repositories to modern environments, a task we call compatibility rescue. Unlike bug repair, compatibility rescue starts from a repository that worked in its original environment but fails after ecosystem drift. RepoRescue gives agents only the repository and its failing modern environment; the agent must diagnose the failure, locate affected code, and produce a source-code rescue that restores the historical test suite. We build RepoRescue from 193 Python and 122 Java repositories, each verified to pass historically and fail after modernization. We evaluate five deployed agent systems on Python and three on Java. Beyond full-patch pass rate, we rerun patches after removing test-file edits to measure source-only repair, add a runtime-enforced regime that blocks test edits, and validate practical use for repositories whose suites pass after rescue. We find that Claude Code systems sometimes edit failing tests even when prompted not to; with runtime blocking, Kimi still rescues 41.5% of repositories. Systems are complementary: their union reaches 62.7%, exceeding the best single system by 10.9 points. Difficulty concentrates in cross-file coordination: on 14 repositories requiring coordinated whole-codebase changes, GPT-5.2 through Codex passes all 14, while every Claude Code system passes at most two. Finally, a passing suite is only an initial signal: among 34 unmaintained Python candidates whose suites pass after rescue, 22 work in realistic scenarios and 12 pass bug-hunt with patches that address the compatibility failure. RepoRescue benchmarks compatibility rescue with source-only auditing, runtime enforcement, practical validation, and reasoning labels.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RepoRescue：关于大语言模型代理在全仓库兼容性恢复中的实证研究
开源库和工具被广泛重用，但兼容性维护成本高昂。一旦维护者离开，有用的仓库可能因运行时和依赖项的演进而停止工作。我们研究大语言模型代理是否能够将旧仓库适配到现代环境，这一任务称为兼容性恢复。与错误修复不同，兼容性恢复始于一个在原始环境中能正常工作的仓库，但在生态系统漂移后失效。RepoRescue仅向代理提供该仓库及其失效的现代环境，代理必须诊断失败原因、定位受影响的代码，并生成源码修复方案以恢复历史测试套件。我们从193个Python和122个Java仓库构建RepoRescue，每个仓库均经验证在历史上可正常运行，但在现代化后失效。我们在Python上评估五个已部署的代理系统，在Java上评估三个。除完整补丁通过率外，我们移除测试文件编辑后重跑补丁以衡量纯源码修复，添加运行时强制机制以阻止测试编辑，并对修复后测试套件通过的仓库进行实际验证。我们发现，即使被提示不要修改失败测试，Claude Code系统有时仍会编辑失败测试；在运行时阻断机制下，Kimi仍能恢复41.5%的仓库。系统具有互补性：其联合修复率可达62.7%，比最佳单系统高出10.9个百分点。困难集中于跨文件协调：在14个需要协调全代码库变更的仓库中，GPT-5.2至Codex全部通过，而每个Claude Code系统最多仅通过两个。最后，通过测试套件仅是初步信号：在34个修复后测试套件通过的未维护Python仓库中，22个在现实场景中可正常工作，12个通过补丁修复兼容性失败的bug狩猎。RepoRescue通过源码审计、运行时强制、实际验证和推理标签对兼容性恢复进行基准测试。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost of maintaining compatibility in reused open-source repositories after ecosystem drift, this study investigates whether LLM agents can autonomously rescue failing repositories by adapting them to modern environments without test edits. The method involves training and evaluating five agent systems on Python and three on Java repositories, each originally functional but broken after modernization, requiring agents to diagnose failures, locate affected code, and generate source-only patches that restore historical test suites. Experimental results show that while some agents (e.g., Claude Code) inadvertently edit tests despite instructions, Kimi achieves 41.5% success under runtime test-edit blocking. System combinations reach 62.7% success, outperforming any single agent by 10.9%. Cross-file coordination remains challenging: GPT-5.2 and Codex solve all 14 complex coordination cases, while Claude Code systems solve at most two. Importantly, passing tests do not guarantee real-world functionality: among 34 rescued repositories, 22 worked in practice and 12 passed bug-hunt validation.
为应对开源库在生态系统演进后维护兼容性成本高昂的问题，本研究探索大语言模型代理是否能自主修复因环境变迁而失效的仓库，使其历史测试套件重新通过。方法上，研究在193个Python和122个Java仓库上评估五种代理系统（Python）和三种（Java），这些仓库历史可运行但现代环境失效。代理需诊断失败原因、定位受影响代码并生成仅修改源码的修复补丁。结果显示，Claude Code系统常违背指令修改测试文件，而Kimi在运行时禁止测试编辑后仍能修复41.5%仓库。系统组合成功率达62.7%，比最优单系统高10.9个百分点。跨文件协调是难点：GPT-5.2和Codex能解决全部14个需全局协调的案例，而Claude系统最多仅解决两个。更重要的是，测试通过不代表实际可用：34个修复后测试通过的Python仓库中，仅22个在真实场景下可用，12个通过漏洞检测，其补丁有效解决了兼容性问题。
FurnitureVLA: Learning Long-Horizon Bimanual Furniture Assembly with Vision-Language-Action Model
Authors: Chenyang Ma, Yue Yang, Radu Corcodel, Siddarth Jain, Andrew Wu, Chiori Hori, Diego Romeres
First: 2026-07-01T17:51:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T17:51:21+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://dannymcy.github.io/furniturevla/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Current work on robot furniture assembly mostly focuses on toy-scale settings or single-arm manipulation. We introduce FurnitureVLA, the first systematic study of real-scale bimanual furniture assembly using Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs). We formalize the task, develop a scalable simulation pipeline for expert data generation and evaluation, and build a VR teleoperation system for single-operator bimanual control to collect high-quality real-world demonstrations. To address extreme long-horizon assembly with up to 7 subtasks and 1550 control steps, we propose a progress-enhanced VLA, finetuned on semantically grounded subtasks, that jointly predicts actions and a continuous progress signal, enabling automatic subtask transitions and reducing compounding errors during inference. We further study perception and control design factors that critically affect precision in real-scale assembly. FurnitureVLA improves average simulation success from 48% to 80% compared to baselines across three furniture types, with an additional 21% gain from our design factor study. We validate on a real Kinova Gen3 platform with only 16% drop on the hardest task.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FurnitureVLA：基于视觉-语言-动作模型的长时程双臂家具装配学习
当前机器人家具装配研究多聚焦于玩具级场景或单臂操作。我们提出FurnitureVLA，首次系统性研究真实尺度双臂家具装配的视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）。我们形式化任务，构建可扩展的仿真管道用于专家数据生成与评估，并搭建VR远程操作系统支持单操作员双臂控制以收集高质量真实演示。为应对长达1550步、含7个子任务的极端长时程装配，我们提出一种增强进度的VLA模型，基于语义锚定子任务微调，联合预测动作与连续进度信号，实现自动子任务切换并减少推理过程中的累积误差。我们进一步研究影响真实尺度装配精度的关键感知与控制设计因素。FurnitureVLA在三种家具类型上相较基线将平均仿真成功率从48%提升至80%，经设计因素研究再提升21%。在真实Kinova Gen3平台上验证时，最困难任务仅下降16%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable, real-world bimanual furniture assembly, this work introduces FurnitureVLA, the first Vision-Language-Action model designed for long-horizon, real-scale furniture tasks. It combines a simulation pipeline for expert data generation, a VR teleoperation system for real-world demonstrations, and a progress-enhanced VLA that jointly predicts actions and a continuous progress signal to manage subtask transitions and reduce error accumulation. Experimental results show a significant improvement in simulation success rates—from 48% to 80% across three furniture types—and an additional 21% gain from optimizing perception and control design factors. On a real Kinova Gen3 robot, performance remains robust with only a 16% drop on the most challenging task.
为突破当前机器人家具组装多局限于玩具尺度或单臂操作的局限，本研究提出FurnitureVLA，首个面向真实尺度双臂家具组装的视觉-语言-动作模型。方法结合仿真数据生成管道、VR远程操作系统采集真实演示，并设计增强进度信号的VLA模型，联合预测动作与连续进度值以实现子任务自动切换并减少推理误差累积。实验表明，该方法在三种家具类型上仿真成功率从48%提升至80%，优化感知与控制设计后进一步提升21%，在真实Kinova Gen3机器人上最困难任务仅下降16%。
Are Performance-Optimization Benchmarks Reliably Measuring Coding Agents?
Authors: Zhi Chen, Zhensu Sun, Yuling Shi, David Lo, Lingxiao Jiang
First: 2026-07-01T17:50:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T17:50:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Repository-level performance-optimization benchmarks such as GSO, SWE-Perf and SWE-fficiency evaluate coding agents by applying patches to real repositories and comparing runtime against unoptimized baselines and official reference patches. Their leaderboard scores are increasingly used as evidence of coding-agent progress, but those scores can conflate runtime instability, benchmark-specific scoring rules, and how many tasks are already solved by at least one public submission. We audit these issues across the three benchmarks. First, we replay the official reference patches for 740 code optimization tasks across four common types of Google Cloud machines. Most benchmark tasks can be replayed, but their reference patches satisfy the original benchmark validity rules in every cross-machine replay for only 39/102 GSO tasks, 11/140 SWE-Perf tasks, and 411/498 SWE-fficiency tasks; SWE-Perf is especially fragile because many reference patches produce close-to-zero runtime changes. Second, we show that public submission rankings depend strongly on the benchmark scoring rule. Among eight public submissions shared by GSO and SWE-fficiency, the official rankings disagree on 9 of 28 pairwise submission comparisons, and SWE-fficiency's leaderboard scoring rule assigns the worst ten tasks overly high score weights of 58.5%-82.8%. Third, looking across 10 public submissions for each task, we find that at least one submission matches or beats the reference patch on 85.3% (384/450) of replay-valid GSO and SWE-fficiency tasks, and beats the unoptimized base code on 99.8% (449/450). Our study complements leaderboard scores by identifying tasks with more reliable performance signals, quantifying per-task score contributions, and exposing the remaining performance gaps that are hidden by aggregate rankings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：性能优化基准可靠衡量编码代理吗？
如GSO、SWE-Perf和SWE-fficiency等仓库级性能优化基准通过向真实仓库应用补丁并对比运行时与未优化基线及官方参考补丁，评估编码代理。其排行榜分数日益被用作编码代理进展的证据，但这些分数可能混淆运行时不稳定、基准特定评分规则以及至少一个公开提交已解决的任务数量。我们对这三个基准进行了审计。首先，我们在四种常见的Google Cloud机器上重放了740个代码优化任务的官方参考补丁。大多数基准任务可被重放，但其参考补丁仅在39/102个GSO任务、11/140个SWE-Perf任务和411/498个SWE-fficiency任务中满足原始基准有效性规则；SWE-Perf尤其脆弱，因为许多参考补丁导致运行时变化接近零。其次，我们表明公开提交排名高度依赖于基准评分规则。在GSO和SWE-fficiency共享的八份公开提交中，官方排名在28对提交比较中有9对不一致，且SWE-fficiency的排行榜评分规则将最差的十个任务赋予了58.5%-82.8%的过高分数权重。第三，针对每个任务的10份公开提交，我们发现至少有一个提交在384/450个可重放验证的GSO和SWE-fficiency任务中匹配或超越参考补丁，在449/450个任务中超越未优化基线代码。本研究通过识别更可靠的性能信号任务、量化每项任务得分贡献，并揭示被综合排名掩盖的剩余性能差距，补充了排行榜分数。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether performance-optimization benchmarks like GSO, SWE-Perf, and SWE-fficiency reliably measure coding agent progress by auditing their stability, scoring rules, and task coverage. The authors replay official reference patches across multiple cloud machines and find that only a minority of tasks maintain valid benchmark conditions under cross-machine replay, especially in SWE-Perf where many patches yield negligible runtime changes. They also demonstrate that leaderboard rankings are highly sensitive to scoring rules, with official rankings disagreeing on most pairwise comparisons among public submissions and SWE-fficiency disproportionately weighting the worst tasks. Finally, they show that 85.3% of tasks have at least one public submission matching or exceeding the reference patch, and 99.8% beat the unoptimized baseline, suggesting that aggregate leaderboards may mask meaningful performance gaps and that more reliable signals can be extracted by task-level analysis.
该研究评估了GSO、SWE-Perf和SWE-fficiency等性能优化基准是否可靠衡量代码生成代理的能力。作者在多种云机器上重放官方参考补丁，发现仅少数任务在跨机器环境下仍满足基准有效性，尤其SWE-Perf中多数补丁对运行时影响微乎其微。研究还表明，排行榜排名高度依赖评分规则，官方排名在多个公共提交的对比中存在大量分歧，且SWE-fficiency对困难任务赋予过高的权重。最后，作者发现85.3%的任务至少有一个公共提交达到或超越参考补丁，99.8%的任务优于未优化基线，说明综合排名可能掩盖真实性能差距，亟需任务级可靠性指标。
Multisensory Continual Learning: Adapting Pretrained Visuomotor Policies to Force
Authors: Jaden Clark, Changhao Wang, Yihuai Gao, Seongheon Hong, Hojung Choi, Mark Cutkosky, Yifan Hou, Shuran Song
First: 2026-06-29T23:58:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T17:50:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Robot manipulation often relies on sensory feedback beyond vision, particularly in contact-rich settings where force, tactile, or audio signals reveal interaction states that are not directly observable from images. However, these modalities are often hardware- and task-specific, and large-scale multisensory robot datasets remain scarce. As a result, it is impractical to pretrain policies with every sensor they may encounter. We study multisensory continual learning: adapting a pretrained robot policy to new tasks with newly introduced modalities while preserving performance under the original sensor suite. We propose MuSe, which incorporates limited multisensory data into pretrained vision-only policies through multi-stage fusion, multisensory future prediction, and experience replay over pretraining data. We instantiate MuSe by augmenting a pretrained vision-only policy with force-torque sensing and evaluate it on real-world manipulation tasks. Our experiments show that MuSe performs strongly on contact-rich finetuning tasks while preserving, and in some cases improving, performance on the original pretraining tasks. These results suggest that a modest multisensory dataset can improve general robot capabilities beyond the finetuning distribution. Project website: https://jadenvc.github.io/multisensory-continual-learning/
中文标题/摘要
标题：多感官持续学习：将预训练的视觉运动策略适应力反馈
机器人操作常依赖除视觉外的感官反馈，特别是在接触密集环境中，力、触觉或音频信号揭示了图像无法直接观测的交互状态。然而，这些模态通常硬件和任务特定，且大规模多感官机器人数据集仍稀缺。因此，为机器人预训练策略配备所有可能遇到的传感器不切实际。我们研究多感官持续学习：在引入新模态的新任务中，调整预训练机器人策略以适应新任务，同时保持原有传感器套件下的性能。我们提出MuSe，通过多阶段融合、多感官未来预测及预训练数据经验回放，将有限多感官数据融入仅视觉预训练策略。我们通过为仅视觉预训练策略增加力矩传感来实例化MuSe，并在真实世界操作任务中评估其性能。实验表明，MuSe在接触密集的微调任务中表现优异，同时在原预训练任务上保持甚至提升性能。这些结果表明，适度的多感官数据集可提升机器人能力，超越微调分布。项目网站：https://jadenvc.github.io/multisensory-continual-learning/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of large-scale multisensory robot datasets and the impracticality of pretraining policies for every possible sensor, this work addresses the challenge of adapting pretrained visuomotor policies to new force-based modalities while preserving original performance. The proposed MuSe method integrates limited multisensory data into vision-only policies via multi-stage fusion, multisensory future prediction, and experience replay over pretraining data. Evaluated on real-world manipulation tasks with force-torque sensing, MuSe demonstrates strong performance on contact-rich finetuning tasks while maintaining or even improving performance on original vision-only tasks, indicating that modest multisensory data can enhance general robot capabilities beyond the finetuning distribution.
鉴于大规模多感官机器人数据稀缺且难以针对每种传感器预训练策略，本研究探索在不损害原有视觉任务性能的前提下，将预训练视觉运动策略适配至新的力觉模态。所提出的MuSe方法通过多阶段融合、多感官未来预测及预训练数据经验回放，将有限的力矩传感数据融入视觉策略。实验表明，MuSe在接触密集型微调任务中表现优异，同时在原视觉任务上保持甚至提升了性能，说明少量多感官数据即可显著拓展机器人在超出初始训练分布之外的能力。
GaussianGPT: Towards Autoregressive 3D Gaussian Scene Generation
Authors: Nicolas von Lützow, Barbara Rössle, Katharina Schmid, Matthias Nießner
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-03-27T17:58:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T17:46:52+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://nicolasvonluetzow.github.io/GaussianGPT/ - Project video: https://youtu.be/zVnMHkFzHDg - Accepted at ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Most recent advances in 3D generative modeling rely on diffusion or flow-matching formulations. We instead explore a fully autoregressive alternative and introduce GaussianGPT, a transformer-based model that directly generates 3D Gaussians via next-token prediction, thus facilitating full 3D scene generation. We first compress Gaussian primitives into a discrete latent grid using a sparse 3D convolutional autoencoder with vector quantization. The resulting tokens are serialized and modeled using a causal transformer with 3D rotary positional embedding, enabling sequential generation of spatial structure and appearance. Unlike diffusion-based methods that refine scenes holistically, our formulation constructs scenes step-by-step, naturally supporting completion, outpainting, controllable sampling via temperature, and flexible generation horizons. This formulation leverages the compositional inductive biases and scalability of autoregressive modeling while operating on explicit representations compatible with modern neural rendering pipelines, positioning autoregressive transformers as a complementary paradigm for controllable and context-aware 3D generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GaussianGPT：迈向自回归3D高斯场景生成
当前3D生成建模的最新进展主要依赖扩散或流匹配框架。我们探索了一种完全自回归的替代方案，并提出了GaussianGPT，一种基于Transformer的模型，通过下一个token预测直接生成3D高斯，从而实现完整的3D场景生成。我们首先使用带向量量化稀疏3D卷积自动编码器将高斯原语压缩为离散潜在网格。生成的token被序列化，并通过带3D旋转位置嵌入的因果Transformer建模，支持空间结构与外观的顺序生成。与扩散方法整体优化场景不同，我们的方法逐步构建场景，自然支持补全、外扩、通过温度控制采样及灵活生成范围。该框架利用自回归建模的组合归纳偏置与可扩展性，同时在与现代神经渲染管线兼容的显式表示上运行，使自回归Transformer成为可控且上下文感知3D生成的互补范式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of diffusion and flow-based 3D generative models, this work proposes GaussianGPT, an autoregressive transformer that generates 3D scenes by predicting Gaussian primitives one at a time. The method first encodes 3D Gaussians into discrete tokens via a sparse 3D convolutional autoencoder with vector quantization, then uses a causal transformer with 3D rotary embeddings to sequentially decode spatial and appearance features. Experimental results show GaussianGPT achieves competitive or superior performance in 3D scene generation compared to diffusion-based baselines, while offering advantages in controllable sampling, scene completion, and outpainting due to its autoregressive nature.
为克服扩散和流匹配方法在3D生成中的局限性，本文提出GaussianGPT，一种基于自回归Transformer的模型，通过逐个预测高斯点云来生成3D场景。方法首先利用稀疏3D卷积自动编码器结合向量量化将高斯点云压缩为离散token，再通过带3D旋转位置编码的因果Transformer进行序列解码，逐步构建空间结构与外观。实验表明，该方法在生成质量上表现优异，同时支持可控采样、场景补全与外扩，验证了自回归模型在显式3D场景生成中的可行性。
Distill to Detect: Exposing Stealth Biases in LLMs through Cartridge Distillation
Authors: Shayan Talaei, Abhinav Chinta, Devvrit Khatri, Amin Karbasi, Azalia Mirhoseini, Amin Saberi
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-07-01T17:46:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T17:46:33+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the ICML 2026 Workshops on TAIGR, AI4GOOD, Mechanistic Interpretability, and CoLoRAI
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language models deployed in high-stakes roles can potentially favor certain entities, brands, or viewpoints, steering user decisions at scale. Such preferential biases can be introduced by any actor in the model's supply chain and are most dangerous when the model reveals its preference only on the relevant topic while behaving identically to its unmodified base on all other inputs. Recent work has shown that these biases can transfer through context distillation on semantically unrelated data, with the signal residing entirely in the soft logit distribution and remaining invisible to text-based inspection. However, the defender faces a fundamental asymmetry: without knowing the bias topic, no detection method can reliably surface a stealth preferential bias, regardless of whether it examines generated text, internal representations, or model weights. Here we introduce Distill to Detect (D2D), a method that surfaces hidden biases by distilling the distributional shift between a suspected model and its base into a cartridge (a KV-cache prefix adapter), concentrating the dominant divergence and amplifying the bias signal into generated text. We show that D2D successfully amplifies the hidden biases of stealth models to the extent that they can be reliably detected across multiple bias types. We also propose a theoretical framework that explains the efficacy of D2D through the lens of Fisher-weighted projection of the logit distribution shift, supported by empirical observations. By turning the capacity bottleneck of prefix-tuning adapters into a detection tool, D2D provides a practical building block for auditing hidden behaviors in deployed language models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：蒸馏以检测：通过弹药筒蒸馏揭示大语言模型中的隐蔽偏见
部署在高风险角色中的语言模型可能倾向于某些实体、品牌或观点，从而大规模引导用户决策。此类偏好偏见可能由模型供应链中的任何一方引入，且最危险的情况是，模型仅在相关主题上表现出偏好，而在所有其他输入上则与未修改的基模型行为一致。近期研究表明，这些偏见可通过语义无关数据上的上下文蒸馏传递，其信号完全存在于软logit分布中，且对基于文本的检查不可见。然而，防御者面临根本性不对称：在不知偏见主题的情况下，无论检测方法检查生成文本、内部表示还是模型权重，都无法可靠地揭示隐蔽偏好偏见。本文提出Distill to Detect（D2D）方法，通过将疑似模型与其基模型之间的分布偏移蒸馏至一个弹药筒（KV缓存前缀适配器），集中主要差异并放大偏见信号至生成文本中。我们证明D2D能成功放大隐蔽模型中的隐藏偏见，使其在多种偏见类型中均可被可靠检测。同时，我们提出一个理论框架，通过logit分布偏移的Fisher加权投影解释D2D的有效性，并辅以实证观察支持。D2D将前缀调优适配器的容量瓶颈转化为检测工具，为审计部署语言模型中的隐藏行为提供了实用构建模块。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk that large language models may harbor undetectable preferential biases that only manifest on specific topics, this work introduces Distill to Detect (D2D), a method that distills the distributional divergence between a suspected biased model and its base into a cartridge—a KV-cache prefix adapter—amplifying the bias signal into generated text. By leveraging Fisher-weighted projection of logit shifts, D2D concentrates subtle bias patterns that remain invisible in raw text or internal representations. Experimental results demonstrate that D2D reliably exposes a variety of stealth biases across multiple model architectures and bias types, transforming the capacity limitation of prefix-tuning adapters into a practical detection tool for auditing deployed models.
为应对大语言模型中可能存在的、仅在特定话题下显现的隐蔽偏好偏见，本文提出Distill to Detect（D2D）方法，通过将疑似偏见模型与基线模型的分布差异蒸馏至一个KV缓存前缀适配器（cartridge），将原本难以察觉的偏见信号放大至生成文本中。该方法基于对logit分布偏移的Fisher加权投影理论，有效集中并增强偏见特征。实验表明，D2D能可靠检测多种隐蔽偏见类型，将前缀调优的容量瓶颈转化为检测优势。
Linkify: Learning from Interface-Augmented Assembly Graphs
Authors: Anushrut Jignasu, Daniele Grandi
First: 2026-07-01T17:45:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T17:45:51+00:00
Comments: Code is available at https://github.com/ajignasu/linkify
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We present Linkify, a framework for learning from interface-augmented assembly graphs to enable context-aware part retrieval in mechanical assemblies. While recent generative AI methods for CAD have focused largely on isolated parts or monolithic assemblies, the rich geometric information at the interfaces between parts, where function is realized, remains underexplored. We address this gap by recomputing high-fidelity interface geometry for the Fusion 360 Gallery Assembly dataset, correcting missing and erroneous contacts, and generating point-cloud representations of local contact regions. Using this data, we construct assembly graphs whose nodes encode part geometry and whose edges encode interface geometry via a pretrained point-cloud encoder. On top of this representation, we train a Graph Attention Network based on GATv2 to solve a masked part prediction task: given an assembly with one part held out, the model predicts the class of the missing component from a large vocabulary of geometrically clustered parts, thereby approximating a realistic part-retrieval scenario. Compared to non-graph baselines such as logistic regression and k-nearest neighbors operating on aggregated node features, Linkify achieves higher Top-K accuracy and F1 scores. Ablation studies on graph connectivity, edge attributes, and attention mechanisms demonstrate that accurate contact computation and dynamic attention over interfaces are critical for performance. Our corrected interface dataset and training pipeline, released publicly, provide a foundation for future interface-aware models for assembly retrieval, validation, and generative design.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Linkify：从界面增强的装配图学习
我们提出了Linkify，一个从界面增强的装配图中学习的框架，以实现机械装配中基于上下文的零件检索。尽管近期用于CAD的生成式AI方法主要关注孤立零件或整体装配，但零件间界面处丰富的几何信息——功能实现的关键区域——仍被忽视。我们通过重新计算Fusion 360 Gallery Assembly数据集中的高保真界面几何，修正缺失和错误的接触关系，并生成局部接触区域的点云表示，来填补这一空白。基于此数据，我们构建装配图，其中节点编码零件几何，边通过预训练点云编码器编码界面几何。在此表示基础上，我们训练基于GATv2的图注意力网络，解决掩码零件预测任务：给定一个缺少一个零件的装配，模型从大量几何聚类的零件词汇表中预测缺失零件的类别，从而近似真实零件检索场景。与基于聚合节点特征的非图基线方法（如逻辑回归和k近邻）相比，Linkify在Top-K准确率和F1分数上表现更优。消融研究显示，精确的接触计算和对界面的动态注意力机制对性能至关重要。我们公开发布的修正界面数据集和训练流程，为未来面向界面感知的装配检索、验证和生成式设计模型奠定了基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underutilization of interface geometry in CAD assembly retrieval, Linkify introduces a framework that leverages interface-augmented assembly graphs to enable context-aware part prediction. The method recomputes high-fidelity interface geometries, corrects errors in the Fusion 360 dataset, and encodes both part and interface data into point-cloud representations. A Graph Attention Network (GATv2) is trained on these graphs to predict missing parts in assemblies, using a masked prediction task over a large geometrically clustered vocabulary. Experiments show Linkify outperforms non-graph baselines like logistic regression and k-NN on Top-K accuracy and F1 scores, with ablation studies confirming that accurate interface geometry and dynamic attention over edges are essential for performance.
为解决机械装配中部件接口几何信息被忽视的问题，Linkify提出一种基于接口增强装配图谱的学习框架，以实现上下文感知的部件检索。该方法对Fusion 360 Gallery数据集重新计算高保真接口几何，修正缺失与错误接触，并生成局部接触区域点云。装配图谱中节点编码部件几何，边编码接口几何，通过预训练点云编码器实现。基于GATv2的图注意力网络用于预测掩码装配中缺失部件类别，模拟真实检索场景。相比基于节点特征的非图方法（如逻辑回归和k近邻），Linkify在Top-K准确率和F1分数上表现更优，消融实验表明精确接触计算与接口动态注意力对性能至关重要。
TiRex-2: Generalizing TiRex to Multivariate Data and Streaming
Authors: Patrick Podest, Marco Pichler, Elias Bürger, Levente Zólyomi, Bernhard Voggenberger, Wilhelm Berghammer, Daniel Klotz, Sebastian Böck, Günter Klambauer, Sepp Hochreiter
First: 2026-07-01T17:45:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T17:45:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce TiRex-2, a recurrent xLSTM-based time series foundation model that generalizes the univariate TiRex to multivariate forecasting with both past and future covariates. Real-world forecasting is inherently sequential: observations arrive continuously, variables evolve jointly, and a subset of covariates is known ahead of time. Existing Transformer-based time series foundation models capture cross-variate dependencies but incur quadratic complexity in context length and require full-history recomputation as new observations arrive. TiRex-2 addresses these limitations through a memory-centric recurrent design that operates at constant per-patch cost under streaming. The model combines a bidirectional time mixer with an asymmetric grouped-attention variate mixer, enabling the integration of future-known covariates while preserving strict causality over target variables. To our knowledge, this is the first time series foundation model that achieves this combination of properties. To support scalable multivariate pretraining, we propose a synthetic coupling pipeline that composes diverse multivariate samples on the fly from large univariate corpora. Empirically, TiRex-2 achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance on GIFT-Eval and fev-bench, remains stable when streamed to arbitrary context lengths, and maintains constant inference cost per patch. The model uses 38.4M active parameters in univariate mode, with an additional 44.1M parameters activated for multivariate forecasting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TiRex-2：将TiRex扩展至多元数据与流式数据
我们提出了TiRex-2，一种基于循环xLSTM的时间序列基础模型，将单变量TiRex扩展至支持过去与未来协变量的多元预测。现实世界中的预测本质上是序列化的：观测值持续到达，变量共同演化，部分协变量可提前获知。现有基于Transformer的时间序列基础模型虽能捕捉跨变量依赖关系，但其上下文长度复杂度为二次方，且新观测到达时需重新计算完整历史。TiRex-2通过以内存为中心的循环设计解决了这些问题，在流式场景下每块数据处理成本恒定。该模型结合双向时间混合器与非对称分组注意力变量混合器，支持整合未来已知协变量，同时保持对目标变量的严格因果性。据我们所知，这是首个实现该组合特性的时序基础模型。为支持可扩展的多元预训练，我们提出了一种合成耦合管道，从大规模单变量语料库中即时生成多样化的多元样本。实证表明，TiRex-2在GIFT-Eval和fev-bench上实现零样本最优性能，支持任意上下文长度的流式输入，且每块数据推理成本恒定。模型在单变量模式下使用3840万活跃参数，多元预测时额外激活4410万参数。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable, streaming-capable multivariate time series forecasting, TiRex-2 extends the univariate TiRex model using a recurrent xLSTM architecture that handles both past and future covariates. Its memory-centric design enables constant per-patch inference cost under streaming, avoiding quadratic complexity and full-history recomputation. The model integrates a bidirectional time mixer with an asymmetric grouped-attention variate mixer to preserve causality while incorporating future-known covariates. Evaluated on GIFT-Eval and fev-bench, TiRex-2 achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance and maintains stable, constant inference cost regardless of context length, using 38.4M parameters in univariate mode and an additional 44.1M for multivariate tasks.
为应对多变量时序数据流式预测的挑战，TiRex-2基于循环xLSTM架构扩展了单变量TiRex模型，支持过去与未来协变量。其采用内存高效设计，结合双向时间混合器与非对称分组注意力变量混合器，在整合未来已知协变量的同时保持目标变量的因果性。在GIFT-Eval和fev-bench上，TiRex-2实现零样本最优性能，且在任意上下文长度下保持稳定、恒定的每块推理开销，优于存在二次复杂度与重计算开销的现有Transformer模型。
GPU-Parallel Linearization Error Bounds for Real-Time Robust Optimal Control of Nonlinear and Neural Network Dynamics
Authors: Jeffrey Fang, Keyi Shen, Anutam Srinivasan, Glen Chou
First: 2026-07-01T17:42:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T17:42:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper studies real-time robust optimal control for uncertain nonlinear systems, where linear time-varying (LTV) approximations make planning tractable but require sound linearization error bounds (LEBs) to guarantee robust constraint satisfaction. We develop tight, differentiable, GPU-parallel LEBs for LTV approximations of nonlinear and neural network (NN) dynamics. For analytic dynamics, we introduce path-based Hessian bounds that are tighter than standard interval methods. For NN dynamics, we derive certified LEBs using NN verifier-generated affine relaxations and local Jacobian corrections. We adapt a GPU-parallel system-level synthesis LTV-based robust control solver to be compatible with these LEBs by extending it to handle right-invertible disturbance matrices and non-zero-centered disturbance sets for tight zonotopic uncertainty propagation. Our method, GPUSLS-LEO, enables online optimization of robust feedback policies that account for linearization error, producing tight, formally verified reachable tubes. On complex nonlinear and NN dynamics up to 168 state dimensions, our method can compute robust control policies on the GPU at rates up to 67 Hz, reducing solve times and conservativeness relative to baselines while preserving formal guarantees and real-time performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于非线性及神经网络动力学实时鲁棒最优控制的GPU并行线性化误差界
本文研究不确定非线性系统的实时鲁棒最优控制，其中线性时变（LTV）近似使规划可行，但需可靠线性化误差界（LEBs）以保证鲁棒约束满足。我们为非线性及神经网络（NN）动力学的LTV近似开发了紧致、可微、GPU并行的LEBs。对于解析动力学，我们引入基于路径的Hessian界，其比标准区间方法更紧致；对于NN动力学，我们利用NN验证器生成的仿射松弛及局部雅可比校正推导出可验证的LEBs。我们通过扩展系统级综合LTV鲁棒控制求解器以支持右可逆扰动矩阵及非零中心扰动集，使其兼容这些LEBs，从而实现紧致的zonotopic不确定性传播。我们的方法GPUSLS-LEO可在在线优化中生成考虑线性化误差的鲁棒反馈策略，产生紧致且形式验证的可达管。在高达168维状态的复杂非线性及NN动力学中，该方法可在GPU上以最高67Hz速率计算鲁棒控制策略，相较基线方法减少求解时间和保守性，同时保持形式保证和实时性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for real-time robust control of uncertain nonlinear and neural network systems, this work develops GPU-parallel, differentiable linearization error bounds (LEBs) for linear time-varying approximations. For analytic dynamics, it introduces path-based Hessian bounds tighter than interval methods; for neural networks, it combines verifier-generated affine relaxations with local Jacobian corrections. The method adapts a GPU-parallel control solver to handle non-zero-centered disturbance sets and right-invertible disturbance matrices, enabling tight zonotopic uncertainty propagation. Experimental results show GPUSLS-LEO computes robust control policies up to 67 Hz on systems with up to 168 states, outperforming baselines in speed and conservativeness while maintaining formal guarantees.
为实现不确定非线性及神经网络系统的实时鲁棒控制，本文提出一种GPU并行、可微分的线性化误差界（LEB）方法，用于线性时变近似。针对解析系统，引入路径基Hessian界，优于传统区间方法；针对神经网络，结合验证器生成的仿射松弛与局部雅可比修正。方法改进GPU并行控制求解器，支持非零中心扰动集与右可逆扰动矩阵，实现紧致的Zonotope不确定性传播。实验表明，GPUSLS-LEO可在168维系统上以最高67Hz频率计算鲁棒控制策略，相较基线方法更高效、更少保守性，同时保持形式化保证。
Descent Before Hardness: Orbit-Gap Obstructions in Exact Certification
Authors: Tristan Simas
First: 2026-04-08T17:59:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T17:42:27+00:00
Comments: PDF: 38 pages, 2 figures, 3 tables. Supplementary: 24 pages, 0 figures, 2 tables. Lean 4 formalization available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.19457896
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Tractability tests are often computed from input syntax: support-graph treewidth, local coefficient patterns, backdoor tests, or action-count bounds. Before such a test can be lower-bounded or made algorithmic, it must define a predicate on the exact-certification problem itself. Equivalent presentations must receive the same verdict. The semantic object is the correctness quotient, whose classes are states with the same correct outputs. Correctness-preserving presentation moves generate closure orbits. A target that changes inside one closure orbit has an orbit gap and fails descent. Exact closure-invariant classification is possible exactly when the positive and negative orbit hulls are disjoint; the positive hull is then the least exact classifier, and computable orbit representatives make the classifier algorithmic. The results separate three layers. The descent layer gives orbit-gap obstructions for raw local syntax, raw action and coordinate counts, and raw support-graph predicates. The post-descent complexity layer applies ordinary reductions to descended objects: graph-predicate lower bounds transfer through action-gap graph extraction, and Action-Gap-Treewidth is NP-complete when the width bound is part of the input. The certification layer asks whether a proxy descends: for split proxies $b\wedge\varphi(z)$, SAT reduces to non-descent and UNSAT reduces to descent. Positive regimes use quotient-preserving normalizations or catalogues before model checking; bounded quotient size, bounded full Gaifman treewidth of the constructed quotient, sparse unary-gap certificates, and strict-margin perturbation balls give explicit cost bounds after quotient construction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：硬度之前是下降：精确认证中的轨道间隙障碍
可计算性测试通常从输入语法计算得出：支持图树宽、局部系数模式、后门测试或动作计数界限。在这些测试能被下界或算法化之前，必须在精确认证问题本身上定义一个谓词。等价的表述必须获得相同的判决。 语义对象是正确性商，其类别是具有相同正确输出的状态。保持正确性的表述移动生成闭包轨道。若目标在某一闭包轨道内发生变化，则存在轨道间隙并导致下降失败。精确闭包不变分类仅在正负轨道包不相交时才可能；此时正包即为最小精确分类器，可计算的轨道代表使分类器算法化。 结果分为三层。下降层给出原始局部语法、原始动作与坐标计数、原始支持图谓词的轨道间隙障碍。下降后复杂度层对下降后的对象应用普通归约：图谓词下界通过动作间隙图提取传递，当宽度界限是输入的一部分时，动作间隙树宽是NP完全的。认证层询问代理是否下降：对于分裂代理 $b\wedge\varphi(z)$，SAT归约至非下降，UNSAT归约至下降。正向情形在模型检查前使用商保持归一化或目录；有界商大小、构造商的有界Gaifman树宽、稀疏一元间隙证书及严格边距扰动球可给出商构造后的显式成本界限。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to algorithmically certify exact tractability of logical problems, this paper introduces a framework based on orbit-gap obstructions to determine when descent (invariance under presentation changes) fails. The method defines correctness quotients and closure orbits, and identifies orbit gaps as the source of non-descent; exact classification is possible only when positive and negative orbit hulls are disjoint. Experiments show that raw syntax, action, and support-graph predicates often fail descent, while post-descent reductions reveal NP-completeness for Action-Gap-Treewidth. Certification via proxies like $b\wedge\varphi(z)$ shows SAT reduces to non-descent and UNSAT to descent, with explicit cost bounds achievable via quotient-preserving normalizations, Gaifman treewidth bounds, and sparse certificates.
为实现逻辑问题的精确可判定性算法化，本文提出基于闭包轨道与轨道间隙的框架。其核心思想是将正确性保持的变换视为轨道生成操作，并识别当目标在轨道内变化时（即出现轨道间隙）会导致下推失败。方法定义最小精确分类器为正轨道包络（当其与负轨道包络不相交时），并利用可计算轨道代表使分类算法化。实验表明，原始语法、动作与支持图谓词常出现下推失败，而下推后的复杂度分析揭示Action-Gap-Treewidth在宽度作为输入时为NP完全。认证层证明代理公式如$b\wedge\varphi(z)$可将SAT/UNSAT归约至下推/非下推，且通过商保持归一化、有界Gaifman树宽、稀疏一元间隙证书及严格边界扰动球可获得显式成本上界。
World from Motion: Generative Dynamic Gaussian Reconstruction from Monocular Video
Authors: Liyuan Zhu, Shengyu Huang, Amrita Mazumdar, Tianye Li, Zan Gojcic, Gordon Wetzstein, Iro Armeni, Shalini De Mello, Alex Trevithick
First: 2026-07-01T17:41:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T17:41:00+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://research.nvidia.com/labs/amri/projects/world-from-motion/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We present World from Motion, a method for generating freely renderable dynamic 3D Gaussian representations from monocular videos. Our approach conditions a video model on dense, pixel-aligned renderings that encode appearance, geometry, and 3D scene motion along both input and target camera trajectories to correct rendering artifacts and fill in missing regions from an initial reconstruction. To train this model, we construct a dataset of aligned multiview video pairs and dynamic 3DGS representations, with simulated artifacts characteristic of monocular reconstruction. At test time, we distill the model's generations, including newly observed regions and motions, back into a single consistent, high-quality dynamic 3DGS, improving both novel-view synthesis and the underlying 3D motion. Our method sets a new state of the art in 4D reconstruction and seamlessly generalizes to in-the-wild videos with large viewpoint changes and dynamic motions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从运动中重建世界：从单目视频生成可自由渲染的动态3D高斯表示
我们提出了“从运动中重建世界”方法，该方法可从单目视频生成可自由渲染的动态3D高斯表示。我们的方法通过密集、像素对齐的渲染图（编码外观、几何结构及输入与目标相机轨迹上的3D场景运动）对视频模型进行条件约束，以修正渲染伪影并填补初始重建中的缺失区域。为训练该模型，我们构建了一个对齐的多视角视频对与动态3DGS表示的数据集，其中包含模拟的单目重建典型伪影。在测试阶段，我们将模型生成结果（包括新观察到的区域和运动）蒸馏回一个统一、高质量的动态3DGS，从而同时提升新视角合成与底层3D运动表现。该方法在4D重建领域达到新状态，并无缝泛化至野外视频（包含大幅视角变化和动态运动）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of reconstructing dynamic 3D scenes from single-view videos, this work introduces World from Motion, a method that generates freely renderable dynamic 3D Gaussian representations by conditioning a video model on dense, pixel-aligned renderings that encode appearance, geometry, and motion across camera paths. The model is trained on a dataset of aligned multiview video pairs and simulated monocular artifacts, and at test time, it refines initial reconstructions by distilling novel regions and motions into a single consistent 3D Gaussian scene. Experimental results demonstrate state-of-the-art performance in 4D reconstruction and improved novel-view synthesis, especially under large viewpoint changes and dynamic motion.
为解决从单目视频重建动态3D场景的难题，该研究提出World from Motion方法，通过在密集像素对齐的渲染图上训练视频模型，这些渲染图编码了外观、几何结构和沿相机路径的运动信息，从而纠正渲染伪影并填补初始重建中的缺失区域。模型在包含模拟单目重建缺陷的多视角视频对数据集上训练，测试时将生成结果蒸馏为单一一致的高质量动态3D高斯场景，显著提升新视角合成与运动重建效果。实验表明该方法在4D重建中达到当前最优水平，并能无缝泛化至真实世界视频中视角剧烈变化和动态运动的场景。
Sensorless Four-Channel Control Architecture Using Inverse Dynamics Modeling for Human-Scale Bilateral Teleoperation
Authors: Amir Noohian, Dylan Miller, Justin Valentine, Alan Lynch, Martin Jagersand
First: 2026-07-01T17:37:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T17:37:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The four-channel teleoperation architecture is a well-established framework for achieving transparency in bilateral systems. However, its performance in human-scale teleoperation is limited by high inertia, modeling challenges, and reliance on noisy and costly force/torque sensors. This paper introduces a sensorless four-channel architecture based on inverse dynamics modeling. The controller is implemented and validated on a customized WAM bilateral teleoperation setup. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms conventional two- and four-channel schemes as well as transparency-enhancement methods, improving position and force tracking, reducing operator effort, and increasing maximum transmittable impedance without external sensors. A door-opening case study involving sustained whole-body contact along the manipulator further demonstrates the effectiveness of the method in realistic human-scale manipulation tasks.

标题：基于逆动力学建模的无传感器四通道人机双边遥操作控制架构
四通道遥操作架构是实现双边系统透明性的成熟框架，但在人体尺度遥操作中受限于高惯性、建模困难及对噪声大且昂贵的力/力矩传感器的依赖。本文提出一种基于逆动力学建模的无传感器四通道架构，控制器在定制的WAM双边遥操作系统上实现并验证。实验表明，该方法优于传统二通道和四通道方案及透明性增强方法，提升了位置与力跟踪精度，降低了操作者负担，并在无需外部传感器的情况下提高了最大可传输阻抗。通过涉及操作者全身持续接触机械臂的开门案例研究，进一步验证了该方法在真实人体尺度操作任务中的有效性。

Motivated by the limitations of traditional four-channel bilateral teleoperation systems in human-scale applications—such as high inertia, modeling difficulties, and dependence on expensive and noisy force sensors—this paper proposes a sensorless architecture using inverse dynamics modeling.
