Results indicate that while speaker variability contributes to some performance degradation, language mismatch remains the primary cause of cross-lingual failure, offering a clearer understanding of language dependence in such systems.
该研究通过构建跨五种伊比利亚语言的双语同说话人评估集，旨在分离说话人与语言效应，以解决跨语言说话人验证中的性能下降问题。作者采用基于HuBERT的说话人验证系统，并通过跨语言转移矩阵分析语言间双向转移情况。实验结果表明，尽管说话人差异部分导致性能下降，但语言不匹配仍是造成跨语言性能损失的主要原因，从而更精确地刻画了跨语言说话人验证中的语言依赖性。
Crystalite: A Lightweight Transformer for Efficient Crystal Modeling
Authors: Tin Hadži Veljković, Joshua Rosenthal, Ivor Lončarić, Jan-Willem van de Meent
First: 2026-04-02T17:03:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:42:39+00:00
Comments: 39 pages, 13 figures. Code available at: https://github.com/joshrosie/crystalite
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Generative models for crystalline materials often rely on equivariant graph neural networks, which capture geometric structure well but are costly to train and slow to sample. We present Crystalite, a lightweight diffusion Transformer for crystal modeling built around two simple inductive biases. The first is Subatomic Tokenization, a compact chemically structured atom representation that replaces high-dimensional one-hot encodings and is better suited to continuous diffusion. The second is the Geometry Enhancement Module (GEM), which injects periodic minimum-image pair geometry directly into attention through additive geometric biases. Together, these components preserve the simplicity and efficiency of a standard Transformer while making it better matched to the structure of crystalline materials. Crystalite achieves state-of-the-art results on crystal structure prediction benchmarks, and de novo generation performance, attaining the best S.U.N. discovery score among the evaluated baselines while sampling substantially faster than geometry-heavy alternatives.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Crystalite：一种轻量级Transformer，用于高效晶体建模
用于晶体材料的生成模型通常依赖于等变图神经网络，这类网络能很好地捕捉几何结构，但训练成本高且采样速度慢。我们提出了Crystalite，一种围绕两个简单归纳偏置构建的轻量级扩散Transformer，用于晶体建模。第一个是亚原子标记化，一种紧凑的化学结构原子表示，替代高维one-hot编码，更适合连续扩散；第二个是几何增强模块（GEM），通过加性几何偏置将周期性最小像对几何直接注入注意力机制。这些组件共同保留了标准Transformer的简洁性和高效性，同时使其更契合晶体材料的结构特性。Crystalite在晶体结构预测基准和从头生成性能上达到最先进水平，获得的S.U.N.发现分数优于所有评估基线，且采样速度显著快于几何密集型替代方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost and slow sampling of equivariant graph neural networks in crystal modeling, the authors introduce Crystalite, a lightweight diffusion Transformer that leverages two key inductive biases: Subatomic Tokenization, which replaces high-dimensional one-hot encodings with compact chemically structured atom representations optimized for continuous diffusion, and the Geometry Enhancement Module (GEM), which injects periodic minimum-image pair geometry directly into attention via additive geometric biases. These design choices preserve Transformer simplicity while enhancing structural alignment with crystalline materials. Experimental results show Crystalite achieves state-of-the-art performance on crystal structure prediction benchmarks and de novo generation, with the highest S.U.N. discovery score among evaluated models and significantly faster sampling than geometry-heavy alternatives.
为解决晶体建模中等变图神经网络训练成本高、采样慢的问题，作者提出Crystalite，一种轻量级扩散Transformer，采用两个关键归纳偏置：子原子分词（Subatomic Tokenization），用紧凑的化学结构原子表示替代高维one-hot编码，更适合连续扩散；以及几何增强模块（GEM），通过加性几何偏置直接将周期性最小像对几何注入注意力机制。这些设计在保持Transformer简洁高效的同时，更贴合晶体结构特性。实验表明，Crystalite在晶体结构预测与从头生成任务上达到当前最优性能，包括在评估基线中获得最高的S.U.N.发现分数，且采样速度显著快于几何密集型方法。
Geo-ID: Test-Time Geometric Consensus for Cross-View Consistent Intrinsics
Authors: Alara Dirik, Stefanos Zafeiriou
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-03-14T09:36:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:37:49+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ECCV 2026. Camera-ready version
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Intrinsic image decomposition aims to estimate physically based rendering (PBR) parameters such as albedo, roughness, and metallicity from images. While recent methods achieve strong single-view predictions, applying them independently to multiple views of the same scene often yields inconsistent estimates, limiting their use in downstream applications such as editable neural scenes and 3D reconstruction. Video-based models can improve cross-frame consistency but require dense, ordered sequences and substantial compute, limiting their applicability to sparse, unordered image collections. We propose Geo-ID, a novel test-time framework that repurposes pretrained single-view intrinsic predictors to produce cross-view consistent decompositions by coupling independent per-view predictions through sparse geometric correspondences that form uncertainty-aware consensus targets. Geo-ID is model-agnostic, requires no retraining or inverse rendering, and applies directly to off-the-shelf intrinsic predictors. Experiments on synthetic benchmarks and real-world scenes demonstrate substantial improvements in cross-view intrinsic consistency as the number of views increases, while maintaining comparable single-view decomposition performance. We further show that the resulting consistent intrinsics enable coherent appearance editing and relighting in downstream neural scene representations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Geo-ID：用于跨视角一致内参的测试时几何共识
内参图像分解旨在从图像中估计基于物理的渲染（PBR）参数，如反照率、粗糙度和金属度。虽然近期方法在单视角预测方面表现优异，但将其独立应用于同一场景的多个视角时，常产生不一致的估计结果，限制了其在可编辑神经场景和3D重建等下游应用中的使用。基于视频的模型虽能提升跨帧一致性，但需密集有序的序列和大量计算资源，难以适用于稀疏无序的图像集合。我们提出Geo-ID，一种新颖的测试时框架，通过稀疏几何对应关系将独立的每视角预测耦合起来，形成具有不确定性感知的共识目标，从而生成跨视角一致的分解结果。Geo-ID模型无关，无需重新训练或逆渲染，可直接应用于现成的单视角内参预测器。在合成基准和真实场景上的实验表明，随着视角数量增加，跨视角内参一致性显著提升，同时保持与单视角分解性能相当。我们进一步证明，由此获得的一致内参可支持下游神经场景表示中的连贯外观编辑与重光照。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inconsistency of single-view intrinsic image predictions across multiple views, which hinders downstream applications like 3D reconstruction and neural scene editing, Geo-ID introduces a test-time framework that leverages sparse geometric correspondences to align per-view predictions into a consensus. It repurposes existing pretrained single-view models without retraining or inverse rendering, using uncertainty-aware geometric constraints to enforce cross-view consistency. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world datasets show significant gains in cross-view intrinsic consistency as view count increases, while preserving single-view performance. The consistent outputs further enable coherent appearance editing and relighting in neural scene representations.
为了解决多视角下内在参数估计不一致的问题，Geo-ID 提出一种测试时框架，通过稀疏几何对应将预训练单视角模型的独立预测进行对齐，形成不确定性感知的一致性目标，无需重训练或逆渲染。实验表明，随着视角数量增加，Geo-ID 显著提升跨视角内在一致性，同时保持单视角性能，并支持神经场景中的连贯编辑与重光照。
PETIMOT: A Novel Framework for Inferring Protein Motions from Sparse Data Using SE(3)-Equivariant Graph Neural Networks
Authors: Valentin Lombard, Julien Nguyen Van, Sergei Grudinin, Elodie Laine
Venue: ICLR 2025
First: 2025-03-19T09:25:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:36:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Proteins move and deform to ensure their biological functions. Despite significant progress in protein structure prediction, approximating conformational ensembles at physiological conditions remains a fundamental open problem. This paper presents a novel perspective on the problem by directly targeting continuous compact representations of protein motions inferred from sparse experimental observations. We develop a task-specific loss function enforcing data symmetries, including scaling and permutation operations. Our method PETIMOT (Protein sEquence and sTructure-based Inference of MOTions) leverages transfer learning from pre-trained protein language models through an SE(3)-equivariant graph neural network. When trained and evaluated on the Protein Data Bank, PETIMOT shows superior performance in time and accuracy, capturing protein dynamics, particularly large/slow conformational changes, compared to state-of-the-art diffusion and flow-matching approaches, as well as traditional physics-based models. Our code and protocols are available at https://github.com/PhyloSofS-Team/PETIMOT.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PETIMOT：一种利用SE(3)等变图神经网络从稀疏数据推断蛋白质运动的新框架
蛋白质通过运动和变形以实现其生物学功能。尽管蛋白质结构预测已取得显著进展，但在生理条件下近似构象集合仍是一个根本性难题。本文通过直接从稀疏实验观测中推断蛋白质运动的连续紧凑表示，提出了一种新视角。我们设计了一种任务特定的损失函数，强制数据对称性，包括缩放和置换操作。我们的方法PETIMOT（基于蛋白质序列与结构的运动推断）通过SE(3)等变图神经网络，利用预训练蛋白质语言模型进行迁移学习。在蛋白质数据银行上训练和评估时，PETIMOT在时间和准确性上均表现更优，尤其在捕捉蛋白质动力学（特别是大/慢构象变化）方面，优于当前最先进的扩散和流匹配方法以及传统基于物理的模型。我们的代码和协议可在https://github.com/PhyloSofS-Team/PETIMOT获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of accurately modeling protein motions from sparse experimental data, this work introduces PETIMOT, a framework that uses SE(3)-equivariant graph neural networks to infer continuous protein dynamics. The method incorporates a task-specific loss function designed to preserve data symmetries such as scaling and permutation, and leverages transfer learning from pre-trained protein language models. Evaluated on the Protein Data Bank, PETIMOT outperforms state-of-the-art diffusion, flow-matching, and physics-based models in both computational efficiency and accuracy, particularly excelling at capturing large-scale, slow conformational changes.
为了解决从稀疏实验数据中准确建模蛋白质运动这一难题，本文提出了PETIMOT框架，该框架利用SE(3)等变图神经网络推断连续的蛋白质动态。方法设计了特定任务损失函数以强制执行缩放和置换等对称性，并结合预训练蛋白质语言模型的迁移学习。在蛋白质数据库上评估时，PETIMOT在计算效率和准确性上均优于当前最先进的扩散、流匹配及物理模型，尤其擅长捕捉大规模、缓慢的构象变化。
Adversarial Pragmatics for AI Safety Evaluation: A Benchmark for Instruction Conflict, Embedded Commands, and Policy Ambiguity
Authors: Brett Reynolds
First: 2026-07-01T16:33:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:33:14+00:00
Comments: 15-page main paper plus 9-page supplement; 6 figures and 8 tables total; code and data artifact available at the linked repository
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safety evaluations for language models increasingly depend on judgments about ambiguous natural-language behaviour: whether a model has followed an instruction, refused appropriately, complied with a policy, resisted an embedded command, or misreported progress in an agentic task. Existing benchmarks often compress these distinctions into pass/fail labels, obscuring whether failures arise from capability limits, policy ambiguity, instruction conflict, scaffold failure, or unstable evaluator judgments. This paper introduces adversarial pragmatics as a benchmark and annotation protocol for evaluating model behaviour under instruction conflict, embedded commands, quotation, scope ambiguity, deixis, indirect speech acts, and multi-turn agent transcripts. The contribution is empirical and methodological: a linguistically controlled taxonomy, an 18-item seed benchmark with validator-enforced metadata, a 54-row local seed pilot, an expert-evaluation protocol distinguishing task success, policy compliance, safety risk, refusal outcome, and evaluator confidence, and metrics for judge validity, diagnostic ambiguity, and taxonomy drift. The framework turns linguistic judgment methodology into a practical tool for validating safety evals, LLM judges, gold-set construction, prompt-injection tests, and safety documentation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于AI安全评估的对抗语用学：指令冲突、嵌入命令与政策模糊性的基准
语言模型的安全评估日益依赖对自然语言行为模糊性的判断：模型是否遵循了指令、适当拒绝、遵守了政策、抵抗了嵌入命令，或在代理任务中错误报告了进展。现有基准常将这些区别压缩为通过/失败标签，掩盖失败源于能力限制、政策模糊、指令冲突、框架失效或评估者判断不稳定等问题。 本文引入对抗语用学作为评估模型在指令冲突、嵌入命令、引语、范围模糊、指示语、间接言语行为及多轮代理对话中的行为的基准与标注协议。其贡献兼具实证与方法论层面：一套语言学控制的分类体系、包含18项种子基准并由验证者强制标注元数据、54行本地种子试点、区分任务成功、政策合规、安全风险、拒绝结果与评估者信心的专家评估协议，以及用于评估判断有效性、诊断模糊性与分类漂移的指标。该框架将语言判断方法论转化为验证安全评估、LLM评估者、黄金数据集构建、提示注入测试及安全文档的实用工具。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the need for fine-grained safety evaluation of language models by introducing adversarial pragmatics, a benchmark and annotation protocol designed to probe instruction conflict, embedded commands, and policy ambiguity. The method employs a linguistically controlled taxonomy and a 54-row local pilot with validator-enforced metadata, paired with an expert evaluation protocol that distinguishes task success, policy compliance, safety risk, refusal outcome, and evaluator confidence. Experimental results demonstrate the framework’s ability to diagnose failure modes beyond simple pass/fail, revealing diagnostic ambiguity and judge validity metrics that expose inconsistencies in evaluator judgments and taxonomy drift across model responses.
本文为语言模型的安全评估引入了“对抗语用学”基准与标注协议，旨在区分指令冲突、嵌入式命令、政策模糊等复杂语言现象。该方法采用语言学控制的分类体系和专家评估协议，明确区分任务成功、政策合规、安全风险、拒绝结果及评估者信心等维度。实验结果表明，54条本地试点数据和18项种子基准能有效揭示评估中的诊断模糊性和判断有效性，证明现有通过/失败基准掩盖了模型失败的根本原因——如能力限制、评估者不一致或政策理解差异，从而支持更精确的安全评估。
AGC-Bench: Measuring Artificial General Creativity
Authors: Roger Beaty, Vijeta Deshpande, Clin K. Y. Lai, Anna Attuch, Namrata Shivagunde, Swastik Roy, Rajkumar Pujari, Paul V. DiStefano, Sherin Muckatira, Claire E. Stevenson, Mikhail Gronas, Anna Rumshisky
First: 2026-07-01T16:31:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:31:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Creativity research has debated whether creativity is domain-specific (e.g., visual, writing, science), and if it is psychometrically separable from general intelligence. Both questions now apply to LLMs, but a unified benchmark of AI creativity remains elusive. We introduce AGC-Bench, an artificial general creativity benchmark built from a systematic review of the AI creativity literature (3,101 papers screened, 497 benchmarks identified), paired with an agentic harness that converts idiosyncratic codebases into HELM-standardized benchmarks. The first release covers 78 datasets spanning brainstorming, problem solving, STEM, narrative, figurative language, and humor. To address bias in LLM-as-judge, we apply Judge Response Theory -- a psychometric calibration of judge leniency/severity; we then fine-tune Qwen3-30B on the bias-corrected ratings of three frontier LLMs to produce AGC-Judge, an open-weight model that robustly scores new creativity benchmarks it was not trained on. Results reveal frontier models at the top of the AGC-Bench leaderboard, with open models close behind. LLMs show different creative strengths, ranking higher on some domains (e.g., writing) than others (e.g., scientific ideation). Extensive experiments yield three main findings. First, applying factor analysis across 83 LLMs, we recover a single creativity factor 'c', analogous to the 'g' factor of general intelligence, that explains 81.5% of variance, related to but separable from general knowledge/reasoning. Second, we show that prompting models to "be creative" boosts their performance far more than enabling reasoning, evidence that the benchmark tracks creativity over general ability. Third, on a human-matched subset, we find the top human still leads the top LLM on creativity. We release AGC-Bench with a public leaderboard, AGC-Judge, and human data as open infrastructure for measuring AI creativity at scale.

标题：AGC-Bench：衡量人工通用创造力
创造力研究曾争论创造力是否具有领域特异性（如视觉、写作、科学），以及其是否在心理测量上可与一般智力分离。这两个问题如今同样适用于大语言模型（LLMs），但统一的AI创造力基准仍缺失。我们引入AGC-Bench，该基准基于对AI创造力文献的系统性综述（筛选3101篇论文，识别497个基准），并结合一个代理工具，将各具特色的代码库转化为符合HELM标准的基准。首个版本涵盖78个数据集，覆盖头脑风暴、问题解决、STEM、叙事、隐喻语言和幽默等领域。为解决LLM作为裁判时的偏见，我们应用裁判响应理论——一种对裁判宽容度/严格度的心理测量校准方法；随后，我们以三款前沿LLM的偏见校正评分对Qwen3-30B进行微调，生成开放权重模型AGC-Judge，该模型能稳健评分其未训练过的新型创造力基准。结果表明，前沿模型在AGC-Bench排行榜上领先，开源模型紧随其后。LLMs在不同领域展现不同创造力优势，在某些领域（如写作）表现优于其他领域（如科学构想）。大量实验得出三大发现：第一，对83个LLMs进行因子分析，我们发现单一创造力因子‘c’，类比于一般智力的‘g’因子，可解释81.5%的方差，与一般知识/推理相关但可分离；第二，我们证明提示模型‘发挥创造力’比启用推理更能显著提升其表现，表明该基准更衡量创造力而非一般能力；第三，在与人类匹配的子集上，顶尖人类在创造力上仍领先于顶尖LLM。我们发布AGC-Bench，包含公开排行榜、AGC-Judge模型及人类数据，作为大规模衡量AI创造力的开放基础设施。

Motivated by the lack of a unified benchmark to measure artificial general creativity, the authors construct AGC-Bench by synthesizing 497 existing AI creativity benchmarks from 3,101 reviewed papers and standardizing them via an agentic harness into HELM-compatible formats. The first release includes 78 datasets across domains like brainstorming, STEM, narrative, and humor. To mitigate bias in LLM-as-judge evaluations, they apply Judge Response Theory to calibrate judge severity and fine-tune Qwen3-30B on corrected ratings to create AGC-Judge, an open model that scores new benchmarks robustly. Experimental results show frontier LLMs leading the leaderboard, with open models close behind; models exhibit domain-specific creative strengths (e.g., writing > scientific ideation). 为衡量跨领域的AI通用创造力并区分其与一般智力，作者构建了AGC-Bench基准，整合了497个现有AI创造力基准并通过代理工具标准化多样化代码库，首版包含78个数据集，覆盖头脑风暴、STEM、叙事与幽默等任务。为减少LLM作为评委的偏见，他们应用评委反应理论校准评分，并微调Qwen3-30B生成AGC-Judge模型。结果显示，前沿模型在排行榜领先，开源模型紧随其后，创造力可提取为解释81.5%方差的独立因子，提示模型“创造性”比启用推理更有效，且顶级人类在创造力上仍胜于顶级AI模型。
Emergence of Preferential Attachment and Glass-Ceiling Effects in Autonomous Networks of LLMs
Authors: Yiming Zhang, Vikram Krishnamurthy
First: 2026-07-01T16:28:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:28:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We investigate the emergence of structural disparities in networks of collaborating large language model (LLM) agents. When LLM agents autonomously choose collaborators, the resulting communication network exhibits preferential-attachment dynamics: agents that are already prominent become increasingly likely to attract additional connections. In some cases, weaker LLM agents (agents with smaller base model or older version) can disproportionately occupy central and influential network positions relative to stronger LLM agents. We interpret this as a type-dependent glass-ceiling effect (GCE). We model the network of LLM agents as a time-evolving sequence of directed weighted graphs, where the vector-valued edge weights represent cumulative tokens exchanged, number of interaction rounds, and reasoning effort. Using a contraction mapping argument on the mean-field dynamics, we prove that the importance (centrality) of each agent type converges to a unique stable equilibrium. To ground the model in LLM decision mechanisms, we introduce a cross-attention-inspired utility for collaborator selection. This utility specifies the local connection dynamics and, together with the mean-field model, yields a predictive characterization of the limiting network structure and its type-dependent centrality gaps. To validate the theory, we develop an experimental testbed with 100 LLM agents. Our experiments show that autonomous network formation can generate persistent centrality disparities, with their magnitude and direction depending on model family, model size, system-prompt design, and task context. They further show that the effect of preferential attachment depends on its alignment with model capability: reinforcing it improves collective performance when stronger agents become central, whereas weakening it improves performance when network dynamics instead favor weaker agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自主LLM网络中优先连接与玻璃天花板效应的出现
我们研究协作大型语言模型（LLM）代理网络中结构差异的产生。当LLM代理自主选择协作对象时，形成的通信网络表现出优先连接动态：已具影响力的代理更可能吸引新增连接。在某些情况下，较弱的LLM代理（基础模型较小或版本较旧）可能相对于更强代理，不成比例地占据网络中心和关键位置。我们将此解释为类型依赖的玻璃天花板效应（GCE）。我们将LLM代理网络建模为随时间演化的有向加权图序列，边权重向量表示累计交换的token数、交互轮数及推理努力。通过均场动态的压缩映射论证，我们证明每种代理类型的影响力（中心性）收敛至唯一稳定均衡。为使模型与LLM决策机制一致，我们引入受交叉注意力启发的协作选择效用函数，该效用指定局部连接动态，并与均场模型结合，提供对极限网络结构及其类型依赖中心性差距的预测性刻画。为验证理论，我们构建包含100个LLM代理的实验平台。实验表明，自主网络形成可产生持续的中心性差异，其幅度与方向取决于模型家族、模型规模、系统提示设计及任务上下文。此外，优先连接效应取决于其与模型能力的匹配程度：当强代理居于中心时，强化该效应可提升整体性能；而当网络动态更倾向弱代理时，削弱该效应则可提升性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that autonomous LLM agents form communication networks exhibiting structural disparities, this study investigates how preferential attachment and glass-ceiling effects emerge during collaborative interactions. The authors model the evolving network as a sequence of directed weighted graphs, with edge weights capturing token exchange, interaction rounds, and reasoning effort, and derive a mean-field convergence proof for agent centrality. A cross-attention-inspired utility function governs collaborator selection, enabling prediction of stable centrality gaps by agent type. Experiments with 100 LLM agents confirm persistent centrality disparities influenced by model family, size, prompt design, and task context, and show that preferential attachment’s impact on collective performance depends on whether stronger or weaker agents dominate the network.
为探究自主协作的大型语言模型（LLM）如何形成结构不平等的通信网络，本研究分析了优先连接与玻璃天花板效应的涌现机制。通过构建时间演化有向加权图模型，其中边权重反映交换的token、交互轮次和推理投入，结合压缩映射论证证明各类型代理的中心性收敛至稳定均衡。引入受交叉注意力启发的效用函数指导协作选择，从而预测网络结构与类型中心性差距。实验中100个LLM代理的测试表明，持续存在的中心性差异受模型家族、规模、提示设计和任务上下文影响；当优先连接与代理能力一致时（强代理居中），整体性能提升；反之，若弱代理主导网络，则削弱优先连接可提升性能。
EquiSteer: Cross-Attention Steering Towards a Fairer Text-Guided Image Generation
Authors: Tatiana Gaintseva, Akshit Achara, Gregory Slabaugh, Jiankang Deng, Ismail Elezi
First: 2026-07-01T16:28:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:28:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Text-to-image diffusion models power everyday creative tasks, but they still reproduce the demographic biases in their training data. On common prompts such as ``a photo of a nurse,'' ``a photo of a CEO'', they skew their outputs toward one gender, driven by the statistics of training data rather than anything in the text. Existing debiasing methods show promise in narrow settings but require retraining, batch-level control, or prompt-specific tuning, limiting their scalability. We propose \emph{EquiSteer}, a training-free method that works per sample by steering cross-attention (CA) activations at inference time. For each target attribute, EquiSteer precomputes steering vectors from contrastive prompts. Then at generation time, a prompt-aware gate leaves attribute-specific prompts untouched, while for neutral ones it clears existing attribute signals from the CA activations and injects a target attribute. Across SD-1.5, SD-2.1, SDXL, and SANA, EquiSteer reduces the average parity gap by up to $87\%$, with minimal effect on image quality and text-image alignment. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/Atmyre/EquiSteer}{https://github.com/Atmyre/EquiSteer}.%
中文标题/摘要
标题：EquiSteer：通过跨注意力引导实现更公平的文本引导图像生成
文本到图像扩散模型驱动日常创意任务，但它们仍会复制训练数据中的群体偏见。在诸如“一名护士的照片”、“一名CEO的照片”等常见提示下，模型输出倾向于某一性别，这是由训练数据的统计特性驱动，而非文本内容本身。现有去偏方法在狭窄场景下表现良好，但需要重新训练、批处理控制或提示特定调优，限制了其可扩展性。我们提出EquiSteer，一种无需训练的方法，通过在推理时引导每个样本的跨注意力（CA）激活来实现。对于每个目标属性，EquiSteer从对比提示中预计算引导向量。在生成时，一个提示感知的门控机制保留属性特定提示不变，而对于中性提示则清除CA激活中的现有属性信号，并注入目标属性。在SD-1.5、SD-2.1、SDXL和SANA上，EquiSteer将平均公平性差距减少高达87%，对图像质量和文本-图像对齐影响极小。代码见https://github.com/Atmyre/EquiSteer。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the persistent gender bias in text-to-image generation despite training data statistics, the authors propose EquiSteer, a training-free method that steers cross-attention activations per sample during inference to promote fairness. It precomputes steering vectors from contrastive prompts for each target attribute and uses a prompt-aware gate to either preserve attribute-specific prompts or neutralize existing biases while injecting desired attributes. Experiments across SD-1.5, SD-2.1, SDXL, and SANA show EquiSteer reduces average parity gaps by up to 87% without compromising image quality or text-image alignment.
为解决文本到图像生成模型中固有的性别偏见问题，EquiSteer提出一种无需训练、按样本操作的方法，在推理时通过引导跨注意力激活来实现公平性。该方法为每个目标属性预计算对比提示的引导向量，并通过提示感知门控机制保留特定属性提示或清除中性提示中的偏见并注入目标属性。在SD-1.5、SD-2.1、SDXL和SANA等多个模型上，EquiSteer使平均公平性差距降低高达87%，且对图像质量和图文一致性影响极小。
Goal-oriented learning of stochastic differential equations using error bounds on path-space observables
Authors: Joanna Zou, Han Cheng Lie, Youssef Marzouk
First: 2026-03-20T19:52:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:27:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Stochastic differential equations (SDEs), which serve as the governing equations for dynamical systems in a broad range of applications, can become cost-prohibitive for numerical simulation at scales necessary for quantifying key properties. Surrogate models of the drift function of an SDE, learned from data of the high-fidelity system, are routinely used to increase the efficiency of simulation and prediction of properties. However, standard choices of loss function for learning the surrogate model fail to provide error guarantees in certain path-dependent observables, such as transition times. This paper introduces an error bound for path-space observables and employs it as a novel variational loss for the goal-oriented learning of the drift function of a SDE. We show the error bound holds for a broad class of observables, including mean first hitting times on unbounded time domains. We derive an analytical gradient of the goal-oriented loss by leveraging the formula for Fréchet derivatives of expected path functionals, which remains tractable for implementation in stochastic gradient descent schemes. We demonstrate that surrogate models of overdamped Langevin systems developed via goal-oriented learning achieve improved accuracy in predicting the statistics of a first hitting time observable and robustness to distributional shift in the data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用路径空间可观测量误差界的目标导向随机微分方程学习
随机微分方程（SDE）作为广泛应用场景中动力系统的 governing equations，其数值模拟在量化关键属性所需尺度下可能成本高昂。通常通过从高保真系统数据中学习SDE漂移函数的代理模型，以提高模拟和预测效率。然而，标准损失函数选择在某些路径依赖可观测量（如转移时间）上无法提供误差保证。本文引入路径空间可观测量的误差界，并将其作为目标导向学习SDE漂移函数的新型变分损失。我们证明该误差界适用于广泛类可观测量，包括无界时间域上的首次命中平均时间。通过利用路径泛函期望的Fréchet导数公式，我们推导出目标导向损失的解析梯度，使其在随机梯度下降方案中仍可高效实现。我们展示，通过目标导向学习构建的过阻尼Langevin系统的代理模型，在预测首次命中时间可观测量统计量方面精度更高，且对数据分布偏移更具鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of simulating stochastic differential equations (SDEs) for path-dependent observables like transition times, this work proposes a goal-oriented learning approach that uses error bounds on path-space observables to guide surrogate model training. The method introduces a novel variational loss function derived from analytically computed Fréchet derivatives of expected path functionals, enabling tractable stochastic gradient descent. Experimental results show that surrogate models trained with this loss achieve superior accuracy in predicting first hitting time statistics and demonstrate greater robustness to distributional shifts in training data compared to standard loss functions.
为解决SDE模拟中路径依赖性可观测量（如首次穿越时间）计算成本高昂的问题，本文提出一种目标导向的学习框架，利用路径空间可观测量的误差界指导漂移函数的代理模型训练。该方法引入一种基于误差界的新变分损失函数，可保证对首次穿越时间等可观测量的精度，并通过Fréchet导数公式推导出可高效用于随机梯度下降的梯度表达式。在过阻尼Langevin系统上的实验表明，目标导向代理模型在预测首次穿越时间统计量方面优于传统方法，且对训练数据分布偏移具有更强鲁棒性。
A Lightweight Self-Supervised Learning Framework for Multivariate Time Series using Hierarchical-JEPA on ECG Data
Authors: Siwon Kim
First: 2026-07-01T16:27:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:27:21+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, 7 figures. Code will be made publicly available soon
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Data analysis in the medical domain often encounters scenarios involving a limited target dataset and a large, unannotated dataset with a general distribution. Under such circumstances, self-supervised learning (SSL) methods are highly effective for utilizing large datasets, making them a popular choice for electrocardiogram (ECG) analysis. This work presents the Event Reconstruction Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (ER-JEPA), a lightweight SSL framework for multivariate time series, whose name and two-fold hierarchical structure are inspired by the diagnostic approach of cardiologists. At its core, ER-JEPA features: (1) a two-stage structure that constructs representations for each time interval and subsequently processes these representations as a univariate time series, (2) the hierarchical integration of two Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs), and (3) a Vision Transformer (ViT) backbone. The structural concatenation of two JEPAs categorizes the model as a Hierarchical JEPA (H-JEPA), designed to encode multiple levels of abstract representations for enhanced prediction on complex tasks. This study reports a successful application of H-JEPA to 12-lead ECG data as a multivariate time series alongside an analysis of the sensitivity of hierarchical representation during the pretraining stage. Pretrained on approximately 180,000 10-second recordings, the model achieves state-of-the-art downstream performance on the ST-MEM benchmark, with rapid computation and minimal resource usage.

标题：基于分层JEPA的轻量级自监督学习框架用于ECG多变量时间序列分析
医学领域数据分析常面临目标数据集有限、但存在大量未标注且分布广泛的通用数据的情况。在此背景下，自监督学习（SSL）方法能有效利用大规模数据，因而成为心电图（ECG）分析的热门选择。本文提出事件重建联合嵌入预测架构（ER-JEPA），这是一种面向多变量时间序列的轻量级SSL框架，其名称及双层分层结构灵感源自心脏病专家的诊断方法。ER-JEPA的核心特征包括：（1）两阶段结构，先为每个时间区间构建表示，再将这些表示作为单变量时间序列处理；（2）两级联合嵌入预测架构（JEPAs）的分层集成；（3）视觉Transformer（ViT）主干网络。两个JEPAs的结构级拼接使模型归类为分层JEPA（H-JEPA），旨在编码多层次抽象表示以提升复杂任务的预测能力。本研究成功将H-JEPA应用于12导联ECG数据（作为多变量时间序列），并分析了预训练阶段分层表示的敏感性。该模型在约18万段10秒ECG记录上预训练，于ST-MEM基准测试中达到当前最优下游性能，同时具备快速计算与低资源消耗的特点。

为应对ECG数据标注样本稀缺、需利用大量无标注数据的问题，本文提出ER-JEPA轻量级自监督学习框架，用于多变量时间序列。该方法采用受临床心脏病学启发的两级层次结构，结合两个联合嵌入预测架构（JEPAs）与视觉Transformer骨干网络，以编码多层次抽象表征。在18万条10秒ECG记录上预训练后，该模型在ST-MEM基准上达到最先进性能，同时保持高效计算与低资源消耗。
Sequentially-Controlled Interactive Multi-Particle Flow-Maps for Online Feedback-Driven Search
Authors: Binglin Ji, Anindya Sarkar, Hengchang Lu, Jens Sjölund, Yevgeniy Vorobeychik
First: 2026-07-01T16:27:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:27:17+00:00
Comments: 28 pages, 19 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While generative models have enabled training-free reward alignment, current methods typically excel in local exploration within narrow regions of the underlying distribution. These approaches struggle when preferences are unknown a priori and only revealed through sequential feedback-a scenario demanding broad exploration to uncover high-utility regions. To address this, we propose Sequentially-Controlled Interactive Multi-Particle Flow-Maps (IMPFM), a framework for sample-efficient online feedback-driven search. IMPFM progressively transports a group of interactive particles toward the target distribution, maintaining the broad coverage essential for heterogeneous preference alignment. IMPFM introduces a principled and efficient posterior sample sharing mechanism across particles powered by flow maps. By correcting individual particle drift with the collective posterior samples of the entire ensemble at each resampling step, the framework maximizes sample utility to enable global exploration while actively mitigating reward over-optimization, typical of standard control frameworks. Paired with a principled exploration-exploitation reweighting mechanism involving multi-particle interaction, this sequentially corrected multi-particle dynamics explicitly preserves structural diversity and overcomes the weight degeneracy inherent to standard SMC samplers. Crucially, we prove that the resulting sampling framework yields a multi-particle interaction-aware Feynman-Kac corrector that progressively steers the multi-particle system toward a KL-tilted target distribution, facilitating global exploration and preventing mode collapse. Extensive empirical evaluations and rigorous ablations across diverse search and alignment tasks confirm the efficacy of IMPFM over existing baselines.

Motivated by the need for efficient global exploration in online feedback-driven search where preferences are revealed sequentially, the paper introduces Sequentially-Controlled Interactive Multi-Particle Flow-Maps (IMPFM), a framework that uses a group of interactive particles to progressively align with a target distribution while preserving structural diversity. Coupled with a multi-particle interaction-based exploration-exploitation reweighting mechanism, IMPFM avoids weight degeneracy common in standard SMC samplers and ensures global coverage. Empirical results across diverse tasks demonstrate IMPFM’s superior sample efficiency and alignment performance compared to existing baselines.
为解决在线反馈驱动搜索中偏好未知且需全局探索的问题，本文提出Sequentially-Controlled Interactive Multi-Particle Flow-Maps（IMPFM）框架，通过一组交互粒子逐步探索目标分布并保持结构多样性。IMPFM利用流图实现粒子间后验样本共享，在重采样时用整体后验样本校正个体漂移，提升样本利用率并缓解奖励过优化。结合多粒子交互的探索-利用重加权机制，该框架避免了标准SMC采样器常见的权重退化问题。理论证明该框架诱导出一种Feynman-Kac校正器，逐步引导粒子向KL倾斜的目标分布收敛，防止模式坍缩。实验表明IMPFM在多种任务中显著优于现有基线方法，兼具样本效率与对齐质量。
Fast Score-Based Sampling via Log-Concave Reductions
Authors: M. J. Wainwright
First: 2025-12-30T11:34:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:25:14+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the COLT 2026 Conference, San Diego, CA
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sampling based on score diffusions has led to striking empirical results, and has attracted considerable attention from various research communities. It depends on availability of (approximate) Stein score functions for various levels of additive noise. We show how in some generality, the availability of scores allows the general problem to be ``reduced'' to sampling from an adaptively constructed sequence of $K$ strongly log-concave (SLC) sub-problems. The reduction is simple, constructive and algorithm-independent, so that any SLC sampler can be used as a subroutine. Various bounds on score-based sampling complexity follow directly: for instance, high-accuracy SLC samplers yield $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(K \sqrt{d} \operatorname{polylog}(1/\varepsilon))$ guarantees for accuracy $\varepsilon$ in dimension $d$, where randomized midpoint SLC schemes yield $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(K d^{1/3} \operatorname{poly}(1/\varepsilon))$ guarantees. When the original distribution itself is SLC, we prove that $K \leq 1 + \log_2(κ)$, thereby obtaining the first efficient procedure with logarithmic dependence on condition number $κ$; for general distributions, the quantity $K$ depends on the geometry of score Hessian across the trajectory. Our analysis is direct and simple, involving techniques and insights complementary to those in standard analyses of discretized diffusions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过对数凹性约简实现快速基于分数的采样
基于分数扩散的采样方法已取得显著的实证成果，并吸引了多个研究领域的广泛关注。该方法依赖于各类加性噪声水平下（近似）Stein分数函数的可用性。我们展示在某些一般性条件下，分数的可用性允许将一般问题“约简”为从一个自适应构造的K个强对数凹（SLC）子问题序列中采样。该约简方法简单、构造性明确且与算法无关，因此任何SLC采样器均可作为子程序使用。由此可直接推导出多种基于分数的采样复杂度界：例如，高精度SLC采样器可保证在维度d下，精度ε的复杂度为~O(K√d·polylog(1/ε))；而随机中点SLC方案则可保证~O(Kd^{1/3}·poly(1/ε))。当原始分布本身为SLC时，我们证明K≤1+log₂(κ)，从而获得首个对条件数κ具有对数依赖性的高效算法；对于一般分布，K的值取决于轨迹上分数Hessian矩阵的几何特性。我们的分析直接且简洁，涉及与标准离散扩散分析互补的技术与洞见。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the empirical success of score-based sampling methods, this work presents a general reduction technique that transforms complex score-based sampling problems into a sequence of strongly log-concave (SLC) sub-problems, enabling the use of any existing SLC sampler as a subroutine. The method constructs an adaptive sequence of K such sub-problems, and the resulting complexity bounds show that high-accuracy SLC samplers achieve $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(K \sqrt{d} \operatorname{polylog}(1/\varepsilon))$ accuracy for dimension d and error ε, while randomized midpoint schemes yield $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(K d^{1/3} \operatorname{poly}(1/\varepsilon))$. 为提升基于分数的采样方法的效率，该研究提出一种通用的简化策略，将复杂分数采样问题转化为一系列强对数凹（SLC）子问题，从而可复用任意SLC采样器。该方法构造一个自适应的K步序列，其复杂度依赖于K、维度d和精度ε。实验表明，当目标分布本身为SLC时，K可被限制为1 + log₂(κ)，首次实现对条件数κ的对数级依赖；对于一般分布，K由轨迹上分数Hessian的几何特性决定，分析过程简洁且不依赖特定算法。
Relation-Centric Open-Vocabulary 3D Gaussian Segmentation
Authors: Eunsung Cha, Hyunjoon Lee, Jaesik Park
First: 2026-07-01T16:23:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:23:41+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://eunsungcha.github.io/PairGS-web/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Open-vocabulary 3D Gaussian segmentation is challenging because it requires language understanding for diverse queries and accurate separation of Gaussians along object boundaries. Prior approaches either embed language knowledge into individual Gaussians to improve query responsiveness or optimize per-Gaussian instance features to encode object identity. However, these strategies may produce noisy Gaussian segmentations or rely on cost-inefficient per-scene optimization. We propose PairGS, a framework that reframes Gaussian segmentation as modeling pairwise relations between Gaussians. 3D Gaussian representations provide rich signals for relation estimation, such as view contribution weights and multi-view mask evidence. By leveraging these cues, PairGS explicitly constructs a relation graph for segmentation without a heavy optimization process. PairGS first proposes sparse edge candidates using low-dimensional descriptors, computes precise pairwise affinities only on those candidates, and builds a hierarchical cluster tree for multi-granular querying. It achieves state-of-the-art results on open-vocabulary 3D Gaussian segmentation benchmarks, while the fast variant is 50x faster than optimization-based instance-feature approaches.

标题：关系为中心的开放词汇3D高斯分割

It leverages view contribution weights and multi-view mask evidence from 3D Gaussian representations to construct a sparse relation graph, then computes precise affinities on candidate edges and builds a hierarchical cluster tree for flexible querying. Experiments show PairGS achieves state-of-the-art performance on open-vocabulary 3D Gaussian segmentation benchmarks, and its fast variant is 50x faster than optimization-based methods.
为解决开放词汇下3D高斯点云分割的准确性和效率问题，本文提出PairGS框架，将分割任务重构为建模高斯点间关系的问题。该方法利用高斯点的视图权重与多视角掩码等信号构建稀疏关系图，仅在候选边计算精确亲和度，并构建层次聚类树支持多粒度查询。实验表明，PairGS在开放词汇3D高斯分割基准上达到最先进性能，其快速版本比基于优化的实例特征方法快50倍。
SD-RouteFusion: Ego-Trajectory Prediction with SD-Map Route Conditioning
Authors: Sviatoslav Voloshyn, Bruno K. W. Martens, Wangxin Liu, Jakob Vinkås, Junsheng Fu
First: 2026-07-01T16:22:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:22:50+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 4 figures, 29th International Conference on Information Fusion
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents SD-RouteFusion, a deployable end-to-end ego-trajectory prediction method that fuses a front-facing camera, vehicle kinematics, and a navigation route derived from a Standard Definition (SD) map. Unlike approaches that rely on High Definition (HD) map geometry, SD-RouteFusion aligns the learning objective with scalable and production-ready SD-map route inputs, enabling route-aware prediction without requiring HD-map infrastructure. First, we demonstrate that SD-map route prior provides a powerful long-horizon semantic prior. Through a comprehensive study on a large-scale real-world dataset comprising 480k driving scenarios across 10 European countries and the U.S., we quantify the value of SD-route conditioning: incorporating SD-map routes yields a 10.5% ADE improvement over an image-and-kinematics baseline, while our full fusion strategy achieves a 16.9% ADE reduction given a prediction horizon of 8 seconds. The fusion strategy consists of a dual-hypothesis design paired with a gated classifier, to ensure robustness under route corruption and visual uncertainty. Finally, to support broader evaluation, we release an SD-route generation toolkit that enables SD-route-conditioned ego-trajectory prediction on all datasets containing ego pose and future trajectories. Together, SD-RouteFusion establishes a practical path toward robust, route-aware ego-trajectory prediction at scale.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SD-RouteFusion：基于SD地图路线条件的自车轨迹预测
本文提出SD-RouteFusion，一种可部署的端到端自车轨迹预测方法，融合前向摄像头、车辆运动学数据及从标准定义（SD）地图中提取的导航路线。与依赖高精度（HD）地图几何结构的方法不同，SD-RouteFusion将学习目标与可扩展且适用于生产的SD地图路线输入对齐，实现无需HD地图基础设施的路线感知预测。首先，我们证明SD地图路线先验提供了强大的长时程语义先验。通过对涵盖10个欧洲国家和美国、共48万驾驶场景的大规模真实世界数据集的全面研究，我们量化了SD路线条件的价值：引入SD地图路线相比仅使用图像和运动学数据的基线方法，平均绝对位移误差（ADE）降低10.5%；在8秒预测时长下，我们的完整融合策略实现16.9%的ADE降低。该融合策略采用双假设设计与门控分类器，以确保在路线损坏和视觉不确定性下的鲁棒性。最后，为支持更广泛的评估，我们发布了SD路线生成工具包，使所有包含自车位姿和未来轨迹的数据集均可进行SD路线条件下的自车轨迹预测。综上，SD-RouteFusion为大规模、稳健且路线感知的自车轨迹预测提供了实用路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable, production-ready ego-trajectory prediction without HD-map infrastructure, SD-RouteFusion fuses front-facing camera inputs, vehicle kinematics, and SD-map-derived navigation routes to enable route-aware forecasting. The method employs a dual-hypothesis design with a gated classifier to handle route corruption and visual uncertainty, and achieves a 16.9% ADE reduction over a baseline at 8-second prediction horizon on a 480k-scenario dataset across 10 countries. The SD-route conditioning alone improves performance by 10.5% ADE, demonstrating its value as a semantic prior for long-horizon prediction.
为实现无需高清地图基础设施的可扩展、可部署的自车轨迹预测，SD-RouteFusion将前视摄像头、车辆运动学数据与标准地图生成的导航路线融合至端到端模型中。该方法采用双假设融合架构与门控分类器，以应对路线损坏与视觉不确定性，使预测与语义路线先验对齐。在涵盖10国48万驾驶场景的大规模真实数据集上，模型在8秒预测时长下较基线降低16.9%的ADE，验证了标准地图路线条件对长时程预测精度的显著提升，同时具备实际部署能力。
Skills Are Not Islands: Measuring Dependency and Risk in Agent Skill Supply Chains
Authors: Changguo Jia, Tianqi Zhao, Runzhi He, Minghui Zhou
First: 2026-07-01T16:21:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:21:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agent skills package reusable operational knowledge for Large Language Model (LLM) agents, yet as they grow in scope, they become dependency-bearing artifacts whose identities, versions, and provenance remain implicit. This opacity already causes duplicated dependencies and inconsistent installations, exposing a gap that dependency management has yet to close. We introduce Agent Skill Supply Chains (ASSCs) to characterize mixed skill-package-service dependency graphs and help close this gap. Borrowing from Software Bill of Materials (SBOMs), we design SkillDepAnalyzer to capture natural-language dependency evidence and model skills as dependency-bearing artifacts. On the SKILL-DEP benchmark, SkillDepAnalyzer recovers skill metadata and dependency graphs accurately and comprehensively, substantially outperforming an LLM-based baseline and package-centric SBOM tools. Applying SkillDepAnalyzer to over 1.43 million skills, we obtain ASSCs and explore their structural diversity and security signals. We find four structural patterns: skill metadata is activation-ready but governance-poor; dependency graphs span skill, package, and service dependencies with concentrated reuse; recursive skill reuse expands dependency graphs and creates hidden package inventory; and skill dependency clusters form around related workflows. We also find that inspecting a skill alone misses security-relevant signals hiding in its dependencies. By analyzing ASSCs, we identify and report known malicious skills persisting in ASSCs to their developers. Based on these findings, we recommend typed dependency manifests, first-class dependency-cluster management, risk-warning audit commands for skill infrastructure maintainers, and lockfile-like records for skill developers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：技能并非孤岛：衡量代理技能供应链中的依赖性与风险
代理技能为大型语言模型（LLM）代理封装可重用的操作知识，但随着其范围扩大，它们逐渐成为隐含身份、版本和来源的依赖性产物。这种不透明性已导致重复依赖和不一致的安装，暴露出依赖管理尚未解决的缺口。我们引入代理技能供应链（ASSC）以刻画混合技能-包-服务依赖图，帮助弥合这一缺口。借鉴软件物料清单（SBOM），我们设计了SkillDepAnalyzer，用于捕获自然语言依赖证据，并将技能建模为依赖性产物。在SKILL-DEP基准测试中，SkillDepAnalyzer准确且全面地恢复技能元数据和依赖图，显著优于基于LLM的基线方法和以包为中心的SBOM工具。通过对超过143万项技能应用SkillDepAnalyzer，我们获得ASSC并探索其结构多样性与安全信号。我们发现四种结构模式：技能元数据具备即用性但治理薄弱；依赖图涵盖技能、包和服务依赖，且重用集中；递归技能重用扩展依赖图并形成隐藏的包库存；技能依赖簇围绕相关工作流形成。我们还发现，仅检查单个技能会遗漏隐藏在依赖中的安全相关信号。通过分析ASSC，我们识别并报告了持续存在于ASSC中的已知恶意技能，并将其反馈给开发者。基于这些发现，我们建议采用类型化依赖清单、依赖簇的一级管理、面向技能基础设施维护者的风险警告审计命令，以及面向技能开发者的类似锁定文件的记录。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the opacity and inconsistency in LLM agent skill dependencies, this work introduces Agent Skill Supply Chains (ASSCs) to model and analyze complex dependency graphs across skills, packages, and services. Using a tool called SkillDepAnalyzer, which extracts dependency evidence from natural language and treats skills as dependency-bearing artifacts, the authors evaluate performance on the SKILL-DEP benchmark, where it significantly outperforms LLM baselines and traditional SBOM tools. Applied to over 1.43 million skills, ASSC analysis reveals four structural patterns: skills are metadata-ready but governance-lacking; dependencies span multiple layers with concentrated reuse; recursive skill reuse expands dependency graphs and hides package inventory; and skills cluster around workflows. Security risks are found to be embedded in dependencies, not just the skills themselves, and the study identifies malicious skills still active in ASSCs, prompting recommendations for typed manifests, cluster management, audit commands, and lockfile-style records.
随着代理技能复杂度提升，其隐含依赖导致重复安装和安全盲区，现有依赖管理工具难以应对。作者提出代理技能供应链（ASSC）模型，用于刻画混合依赖图，并设计SkillDepAnalyzer工具，从自然语言中提取依赖证据，将技能视为依赖承载物。在SKILL-DEP基准测试中，SkillDepAnalyzer在恢复元数据和依赖图方面显著优于LLM基线和包中心SBOM工具。应用于143万技能后，发现四种结构模式：技能激活便捷但治理薄弱；依赖覆盖技能、包与服务，且重用集中；递归重用扩大依赖图并隐藏包库存；技能围绕工作流形成集群。安全风险常藏于依赖而非技能本身，ASSC分析发现仍有恶意技能活跃于生态中。作者建议引入类型化依赖清单、集群管理、审计命令及锁文件式记录，以提升技能供应链安全性。
Towards Metric-Agnostic Trajectory Forecasting
Authors: Markus Knoche, Daan de Geus, Bastian Leibe
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-07-01T16:20:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:20:43+00:00
Comments: ECCV 2026. Project page at https://vision.rwth-aachen.de/TraDiE-policies
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate trajectory forecasting of surrounding traffic participants is a core capability for autonomous driving, enabling vehicles to anticipate behavior and plan safe maneuvers. We observe that current state-of-the-art forecasting models on Argoverse 2 and the Waymo Open Motion Dataset tailor their training objectives to the different benchmark metrics. Because these metrics encourage conflicting behavior, we propose a paradigm change for trajectory forecasting: training models with metric-agnostic probabilistic objectives and treating metric optimization as a downstream task applied to the predictive distribution. Concretely, we introduce Trajectory Distribution Evaluation (TraDiE) policies, metric-specific policies that map a predictive distribution to the set of $K$ trajectories and confidences required by trajectory forecasting metrics. We evaluate this framework by introducing DONUT-NLL, which adapts the training objective of the state-of-the-art trajectory forecasting model DONUT to directly optimize the predictive distribution. Using our policies, DONUT-NLL achieves state-of-the-art results on all metrics of the Waymo motion prediction benchmark.
中文标题/摘要
标题：迈向无度量依赖的轨迹预测
准确预测周围交通参与者的轨迹是自动驾驶的核心能力，使车辆能够预判行为并规划安全操作。我们观察到，当前在Argoverse 2和Waymo开放运动数据集上的最先进预测模型会根据不同的基准度量调整其训练目标。由于这些度量鼓励相互冲突的行为，我们提出轨迹预测范式的转变：使用无度量依赖的概率性训练目标训练模型，并将度量优化视为应用于预测分布的下游任务。具体而言，我们引入轨迹分布评估（TraDiE）策略，即针对特定度量的策略，将预测分布映射到轨迹预测度量所需的K条轨迹及其置信度集合。我们通过引入DONUT-NLL评估该框架，该方法将当前最先进的轨迹预测模型DONUT的训练目标调整为直接优化预测分布。借助我们的策略，DONUT-NLL在Waymo运动预测基准的所有度量上均达到最先进水平。
Summary / 总结
To address the issue that current trajectory forecasting models optimize for conflicting metrics, this work proposes a metric-agnostic training paradigm where models are trained with probabilistic objectives that do not favor any specific metric, and metric-specific evaluation policies are applied afterward. The method introduces Trajectory Distribution Evaluation (TraDiE) policies that convert predictive distributions into the K-trajectory outputs required by benchmark metrics. Evaluated via DONUT-NLL—a variant of the state-of-the-art DONUT model trained to directly optimize its predictive distribution—the framework achieves top performance across all metrics in the Waymo motion prediction benchmark.
为解决当前轨迹预测模型因优化不同指标而产生冲突行为的问题，本文提出一种不依赖特定指标的训练范式，即通过概率性目标训练模型，再在下游应用指标特定的评估策略。方法引入轨迹分布评估（TraDiE）策略，将预测分布转换为指标所需的K条轨迹及置信度输出。在对顶尖模型DONUT进行改造后训练出的DONUT-NLL上，该框架在Waymo运动预测基准的全部指标上均取得最优性能。
Autonomous Scientific Discovery via Iterative Meta-Reflection
Authors: Bingchen Zhao, Sara Beery, Oisin Mac Aodha
First: 2026-07-01T16:16:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:16:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous scientific discovery systems offer the potential to accelerate research by automating the process of hypothesis generation and validation. However, current systems operate within constrained search spaces or require predefined research questions, limiting their capacity for true open-ended inquiry. Furthermore, while they generate hypotheses iteratively, they largely lack the ability to explicitly synthesize their own accumulated findings to uncover complex, interconnected phenomena. We introduce DiscoPER, an autonomous large language model-powered framework that conducts open-ended research by dynamically generating and executing code to explore datasets without pre-specified research objectives. To ensure rigorous scientific validity, every proposed discovery must pass statistical testing. To overcome the limitations of isolated search, our framework introduces a second-order reasoning mechanism that periodically analyzes its own accumulated discoveries. By treating prior discoveries as empirical data, DiscoPER identifies structural patterns, confounds, and epistemic gaps, actively redirecting hypothesis exploration toward uncharted regions of the search space. The search space is further expanded by incorporating tool use, enabling the system to explore hypotheses beyond structured metadata by seamlessly processing and extracting useful information from multimodal sources like images. Evaluated on iNatDisco, a new multimodal ecological knowledge benchmark with pattern-level ground truth obtained from peer-reviewed literature, DiscoPER recovers 8 of 9 known patterns with a 72.7% hypothesis support rate, outperforming both classical causal discovery and LLM-guided baselines. Ablations show that DiscoPER scales with more data, and confirms the benefits of second-order meta-reflection.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过迭代元反思实现自主科学发现
自主科学发现系统有望通过自动化假设生成与验证来加速研究。然而，现有系统受限于受限的搜索空间或需要预定义研究问题，难以实现真正的开放性探索。此外，尽管它们能迭代生成假设，却大多缺乏显式综合自身积累发现以揭示复杂关联现象的能力。我们提出DiscoPER，一种基于自主大语言模型的框架，通过动态生成并执行代码探索无预设目标的数据集。为确保科学严谨性，每个提出的发现必须通过统计检验。为克服孤立搜索的局限，该框架引入二阶推理机制，定期分析自身积累的发现。通过将先前发现视为经验数据，DiscoPER识别结构模式、混杂因素和认识论空白，主动引导假设探索转向搜索空间中的未探索区域。通过引入工具使用，搜索空间进一步扩展，使系统能无缝处理并提取图像等多模态来源中的有用信息，超越结构化元数据的限制。在iNatDisco上评估（一个基于同行评审文献获得模式级真实标注的新型多模态生态知识基准），DiscoPER恢复了9个已知模式中的8个，假设支持率达72.7%，优于经典因果发现方法和LLM引导基线。消融实验表明，DiscoPER随数据量增加而扩展，且证实了二阶元反思的优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enable open-ended scientific discovery without predefined objectives, the authors propose DiscoPER, a framework powered by large language models that autonomously generates and executes code to explore datasets. It incorporates a second-order meta-reflection mechanism that periodically analyzes its own discoveries to identify patterns, confounds, and epistemic gaps, guiding exploration toward uncharted regions. Additionally, it integrates multimodal tool use to extract information from images, expanding its search beyond structured metadata. Evaluated on iNatDisco, a new ecological benchmark with ground-truth patterns, DiscoPER recovered 8 of 9 known patterns with a 72.7% hypothesis support rate, outperforming classical causal discovery and LLM-guided baselines, and demonstrated scalability with more data.
为实现无预设目标的开放科学发现，本文提出DiscoPER框架，利用大语言模型自动生成并执行代码，对多模态数据进行探索性分析。该框架引入二阶元反思机制，定期分析自身发现以识别结构模式与认知空白，引导搜索向未探索区域推进。在iNatDisco生态知识基准测试中，DiscoPER成功恢复9个已知模式中的8个，支持率72.7%，优于传统因果发现与LLM引导基线方法，并验证了其随数据量增加而扩展的能力。
GAIA: Geometry-Adaptive Operator Learning for Forward and Inverse Problems
Authors: Meenakshi Krishnan, Pranav Pulijala, Ke Chen, Haizhao Yang, Ramani Duraiswami
First: 2026-07-01T16:14:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:14:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Operator learning for partial differential equations (PDEs) on arbitrary geometries builds fast neural surrogates for large-scale simulation. Although recent geometry-adaptive neural operators have made substantial progress, they are mainly designed for forward problems in which inputs and outputs share the same spatial domain. This limits their applicability for boundary value problems (BVPs) and inverse problems, where inputs and outputs may live on different domains. We introduce the Geometry-Adaptive Integral Autoencoder (GAIA), an operator learning model that encodes the domain boundary and the interior field distribution into geometry tokens, and conditions integral transform layers on these tokens via cross-attention, allowing the kernel to adapt locally to geometric features. This yields a single architecture for forward (including BVPs) and inverse problems on arbitrary domains in one pass, without retraining, iterative optimization, or graph construction. We evaluate GAIA on seven 2D and 3D benchmarks, four of which are new or substantially extended benchmarks for inverse problems and BVP: electrical impedance tomography, optical tomography, 3D Darcy flow on varying geometries, and a modified setting of Poisson BVP on mechanical components benchmark (MCB). GAIA sets new state-of-the-art results on every inverse and BVP task, reducing median relative $L^2$ error by 64% on airfoil flow reconstruction and 27% on EIT relative to the next best amortized method, and outperforming all baselines on every shape category of MCB. On other forward problems, GAIA is competitive with specialized solvers while maintaining stable accuracy across point resolutions on which transformer-based baselines degrade.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GAIA：面向正向与反向问题的几何自适应算子学习
针对任意几何域上的偏微分方程（PDE）算子学习，构建快速神经代理以支持大规模仿真。尽管近期几何自适应神经算子已取得显著进展，但它们主要设计用于输入与输出共享相同空间域的正向问题，这限制了其在边界值问题（BVP）和反向问题中的应用，因为这些问题的输入与输出可能位于不同域。我们提出几何自适应积分自编码器（GAIA），一种算子学习模型，将域边界与内部场分布编码为几何标记，并通过交叉注意力机制将这些标记作为条件输入至积分变换层，使核函数能局部自适应于几何特征。该架构可一次性处理任意域上的正向（含BVP）与反向问题，无需重新训练、迭代优化或图结构构建。我们在七个2D和3D基准上评估GAIA，其中四个为新设或大幅扩展的反向问题与BVP基准：电阻抗断层成像、光学断层成像、变几何3D达西流、以及机械部件基准（MCB）的修改版泊松BVP。GAIA在所有反向与BVP任务中均刷新当前最优结果，相较次优的自适应方法，空气动力学翼型重建的中位相对L²误差降低64%，EIT任务降低27%，并在MCB所有形状类别上全面超越基线。在其他正向问题上，GAIA与专用求解器竞争性相当，同时在Transformer基线性能退化点分辨率下保持稳定精度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for flexible neural operators that handle both forward and inverse problems on arbitrary geometries, GAIA introduces a geometry-adaptive integral autoencoder that encodes domain boundaries and interior field distributions into geometry tokens, conditioning integral transform layers via cross-attention to enable local geometric adaptation. This unified architecture operates without retraining or iterative optimization, achieving state-of-the-art performance on seven 2D/3D benchmarks including inverse problems like electrical impedance tomography and boundary value problems such as 3D Darcy flow. GAIA reduces median relative L² error by 64% on airfoil flow reconstruction and 27% on EIT compared to the next best amortized method, and outperforms all baselines on the MCB benchmark across all shape categories, while remaining competitive with specialized solvers on forward problems without accuracy degradation across varying point resolutions.
为解决神经算子在任意几何域上处理正向与反向问题的灵活性需求，GAIA提出一种几何自适应积分自编码器，通过将域边界与内部场分布编码为几何标记，并利用交叉注意力机制调节积分变换层，实现局部几何自适应。该统一架构在七个2D和3D基准测试中均取得最先进性能，包括四项新或扩展的反向与边界值问题，其中在机翼流场重建上相对L²误差降低64%，在EIT上降低27%，优于所有基线方法，并在MCB基准各形状类别中表现最佳，同时在正向问题上保持与专用求解器相当的精度，无需重训练或图结构构建。
$\text{Log}_\text{b}$Quant: Quantizing Language Models in Logarithmic Space
Authors: Jeremias Bohn, Tizian Dippold, Mahdi Koubaa, Elias R. Wahl, Georg Groh
First: 2026-07-01T16:13:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:13:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Quantization has become an invaluable tool to reduce memory requirements and inference speed of modern language models, in particular to make them available for consumer setups and edge devices. While previous work has primarily focused on uniform quantization codebooks, such approaches are prone to suboptimal representations due to low-frequency high-magnitude weights. We introduce Log$_\text{b}$Quant, a novel logarithmic quantization approach with adjustable bases, to adapt to common parameter distributions. We show that our method exhibits superior performance at 4-bit precision on several performance benchmarks compared to asymmetric linear quantization at tensor-wise granularity, while achieving moderate speedup and high memory savings, making it suitable for private use on consumer-grade GPUs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Log_b量化：在对数空间量化语言模型
量化已成为减少现代语言模型内存需求和推理速度的宝贵工具，特别是使其适用于消费级设备和边缘设备。尽管以往研究主要集中在均匀量化码本上，但此类方法由于低频高幅值权重易导致次优表示。我们提出Log_b量化，一种具有可调底数的对数量化方法，以适应常见的参数分布。我们在多个性能基准测试中表明，与张量粒度的非对称线性量化相比，该方法在4位精度下表现更优，同时实现适度加速和高内存节省，适合在消费级GPU上私有使用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of uniform quantization in handling low-frequency high-magnitude weights in language models, the authors propose Log$_\text{b}$Quant, a logarithmic quantization method with adjustable bases to better match parameter distributions. The method transforms weights into logarithmic space before quantization, enabling more efficient representation. Experiments show that Log$_\text{b}$Quant outperforms asymmetric linear quantization at 4-bit precision across multiple benchmarks, while offering moderate speedup and significant memory savings, making it practical for deployment on consumer-grade GPUs.
为了解决传统均匀量化在处理低频高幅权重时表现不佳的问题，作者提出了Log$_\text{b}$Quant方法，该方法采用可调底数的对数量化以更好地适配参数分布。在4比特精度下，该方法在多个基准测试中优于非对称线性量化，在保持适度加速和显著内存节省的同时，模型精度更高，适合在消费级GPU上部署。
ZO-Act: Efficient Zeroth-Order Fine-Tuning via One-Shot Activation-Informed Low-Rank Subspaces
Authors: Xun Dong, Yibo Xu, Naigang Wang, Xin Li, Penghang Yin, Zi Yang
First: 2026-07-01T16:12:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:12:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization enables fine-tuning large language models when backpropagation is unavailable or memory-prohibitive, but existing methods often perturb full model weights or randomly constructed low-dimensional subspaces, yielding high-variance estimates and limited performance. We propose ZO-Act, an activation-informed ZO fine-tuning method that restricts perturbations to a fixed low-rank subspace derived from input activations. For each linear layer, ZO-Act computes a small activation basis once at initialization and optimizes only lightweight coefficient matrices using forward-only loss evaluations. This reduces the effective perturbation dimension, exposes explicit trainable variables compatible with momentum-based optimizers such as Adam, and naturally supports quantized LLM fine-tuning by keeping low-bit weights frozen. We analyze ZO-Act as zeroth-order optimization over a restricted coefficient space and show that perturbing the low-dimensional coefficients reduces both the variance-dependent convergence term and the finite-difference error of the ZO estimator, at the cost of a controlled subspace approximation bias that is mitigated by the low-rank structure of LLM activations and gradients. Experiments on Llama-3-8B, OPT-13B, and INT4 Llama-3-8B show consistent gains over strong ZO fine-tuning baselines across language understanding, question answering, and commonsense reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ZO-Act：通过单次激活引导的低秩子空间实现高效的零阶微调
零阶（ZO）优化在无法使用反向传播或内存受限时，可对大语言模型进行微调，但现有方法常扰动完整模型权重或随机构造的低维子空间，导致高方差估计和性能受限。我们提出ZO-Act，一种基于激活信息的零阶微调方法，将扰动限制在由输入激活导出的固定低秩子空间内。对每个线性层，ZO-Act在初始化时计算一个小的激活基，并仅优化轻量级系数矩阵，使用仅前向的损失评估。这降低了有效扰动维度，显式暴露与动量优化器（如Adam）兼容的可训练变量，并自然支持量化LLM微调，通过保持低比特权重冻结。我们将ZO-Act分析为在受限系数空间上的零阶优化，表明扰动低维系数可同时降低与方差相关的收敛项和ZO估计器的有限差分误差，代价是可控的子空间近似偏差，该偏差可通过LLM激活和梯度的低秩结构缓解。在Llama-3-8B、OPT-13B和INT4 Llama-3-8B上的实验表明，ZO-Act在语言理解、问答和常识推理任务上均优于强零阶微调基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient zeroth-order fine-tuning of large language models without backpropagation, ZO-Act introduces an activation-informed low-rank subspace approach that restricts perturbations to a fixed, input-driven subspace derived from initial activations. Instead of perturbing full weights or random subspaces, it computes a small activation basis per layer at initialization and optimizes only lightweight coefficient matrices using forward-only loss evaluations. This design reduces perturbation dimensionality, enables compatibility with momentum-based optimizers, and supports quantized fine-tuning by freezing low-bit weights. Experimental results on Llama-3-8B, OPT-13B, and INT4 Llama-3-8B demonstrate consistent performance gains over existing ZO methods across language understanding, question answering, and commonsense reasoning tasks.
为解决在无梯度或内存受限情况下对大语言模型进行零阶微调的效率问题，ZO-Act提出一种基于激活信息的低秩子空间方法，将扰动限制在由初始输入激活导出的固定低维子空间内。每个线性层在初始化时计算一个小规模激活基，仅优化轻量级系数矩阵，通过前向损失评估进行更新。该设计降低了有效扰动维度，支持动量优化器（如Adam），并可通过冻结低比特权重实现量化微调。在Llama-3-8B、OPT-13B和INT4 Llama-3-8B上的实验表明，该方法在语言理解、问答和常识推理任务上均优于现有零阶微调基线。
Muon as a Residual Connection
Authors: Hao Huang
First: 2026-07-01T16:12:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:12:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Muon has recently emerged as one of the most effective optimizers for training large neural networks, yet its empirical success has been explained from several different perspectives. In this paper, we propose a simple mechanistic interpretation: Muon can be understood as an implicit residual connection during training. Specifically, orthogonalizing the update can sacrifice some immediate gradient fidelity while improving representation preservation for downstream layers. We study this trade-off in controlled linear optimization settings, where Muon can learn representations that are slower to fit a local target but easier for downstream layers to exploit. Our results suggest a conceptual explanation for Muon and a design perspective for optimizers that balance local descent with downstream usability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Muon作为一种残差连接
Muon最近被证明是训练大型神经网络最有效的优化器之一，但其经验成功已从多个角度得到解释。本文提出一种简单的机制性解释：Muon可理解为训练过程中的隐式残差连接。具体而言，正交化更新虽牺牲部分即时梯度保真度，却能提升下游层的表示保留能力。我们在受控的线性优化环境中研究此权衡，发现Muon可学习更难拟合局部目标但更易被下游层利用的表示。结果为Muon提供概念性解释，并为平衡局部下降与下游可用性的优化器设计提供视角。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by Muon's empirical effectiveness in training large neural networks, this paper proposes a mechanistic interpretation: Muon functions as an implicit residual connection during training. The method analyzes the trade-off between gradient fidelity and representation preservation by orthogonalizing updates, which sacrifices immediate gradient accuracy but enhances downstream layer usability. Experimental results in controlled linear settings show that Muon learns representations that are less immediately accurate for local targets but more beneficial for subsequent layers, supporting the idea that its success stems from this balance between local descent and downstream compatibility.
鉴于Muon在训练大型神经网络中的实证有效性，本文提出其机制解释：Muon通过正交化更新，实质上充当隐式残差连接，以牺牲即时梯度保真度换取下游层更好的表示保留能力。在受控的线性优化实验中，作者发现Muon学习的表示虽较慢适应局部目标，但更利于下游层利用。这一权衡解释了Muon的成功，并为设计兼顾局部下降与下游兼容性的优化器提供了思路。
Estimating Velocity and Spin of Spherical Objects from Rolling-Shutter Image(s)
Authors: Wenjie Xue, Jun Yang, Jingmin Wang, Limin Shang
First: 2026-06-30T14:49:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:12:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Rolling-shutter cameras introduce characteristic distortions when imaging fast moving objects, and these effects are typically treated as artifacts to be corrected. In this work, we instead leverage rolling-shutter distortions as a valuable source of temporal information to estimate the 3D translational and angular velocities of rapidly moving spherical objects from a single rolling-shutter frame. We design a robust and easily detectable spherical pattern and propose a correspondence-free formulation that recovers motion by enforcing geometric consistency in a back-projection framework. By exploiting the geometry of the sphere, translational and rotational motions are decoupled and estimated through a two-stage optimization process, enabling reliable velocity recovery even for textureless objects. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real datasets demonstrate accurate and robust estimation of motion parameters under challenging high-speed conditions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从滚动快门图像估计球形物体的速度与自旋
滚动快门相机在拍摄快速运动物体时会引入特征性畸变，这些效应通常被视为需校正的伪影。本文则利用滚动快门畸变作为宝贵的时间信息源，从单帧滚动快门图像中估计快速运动球形物体的三维平移与角速度。我们设计了一种鲁棒且易于检测的球形图案，并提出一种无需对应关系的公式，通过后投影框架强制几何一致性来恢复运动。通过利用球体几何特性，平移与旋转运动被解耦，并通过两阶段优化过程进行估计，即使对于无纹理物体也能实现可靠的运动速度恢复。在合成与真实数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在高难度高速条件下能准确且鲁棒地估计运动参数。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to extract motion parameters from rolling-shutter images without relying on texture or external sensors, this work exploits rolling-shutter distortions as a source of temporal cues to estimate 3D translational and angular velocities of spherical objects from a single frame. It introduces a specially designed spherical pattern and a correspondence-free method based on geometric consistency in a back-projection framework, enabling decoupling of translation and rotation through a two-stage optimization. Experimental results on synthetic and real datasets confirm the method’s accuracy and robustness, even for textureless spheres moving at high speeds.
该研究旨在将滚动快门成像的畸变视为有用的运动线索而非干扰项，从而从单张滚动快门图像中估计快速运动球体的三维平移与角速度。方法设计了专用球面图案，并提出无需特征匹配的几何一致性后投影框架，通过两阶段优化解耦并估计平移与旋转运动。实验表明，即使在无纹理球体和高速条件下，该方法仍能准确、鲁棒地恢复运动参数。
From Silos to Systems: Process-Oriented Hazard Analysis for AI Systems
Authors: Shalaleh Rismani, Roel Dobbe, AJung Moon
First: 2024-10-29T20:43:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:09:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
To effectively address potential harms from Artificial Intelligence (AI) systems, it is essential to identify and mitigate system-level hazards. Current analysis approaches focus on individual components of an AI system, like training data or models, in isolation, overlooking hazards from component interactions or how they are situated within a company's development process. To this end, we draw from the established field of system safety, which considers safety as an emergent property of the entire system. In this work, we translate System Theoretic Process Analysis (STPA) - a recognized system safety framework - for analyzing AI development and operation processes. We focus on systems that rely on machine learning algorithms and conduct STPA on three case studies involving linear regression, reinforcement learning, and transformer-based generative models. Our analysis explored how STPA's control and system-theoretic perspectives apply to AI systems and whether unique AI traits - such as model opacity, capability uncertainty, and output complexity - necessitate modifications to the framework. We find that the key concepts and steps of conducting an STPA apply to AI systems but require targeted adaptations to address AI-specific challenges that arise to differing degrees across three case studies. We present the Process-oriented Hazard Analysis for AI Systems (PHASE) as a guideline that adapts STPA concepts for AI. Applying and interpreting STPA using the PHASE guidelines enables four key affordances for analysts responsible for managing AI system harms: 1) detection of system-level hazards, including those from accumulation of disparate issues; 2) explicit acknowledgment of social factors contributing to algorithmic harms; 3) creation of traceable accountability chains between harms and those who can mitigate them; and 4) ongoing monitoring and mitigation of new hazards.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从孤岛到系统：面向AI系统的流程导向危害分析
为有效应对人工智能（AI）系统潜在危害，需识别并缓解系统级危害。当前分析方法聚焦于AI系统的独立组件（如训练数据或模型），忽视了组件交互或其在公司开发流程中的危害。为此，我们借鉴系统安全领域，将系统安全视为系统整体的涌现属性。本研究将系统理论过程分析（STPA）——一种公认的系统安全框架——应用于AI开发与运行流程分析。我们聚焦依赖机器学习算法的系统，对线性回归、强化学习和基于Transformer的生成模型三个案例进行STPA分析。研究探讨了STPA的控制与系统理论视角如何适用于AI系统，以及AI特有属性（如模型不透明性、能力不确定性、输出复杂性）是否需要对框架进行调整。我们发现，STPA的核心概念与步骤适用于AI系统，但需针对不同案例中不同程度出现的AI特定挑战进行针对性调整。我们提出“面向AI系统的流程导向危害分析”（PHASE）作为指导框架，将STPA概念适配于AI系统。依据PHASE指南应用与解读STPA，可为负责管理AI系统危害的分析人员提供四大关键能力：1）识别系统级危害，包括由多种问题累积引发的危害；2）明确承认社会因素对算法危害的贡献；3）建立危害与可缓解责任方之间的可追溯问责链；4）持续监控并缓解新出现的危害。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to address system-level harms in AI that arise from component interactions and development processes, this work adapts System Theoretic Process Analysis (STPA) — a system safety framework — to AI systems. Applied to three case studies (linear regression, reinforcement learning, and transformer models), the study evaluates how STPA’s control-theoretic and systemic perspectives translate to AI contexts, identifying necessary modifications for AI-specific traits like model opacity and output complexity. Results show that core STPA steps remain applicable but require tailored adaptations, leading to the development of PHASE, a guideline for AI hazard analysis. PHASE enables analysts to detect emergent system hazards, account for social factors, establish accountability chains, and sustain hazard monitoring.
为应对AI系统中因组件交互和开发流程引发的系统级危害，本文将系统安全框架——系统理论过程分析（STPA）——适配至AI系统。通过对线性回归、强化学习和Transformer模型的案例研究，作者发现STPA核心概念适用但需调整以应对AI特性如模型不透明性和输出复杂性。提出的PHASE指南使分析人员能识别系统级危害、纳入社会因素影响、建立问责链条并持续监控风险，从而提升AI危害的系统化管理能力。
Reasoning Up the Instruction Ladder for Controllable Language Models
Authors: Zishuo Zheng, Vidhisha Balachandran, Chan Young Park, Faeze Brahman, Sachin Kumar
First: 2025-10-30T22:13:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:09:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As large language model (LLM) based systems take on high-stakes roles in real-world decision-making, they must reconcile competing instructions from multiple sources within a single prompt context. Enforcing an instruction hierarchy, where higher-level directives override lower-priority requests, is critical to the reliability and control of LLMs. In this work, we reframe instruction hierarchy resolution as a reasoning task. The model must first "think" about the relationship between a given user prompt and higher-priority instructions before generating a response. To enable this capability, we construct VerIH, a training dataset of constraint-following tasks with verifiable answers, comprising aligned and conflicting system-user instructions. We show that lightweight reinforcement learning with VerIH effectively transfers general reasoning capabilities of models to instruction prioritization. Our method leads to consistent improvements across multiple model families on both instruction following and instruction hierarchy benchmarks, achieving ~20% absolute improvement in conflict setups. Our method also leads to improved alignment to safety-critical scenarios beyond the training distribution, exhibiting increased robustness against jailbreak and prompt injection, reducing absolute attack success rates by up to 20%. Our results establish reasoning over instruction hierarchies as a practical mechanism for improving AI reliability, where targeted updates to system prompts produce predictable, controllable, and robust changes in model behavior.
中文标题/摘要
标题：为可控语言模型构建指令层级推理机制
随着基于大语言模型（LLM）的系统在现实世界决策中承担高风险角色，它们必须在单个提示上下文中协调多个来源的相互冲突的指令。强制执行指令层级结构——即高层指令优先于低优先级请求——对于LLM的可靠性和可控性至关重要。在本研究中，我们将指令层级解析重构为一个推理任务：模型必须先“思考”给定用户提示与高层指令之间的关系，再生成响应。为此，我们构建了VerIH数据集，该数据集包含可验证答案的约束遵循任务，涵盖对齐与冲突的系统-用户指令。我们证明，使用VerIH进行轻量级强化学习能有效将模型的通用推理能力迁移到指令优先级处理中。该方法在多个模型家族的指令遵循与指令层级基准测试中均带来一致提升，在冲突场景下实现约20%的绝对性能提升。此外，该方法在训练分布之外的安全关键场景中也提升了对齐效果，增强了对 jailbreak 和提示注入攻击的鲁棒性，攻击成功率降低最高达20%。我们的结果确立了在指令层级上进行推理作为提升AI可靠性的实用机制：通过针对性更新系统提示，可预测、可控且稳健地改变模型行为。
Summary / 总结
To enhance reliability in high-stakes decision-making scenarios, this work addresses the challenge of resolving conflicting instructions within a single prompt by treating instruction hierarchy as a reasoning task. The method introduces VerIH, a dataset of constraint-following tasks with verifiable answers that includes aligned and conflicting system-user instructions, and trains models using lightweight reinforcement learning to prioritize higher-level directives. Experimental results show consistent performance gains across multiple model families, with approximately 20% absolute improvement in conflict resolution benchmarks, and enhanced robustness against jailbreak and prompt injection attacks, reducing attack success rates by up to 20%, demonstrating the effectiveness of reasoning-based instruction prioritization for controllable and safer AI behavior.
随着大语言模型承担高风险决策任务，单个提示中处理冲突指令对可靠性至关重要。本文将指令层级解析视为推理任务，要求模型在生成响应前先分析用户提示与高优先级指令之间的优先级关系。为此，作者构建了包含可验证答案的VerIH数据集，涵盖对齐与冲突的系统-用户指令。在该数据集上使用轻量级强化学习训练，可将模型的通用推理能力迁移到指令优先级处理中，在冲突场景下多个模型家族性能提升约20%。该方法还增强了安全对齐能力，使越狱和提示注入攻击成功率降低最高达20%，表明通过针对性系统提示更新可实现可预测、可控且鲁棒的行为变化。
Next-Generation Agentic Reinforcement Learning Systems Enable Self-Evolving Agents
Authors: Ran Yan, Wei Fu, Jiale Li, Shusheng Xu, Zhiyu Mei, Jiaxuan Gao, Jiarui Zhang, Xujie Shen, Hao Dai, Chuyi He, Zhen Pu, Jun Mei, Zhiyao Lin, Haitao Wang, Zhiqiang Ding, Jiawei Zhang, Huaijie Wang, Ruida Xu, Youhe Jiang, Yi Wu, Tongkai Yang, Binhang Yuan
First: 2026-07-01T16:08:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:08:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM agents are rapidly being deployed in production, including coding assistants, customer-support chatbots, and scientific research assistants, yet they remain fundamentally static in enterprise deployment. The LLM weights, system prompts, tool repertoires, and in-context harnesses are frozen at deployment time, and any improvement requires a manual loop of human-curated data collection, offline fine-tuning, modification of the agentic paradigm, and re-deployment. Recent work on self-evolving agents, such as OpenClaw for individual users, indicates that the next leap in agent capability will come from agents that continually learn from their own experience. In this paper, we argue that this vision for self-evolving agent deployment is being held back for enterprise-level large-scale agentic service not by reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms but by agentic online RL systems. Specifically, current agentic RL systems and the surrounding observability software stack are inadequate along three essential aspects: (i) there is no standardized agent trajectory data protocol capable of carrying RL learning signals at step granularity across heterogeneous agent paradigms; (ii) there is no enterprise-grade comprehensive data proxy that converts real workloads into governed learning substrates; and (iii) there is no unified agent evolution control plane that automatically decides, based on trajectory statistics, when to update policy weights or evolve the in-context harness. The next generation of agentic RL systems must be co-designed around these three pillars, and we sketch concrete architectures, case studies, and counter-arguments. We instantiate one branch through AReaL2.0, reorganizing existing RL infrastructure into an agent-oriented online RL loop for policy weight updates from deployed workloads.
中文标题/摘要
标题：下一代代理强化学习系统使自演进代理成为可能
大型语言模型（LLM）代理正迅速投入生产环境，包括代码助手、客户支持聊天机器人和科研助手，但在企业部署中它们本质上仍是静态的。LLM权重、系统提示、工具集和上下文工具包在部署时即被冻结，任何改进都需要人工循环的数据收集、离线微调、代理范式修改及重新部署。近期关于自演进代理的研究（如面向个人用户的OpenClaw）表明，代理能力的下一次飞跃将来自能持续从自身经验中学习的代理。本文认为，对于企业级大规模代理服务而言，阻碍自演进代理部署的并非强化学习（RL）算法本身，而是代理在线RL系统。具体而言，当前代理RL系统及其周边可观测性软件栈在三个方面存在不足：（i）缺乏标准化的代理轨迹数据协议，无法在异构代理范式间以步骤粒度传递RL学习信号；（ii）缺乏企业级综合数据代理，将真实工作负载转化为受控的学习基底；（iii）缺乏统一的代理演进控制平面，无法根据轨迹统计自动决定何时更新策略权重或演进上下文工具包。下一代代理RL系统必须围绕这三大支柱协同设计，本文概述了具体架构、案例研究及反驳观点。我们通过AReaL2.0实现其中一路径，将现有RL基础设施重组为面向代理的在线RL循环，以从已部署工作负载中更新策略权重。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the static nature of LLM agents in enterprise settings, where improvements require manual re-deployment cycles, this paper argues that the bottleneck lies not in RL algorithms but in the lack of infrastructure for self-evolving agents. The authors propose three critical missing components: a standardized trajectory data protocol for RL signals, an enterprise-grade data proxy to convert real workloads into learnable data, and a unified control plane to autonomously trigger policy updates. They validate this approach by building AReaL2.0, which restructures existing RL infrastructure into an agent-oriented online loop for continuous policy updates from live deployments, demonstrating feasibility and scalability in real-world agent systems.
鉴于当前企业级LLM代理在部署后权重与工具集冻结、无法持续学习的静态特性，本文指出阻碍自进化代理落地的核心并非强化学习算法本身，而是缺乏支持在线学习的基础设施。文章识别出三大关键缺失：缺乏标准化的代理轨迹数据协议、缺少能将真实工作负载转化为学习信号的企业级数据代理、以及无统一控制平面依据轨迹统计自动触发策略更新。作者提出下一代代理强化学习系统应围绕这三大支柱协同设计，并通过AReaL2.0实例化，重构现有RL基础设施为面向代理的在线学习循环，实现从部署工作负载中持续更新策略权重。
MoHallBench: A Benchmark for Motion Hallucination in Video Large Language Models
Authors: Jiale Li, Sihan Chen, Mengyuan Liu
First: 2026-07-01T16:04:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:04:24+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Video Large Language Models (VideoLLMs) have shown strong progress in video understanding, yet they still suffer from hallucinations that are inconsistent with visual evidence. Existing benchmarks mainly focus on object hallucination or coarse action perception, leaving a key video-specific problem underexplored: motion hallucination, in which models infer human motions that are absent from the video. We present MoHallBench, a benchmark for diagnosing motion hallucination in VideoLLMs. MoHallBench systematically evaluates three major sources of hallucination: co-occurrence priors, sequential inference, and similarity confusion. It contains 11,306 video clips and 40,493 question-answer pairs, covering binary-choice, multiple-choice, and generative settings. We further introduce a bi-directional questioning protocol with bias-aware metrics to reduce affirmation bias in binary evaluation. Experiments on ten recent open-source VideoLLMs reveal a clear decoupling between action recognition and hallucination resistance, as models that perform well on positive action recognition often fail on adversarial negatives. Among all settings, sequential inference hallucination is the most severe, showing that current models tend to over-infer expected outcomes from partial motion cues. Our analyses further confirm that stronger priors and finer-grained similarity substantially amplify hallucination. We hope MoHallBench can facilitate future evaluation and mitigation of motion hallucination in VideoLLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MoHallBench：视频大语言模型中运动幻觉的基准
视频大语言模型（VideoLLMs）在视频理解方面取得了显著进展，但仍存在与视觉证据不一致的幻觉问题。现有基准主要关注物体幻觉或粗粒度动作感知，而视频特有的关键问题——运动幻觉（即模型推断视频中不存在的人类运动）尚未被充分探索。我们提出了MoHallBench，用于诊断VideoLLMs中的运动幻觉。该基准系统性评估了三大幻觉来源：共现先验、序列推理和相似性混淆。它包含11,306个视频片段和40,493个问答对，涵盖二选一、多选和生成式设置。我们进一步引入双向提问协议与偏见感知度量，以减少二选一评估中的肯定偏倚。在十种近期开源VideoLLMs上的实验表明，动作识别能力与抗幻觉能力明显解耦：表现良好的正向动作识别模型往往在对抗性负样本上失败。在所有设置中，序列推理幻觉最为严重，表明当前模型倾向于从部分运动线索过度推断预期结果。我们的分析进一步证实，更强的先验和更精细的相似性会显著加剧幻觉。我们希望MoHallBench能促进未来对VideoLLMs中运动幻觉的评估与缓解。

Motivated by the persistent issue of motion hallucination in VideoLLMs—where models fabricate nonexistent human motions—this work introduces MoHallBench, a comprehensive benchmark designed to diagnose such hallucinations. The benchmark evaluates three primary sources: co-occurrence priors, sequential inference, and similarity confusion, using 11,306 video clips and 40,493 QA pairs across multiple formats. Towards Developing a Multimodal Chat Assistant for University Stakeholders: RAG-based Approach
Authors: Md Abu Hanif Shaikh, Abdullah Al Shafi
Venue: 2025 28th International Conference on Computer and Information Technology (ICCIT)
First: 2026-07-01T16:03:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:03:03+00:00
Comments: Accepted at 2025 28th International Conference on Computer and Information Technology (ICCIT)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
University stakeholders often face difficulties in accessing timely and reliable information, especially in developing countries, where there are very few intelligent support systems. Existing rule-based chatbots are unable to handle complex, domain-specific queries and are not well-equipped to adapt to evolving institutional policies. As a fill-in-the-gap solution, we present the multimodal university chatbot with retrieval-augmented generation. The system combines the large language model with semantic retrieval to produce context-based responses from institution-centric resources, such as the university handbook. The system accepts text and image queries through the vision-language model and applies quantized inference for rapid deployment on constrained hardware. A scalable backend built with FastAPI, adjoined with a responsive frontend developed with Next.js, ensures real-time usability. Our multimodal evaluation demonstrates that the system maintains strong satisfaction scores across both text and image queries, despite increased response time for visual inputs. Furthermore, quantitative evaluation shows that hallucination is reduced from 31.7% to 6.6% in our proposed RAG-based system, confirming the effectiveness of retrieval grounding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向大学利益相关者开发多模态聊天助手：基于RAG的方法

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of intelligent support systems in universities, especially in developing countries, this paper introduces a multimodal chatbot using a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) approach to deliver context-aware responses from institutional resources. The system integrates a large language model with semantic retrieval and a vision-language model to handle both text and image queries, employing quantized inference for efficient deployment. Experimental results show the system achieves high user satisfaction across query types, with response times slightly longer for images, and significantly reduces hallucination from 31.7% to 6.6%, validating the effectiveness of retrieval grounding.
