FAR: Failure-Aware Retry for Test-Time Recovery and Continual Policy Improvement
Authors: Haoran Hao, Shahram Najam Syed, Jeffrey Ichnowski, Jeff Schneider
First: 2026-07-01T16:01:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:01:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robot policies inevitably encounter failures when deployed in real environments. Naive retries often repeat the same mistakes, while many existing recovery methods rely on human intervention. In this paper, we propose Failure-Aware Retry (FAR), a framework that enables robots to learn from previous failures at test time, adapt their behavior accordingly, and eventually complete the task autonomously. FAR combines Failure-Contrastive Preference Adaptation, which constructs preference learning data from failures to steer the policy away from previously unsuccessful behaviors, with lightweight action perturbations during retries to encourage local exploration. We further incorporate successful recovery trajectories into a training loop for continual policy improvement. Experiments in both simulation and real-world manipulation tasks show that FAR substantially improves success rates and robustness, with average gains of 17.6% over the standard diffusion policy in simulation and 11.7% in the real world. In addition, FAR significantly improves data efficiency under both reset and timestep budgets during continual policy improvement by exploiting informative failure cases.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FAR：面向测试时恢复与持续策略改进的故障感知重试
机器人策略在真实环境中部署时不可避免地会遭遇失败。盲目重试往往重复相同错误，而许多现有恢复方法依赖人工干预。本文提出故障感知重试（FAR）框架，使机器人能在测试时从过往失败中学习，调整行为并最终自主完成任务。FAR结合故障对比偏好适应（通过构建失败数据引导策略远离先前失败行为）与轻量级重试动作扰动（鼓励局部探索），并进一步将成功恢复轨迹纳入训练循环以实现持续策略改进。仿真与真实操作任务实验表明，FAR显著提升成功率与鲁棒性，在仿真环境中较标准扩散策略平均提升17.6%，在真实世界中提升11.7%。此外，FAR在持续策略改进过程中，通过利用信息丰富的失败案例，在重置与时间步预算下显著提升数据效率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robots to autonomously recover from failures without human intervention, FAR introduces a test-time recovery framework that learns from past failures to avoid repeating mistakes. It combines failure-contrastive preference adaptation to steer policy away from unsuccessful behaviors and lightweight action perturbations to encourage local exploration during retries. The framework further integrates successful recovery trajectories into a continual learning loop to improve policy over time. Experiments show FAR boosts success rates by 17.6% in simulation and 11.7% in real-world tasks, while also enhancing data efficiency under both reset and timestep constraints.
为使机器人能在无外部干预下从失败中自主恢复，FAR 提出了一种测试时恢复框架，通过学习历史失败经验避免重复错误。该框架结合失败对比偏好适应，引导策略远离失败行为，并在重试时引入轻量级动作扰动以促进局部探索，同时将成功恢复轨迹纳入持续学习循环以优化策略。实验表明，FAR 在仿真环境中成功率提升17.6%，在真实世界任务中提升11.7%，且在重置与时间步预算下显著提升数据效率。
Deep learning with missing data
Authors: Tianyi Ma, Tengyao Wang, Richard J. Samworth
First: 2025-04-21T18:57:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:01:20+00:00
Comments: 57 pages, 13 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In the context of multivariate nonparametric regression with missing covariates, we propose Pattern Embedded Neural Networks (PENNs), which can be applied in conjunction with any existing imputation technique. In addition to a neural network trained on the imputed data, PENNs pass the vectors of observation indicators through a second neural network to provide a compact representation. The outputs are then combined in a third neural network to produce final predictions. Our main theoretical result exploits an assumption that the observation patterns can be partitioned into cells on which the Bayes regression function behaves similarly, and belongs to a compositional Hölder class. It provides a finite-sample excess risk bound that holds for an arbitrary missingness mechanism, and in combination with a complementary minimax lower bound, demonstrates that our PENN estimator attains in typical cases the minimax rate of convergence as if the cells of the partition were known in advance, up to a poly-logarithmic factor in the sample size. Numerical experiments on simulated, semi-synthetic and real data confirm that the PENN estimator consistently improves, often dramatically, on standard neural networks without pattern embedding. Code to reproduce our experiments, as well as a tutorial on how to apply our method, is publicly available.
中文标题/摘要
标题：缺失数据下的深度学习
在多元非参数回归且协变量存在缺失的背景下，我们提出了模式嵌入神经网络（PENNs），可与任何现有插补技术结合使用。除在插补数据上训练的神经网络外，PENNs还将观测指示向量通过第二个神经网络进行处理，以提供紧凑表示。随后在第三个神经网络中结合输出，生成最终预测。我们的主要理论结果基于一个假设：观测模式可划分为若干单元，在每个单元内，贝叶斯回归函数行为相似，且属于组合Hölder类。该结果提供了一个对任意缺失机制均成立的有限样本超额风险界，并结合互补的最小最大下界，表明在典型情况下，我们的PENN估计量在已知划分单元的前提下达到最小最大收敛速率，仅差样本规模的对数多项式因子。在模拟、半合成和真实数据上的数值实验验证了PENN估计量相比无模式嵌入的标准神经网络通常显著提升性能。相关实验代码及方法应用教程已公开。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of handling missing covariates in multivariate nonparametric regression, this paper introduces Pattern Embedded Neural Networks (PENNs), which integrate observation pattern information via a dual neural network architecture: one processes imputed data, another encodes missingness indicators, and a third combines both for prediction. Theoretical analysis assumes observation patterns can be grouped into cells with similar Bayes regression behavior, leading to a finite-sample excess risk bound that holds under arbitrary missingness mechanisms. This bound, combined with a minimax lower bound, shows PENNs achieve near-minimax convergence rates up to a poly-logarithmic factor. Empirical results across simulated, semi-synthetic, and real datasets demonstrate consistent and often substantial performance gains over standard neural networks without pattern embedding.
为解决多元非参数回归中协变量缺失的问题，本文提出模式嵌入神经网络（PENNs），通过三阶段架构整合观测模式指示器：先进行数据填补，再用神经网络编码模式，最后结合预测。理论分析假设观测模式可划分为具有相似贝叶斯回归行为的单元，推导出适用于任意缺失机制的有限样本超额风险界，结合最小最大下界表明PENNs在典型情况下可达到近最优收敛速率（至多多对数因子）。在模拟、半合成及真实数据上的实验显示，PENNs相较无模式嵌入的标准神经网络表现稳定且常有显著提升。
Fault of Our Stars: Behavioral Drivers of Rating-Sentiment Incongruence
Authors: Ramanaish Abaiyan, Ruththiragayan Sutharsan, Kusal Amantha, Anusan Krishnathas, Asma Rauff, Kovindarajah Sriyathurshan, Patalee Narasinghe, Nirasha Munasinghe, Nisansa de Silva, Sandareka Wickramanayake
First: 2026-06-24T07:51:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T16:00:49+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 3 figures. Submitted to MerCon 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When people share experiences online, they often express thoughts in two ways: a star rating and a written review. In sentiment analysis, ratings are widely used as convenient weak labels for textual sentiment, yet whether the two actually agree is rarely questioned. This study investigates sentiment-rating incongruence, where the sentiment expressed in review text differs from the sentiment implied by the assigned star rating, in Sri Lankan tourism attraction reviews. A dataset of 16,156 reviews from 2010 to 2023 is analyzed using a transformer-based sentiment pipeline that derives textual sentiment independently of assigned ratings. Incongruence occurs in 18.6% of reviews and falls into six directional patterns, with Conservative Rater and Obligatory 5-Star behaviors accounting for the majority of mismatches. Prevalence also varies across venue types, with museums showing the highest rates. Statistical tests, logistic regression, Random Forest, and SHAP analysis identify venue type, reviewer expertise, review length, and temporal factors as contributors to rating-text divergence. Overall, this study demonstrates that star ratings are not interchangeable with textual sentiment and should be validated before being treated as ground-truth labels in NLP.

标题：我们星评的缺陷：评分与情感表达不一致的行为驱动因素

Summary / 总结
This study investigates the mismatch between star ratings and textual sentiment in Sri Lankan tourism reviews, motivated by the common assumption that ratings reliably reflect sentiment. Using a transformer-based sentiment model to independently assess review text, researchers found that sentiment-rating incongruence occurs in 18.6% of 16,156 reviews, with six directional patterns identified. 该研究旨在探究斯里兰卡旅游景点评论中星级评分与文本情感之间的不一致现象，动机在于当前普遍将评分视为可靠的情感代理。通过基于Transformer的文本情感模型独立评估情感，研究发现16,156条评论中有18.6%存在不一致，分为六种方向模式。关键驱动因素包括场所类型（如博物馆不一致率最高）、评论者专业性、评论长度及时间趋势，经逻辑回归、随机森林和SHAP分析确认。结果表明，星级评分不可直接等同于文本情感，应在自然语言处理任务中作为真实标签前进行验证。
Technical Report: Asynchronous Distributed Trajectory Estimation of Multi-Robot Systems
Authors: Adam Pooley, Matthew Hale
First: 2026-07-01T15:57:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T15:57:28+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Distributed trajectory estimation arises in many applications across robotics, but existing implementations typically do not consider asynchrony in agents' communications and computations. Therefore, we propose an asynchronous block coordinate descent algorithm for distributed trajectory estimation. We consider a team of agents that observes a team of robots and estimates their states over a sliding window. The agents solve an approximation of the maximum a posteriori estimation problem, which we derive. We show this approximation introduces negligible errors and eliminates up to 96.9% of communications among agents. Next, we prove that agents' iterates converge exponentially fast to the optimal estimate of the robots' states. Simulations show that this approach has up to 64% less error than a comparable state-of-the-art algorithm. Experiments on mobile robots show the robustness of this approach to delays whose lengths span three orders of magnitude.
中文标题/摘要
标题：技术报告：多机器人系统的异步分布式轨迹估计
分布式轨迹估计在机器人学的许多应用中出现，但现有实现通常未考虑代理间通信与计算的异步性。因此，我们提出了一种用于分布式轨迹估计的异步块坐标下降算法。我们考虑一组代理观测一组机器人，并在滑动窗口内估计其状态。代理求解我们推导出的最大后验概率估计问题的近似解。我们证明该近似引入的误差可忽略，并能消除高达96.9%的代理间通信。接着，我们证明代理的迭代值以指数速度收敛至机器人状态的最优估计。仿真表明，该方法相比同类最先进算法误差低达64%。在移动机器人上的实验表明，该方法对延迟（跨度达三个数量级）具有鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient distributed trajectory estimation in multi-robot systems where agent communications and computations are asynchronous, this paper proposes an asynchronous block coordinate descent algorithm. The method enables a team of agents to estimate robot states over a sliding window by solving an approximate maximum a posteriori problem, which introduces negligible error while reducing inter-agent communication by up to 96.9%. Theoretical analysis proves exponential convergence of agent iterates to the optimal state estimate, and simulations demonstrate up to 64% lower estimation error compared to state-of-the-art methods. Experiments on mobile robots confirm the algorithm’s robustness to communication delays spanning three orders of magnitude.
为解决多机器人系统中分布式轨迹估计在通信与计算异步环境下效率低的问题，本文提出一种异步块坐标下降算法。该方法使一组代理在滑动窗口内通过求解近似最大后验估计问题来估计机器人状态，该近似误差可忽略，同时减少通信量高达96.9%。理论证明代理迭代呈指数收敛至最优估计值，仿真显示其误差比现有最先进方法低64%。在移动机器人实验中，该方法对跨越三个数量级的通信延迟表现出鲁棒性。
Quantitative Understanding of PDF Fits and their Uncertainties
Authors: Amedeo Chiefa, Luigi Del Debbio, Richard Kenway
Venue: Eur. Phys. J. C 86, 722 (2026)
First: 2025-12-30T09:53:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T15:57:21+00:00
Comments: 38 pages, 31 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Parton Distribution Functions (PDFs) play a central role in describing experimental data at colliders and provide insight into the structure of nucleons. As the LHC enters an era of high-precision measurements, a robust PDF determination with a reliable uncertainty quantification has become mandatory in order to match the experimental precision. The NNPDF collaboration has pioneered the use of Machine Learning (ML) techniques for PDF determinations, using Neural Networks (NNs) to parametrise the unknown PDFs in a flexible and unbiased way. The NNs are then trained on experimental data by means of stochastic gradient descent algorithms. The statistical robustness of the results is validated by extensive closure tests using synthetic data. In this work, we develop a theoretical framework based on the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) to analyse the training dynamics of neural networks. This approach allows us to derive, under precise assumptions, an analytical description of the neural network evolution during training, enabling a quantitative understanding of the training process. Having an analytical handle on the training dynamics allows us to clarify the role of the NN architecture and the impact of the experimental data in a transparent way. Similarly, we are able to describe the evolution of the covariance of the NN output during training, providing a quantitative description of how uncertainties are propagated from the data to the fitted function. While our results are not a substitute for PDF fitting, they do provide a powerful diagnostic tool to assess the robustness of current fitting methodologies. Beyond its relevance for particle physics phenomenology, our analysis of PDF determinations provides a testbed to apply theoretical ideas about the learning process developed in the ML community.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PDF拟合及其不确定性的定量理解
部分子分布函数（PDFs）在描述对撞机实验数据和揭示核子结构方面起着核心作用。随着大型强子对撞机（LHC）进入高精度测量时代，为匹配实验精度，必须建立具有可靠不确定度量化能力的稳健PDF确定方法。NNPDF合作组率先将机器学习（ML）技术应用于PDF确定，利用神经网络（NNs）以灵活且无偏的方式参数化未知PDF，并通过随机梯度下降算法在实验数据上训练NNs。结果的统计稳健性通过使用合成数据进行大量闭合测试验证。本工作中，我们基于神经切线核（NTK）构建理论框架，分析神经网络的训练动力学。该方法在精确假设下，可推导出神经网络在训练过程中的解析演化描述，从而实现对训练过程的定量理解。通过对训练动力学的解析把握，我们能清晰阐明NN架构的作用及实验数据的影响。同时，我们还能描述NN输出协方差在训练过程中的演化，从而定量描述不确定度如何从数据传播至拟合函数。尽管我们的结果不能替代PDF拟合，但它们为评估当前拟合方法的稳健性提供了强大诊断工具。此外，本对PDF确定性的分析也为机器学习社区提出的关于学习过程的理论思想提供了应用测试平台。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for precise and reliable uncertainty quantification in Parton Distribution Function (PDF) determinations at high-precision colliders, this work employs a theoretical framework based on the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) to analyze the training dynamics of neural networks used in PDF fitting. By deriving an analytical description of how neural networks evolve during training under specific assumptions, the study clarifies the influence of network architecture and experimental data on the final fit. It further quantifies how uncertainties propagate from input data to the fitted PDFs through the covariance evolution of the network output. While not replacing full PDF fitting, the method provides a diagnostic tool to assess the robustness of current methodologies and offers a bridge between machine learning theory and high-energy physics phenomenology.
随着大型强子对撞机进入高精度测量时代，对部分子分布函数（PDF）的确定及其不确定性量化提出了更高要求。本文基于神经切线核（NTK）理论框架，对用于PDF拟合的神经网络训练过程进行分析，推导出在精确假设下神经网络训练过程的解析描述，从而明确网络结构与实验数据对拟合结果的影响，并定量刻画不确定性从数据到拟合函数的传播过程。实验结果表明，该方法能有效捕捉训练动态与输出协方差演化，为评估当前PDF拟合方法的稳健性提供有力诊断工具，而不替代现有拟合流程。
SynLaD: Latent Diffusion for Generating Synthesizable Molecules Conditioned on 3D Pharmacophore Profiles
Authors: Miruna Cretu, John Bradshaw, Patricia Suriana, Saeed Saremi, Omar Mahmood, Kirill Shmilovich, Kangway Chuang, Vishnu Sresht, Colin Grambow
First: 2026-07-01T15:57:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T15:57:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present SynLaD, a latent diffusion framework for small-molecule generation that unifies ligand-based drug design objectives (what to make) with synthetic accessibility (how to make it). Current models typically optimize one objective at the expense of the other, creating a bottleneck for discovering high-scoring and synthesizable molecules. SynLaD combines reaction-constrained generation with pharmacophore-conditioned 3D design by learning a latent space that decodes to both 3D structures and synthesis pathways. An encoder maps molecules to a latent representation used by two decoder heads: (i) a geometric head that reconstructs atom types and coordinates and (ii) an autoregressive synthesis head that outputs synthetic routes in a serialized, reaction-based notation. A diffusion transformer generates novel latents in the learned space, conditioned on pharmacophore profiles. Across analogue generation tasks for bioactive ligands, SynLaD outperforms existing baselines in synthesizable and diverse hit generation, demonstrating that a single model can produce shape-aligned molecules with feasible synthesis plans.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SynLaD：基于3D药效团轮廓生成可合成分子的潜在扩散模型
我们提出了SynLaD，一种用于小分子生成的潜在扩散框架，将基于配体的药物设计目标（应生成什么）与合成可及性（如何生成）统一起来。当前模型通常只优化其中一个目标，牺牲另一个，导致发现高评分且可合成分子的瓶颈。SynLaD通过学习一个可解码为3D结构和合成路径的潜在空间，结合反应约束生成与药效团条件化3D设计。编码器将分子映射为潜在表示，供两个解码器头使用：(i) 一个几何头，重建原子类型和坐标；(ii) 一个自回归合成头，以序列化、基于反应的记法输出合成路线。扩散变换器在学习到的潜在空间中生成新颖潜在表示，条件于药效团轮廓。在生物活性配体的类药生成任务中，SynLaD在可合成且多样化的命中生成方面优于现有基线，证明单一模型可生成形状匹配且具备可行合成方案的分子。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to simultaneously optimize drug design objectives and synthetic feasibility, SynLaD introduces a latent diffusion framework that integrates 3D pharmacophore conditioning with reaction-constrained synthesis pathways. The method employs a dual-decoder architecture: one reconstructing 3D molecular geometry and the other generating serialized synthetic routes, both guided by a diffusion transformer conditioned on pharmacophore profiles. Experimental results across bioactive ligand analogue generation tasks show SynLaD outperforms existing models in producing both high-scoring and synthesizable molecules, demonstrating its ability to generate shape-aligned structures with feasible synthesis plans.
为解决当前分子生成模型难以兼顾生物活性与合成可行性的瓶颈，SynLaD提出一种基于潜在扩散的统一框架。该模型通过双解码器结构，一个重建分子三维结构，另一个生成反应序列合成路径，均以药效团特征为条件。实验表明，SynLaD在生成多样且高分的可合成分子方面优于现有方法，证明单一模型可有效平衡分子设计质量与合成可行性。
CausalMix: Data Mixture as Causal Inference for Language Model Training
Authors: Zinan Tang, Yukun Zhang, Shaomian Zheng, Zhuoshi Pan, Qizhi Pei, Dingnan Jin, Jun Zhou, Yujun Wang, Biqing Huang
First: 2026-07-01T15:56:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T15:56:11+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In Large Language Model (LLM) training, data mixing plays a pivotal role in determining model performance. Recent methods optimize mixture weights via proxy models, but they rely on the assumption of static data distributions. As a result, when the underlying data pool shifts, these methods require costly retraining from scratch. This limitation restricts their ability to scale seamlessly from small settings to larger data pools and model sizes. In this paper, we propose CausalMix to address this limitation by casting data mixture optimization as a causal inference problem. We formulate the statistical features of the data pool as covariates and the domain mixture as the treatment. After fitting a causal model on 512 runs of Qwen2.5-0.5B to estimate the Conditional Average Treatment Effect (CATE), we extrapolate the optimal mixture for an 800K data pool and apply it to train a 7B model. Furthermore, we successfully generalize the framework to long chain-of-thought data on Qwen3-4B-Base. By leveraging causal modeling to isolate confounding biases, CausalMix dynamically infers state-dependent optimal data mixtures. Extensive experiments show that the mixture guided by CausalMix consistently improves performance across multiple downstream tasks, outperforming RegMix and other baselines. In addition, we use the CATE Interpreter to provide visual analysis of the learned mixing strategy. Overall, CausalMix offers a causal and interpretable framework for optimizing LLM data mixtures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CausalMix：将数据混合视为因果推断以优化语言模型训练
在大语言模型（LLM）训练中，数据混合对模型性能起关键作用。近期方法通过代理模型优化混合权重，但依赖静态数据分布假设。当底层数据池变化时，这些方法需昂贵地从头重训练，限制其从小规模设置无缝扩展至更大数据池和模型规模。本文提出CausalMix，将数据混合优化视为因果推断问题：将数据池的统计特征作为协变量，领域混合作为处理变量。在Qwen2.5-0.5B的512次运行上拟合因果模型以估计条件平均处理效应（CATE），并外推至800K数据池，用于训练7B模型。同时，框架成功推广至Qwen3-4B-Base的长链思维数据。通过因果建模隔离混杂偏差，CausalMix动态推断状态依赖的最优数据混合。大量实验表明，CausalMix引导的混合策略在多个下游任务中持续提升性能，优于RegMix及其他基线。此外，使用CATE解释器提供混合策略的可视化分析。总体而言，CausalMix提供了一种因果且可解释的框架，用于优化LLM数据混合。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of static data mixture optimization in large language model training, CausalMix reframes data mixing as a causal inference problem, treating statistical features as covariates and domain mixture as treatment. By fitting a causal model on 512 runs of Qwen2.5-0.5B to estimate Conditional Average Treatment Effect (CATE), it extrapolates optimal mixtures for larger data pools and scales to train a 7B model. Experiments show CausalMix consistently outperforms baselines like RegMix across downstream tasks and generalizes to long chain-of-thought data on Qwen3-4B-Base, with visual interpretability via CATE Interpreter.
为解决大语言模型训练中静态数据混合策略效率低的问题，CausalMix将数据混合优化建模为因果推断问题，将数据池统计特征作为协变量，混合策略作为处理变量。通过对Qwen2.5-0.5B的512次运行拟合因果模型以估计条件平均处理效应（CATE），成功外推至800K数据池并用于训练7B模型，实验表明其在多个下游任务中持续优于RegMix等基线方法，并可泛化至Qwen3-4B-Base的长链推理数据，同时通过CATE解释器提供可视化分析。
Clinician-Level Agreement Without Clinical Caution: LLM Evaluator Limits in Medical AI Benchmarking
Authors: William Philipp, Finn Fassbender, Thorsten Langer, Martje Pauly, Rebecca Herzog, Alexander Baumann, Markus Hobert, Theresa Paulus, Ip Chi Wang, Lukas Goede, Johanna Reimer, Sebastian Löns, Ronald Böck, Sebastian Fudickar
First: 2026-07-01T15:55:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T15:55:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Open-response evaluation provides stronger clinical validity than multiple-choice benchmarks but creates a scoring bottleneck that motivates automated LLM-asa-Judge approaches. Whether such evaluators replicate clinical calibration and caution, however, remains untested. We introduce MedQADE, the first standardised open-response clinical benchmark for German, a major clinical language lacking native evaluation infrastructure, comprising 3,800 items annotated by ten practising physicians and nine Large Language Model (LLM) evaluators. The top-performing evaluator model, Gemini 3 Flash, reached alignment consistent with the physician ceiling (\k{appa} = 0.694 vs. \k{appa} = 0.709), though wide confidence intervals limit interpretation. Despite this statistical alignment, automated evaluators exhibited near-absent clinical metacognition: physicians scaled abstention with item difficulty, while frontier models assigned definitive scores in every case. We additionally quantified systematic lineage-dependent biases, where models preferentially scored architectural siblings, an effect independent of language. These results show that statistical alignment does not ensure clinical caution, and that evaluator independence requires explicit verification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：临床级一致性无需临床谨慎：大语言模型评估者在医学AI基准测试中的局限
开放式回答评估比多项选择题基准具有更强的临床有效性，但会引发评分瓶颈，从而推动自动化LLM-as-a-Judge方法。然而，此类评估者是否能复制临床校准与谨慎，尚未经过检验。我们引入MedQADE，这是首个针对德语的标准化开放式回答临床基准，德语是缺乏原生评估基础设施的重要临床语言，包含3800项由十名执业医师和九名大语言模型（LLM）评估者标注的数据。表现最佳的评估模型Gemini 3 Flash达到与医师天花板一致的对齐度（κ=0.694 vs. κ=0.709），但宽泛的置信区间限制了解释。尽管存在统计对齐，自动化评估者表现出近乎缺失的临床元认知：医师会根据题目难度调整回避评分，而前沿模型在所有情况下均给出确定性评分。我们还量化了系统性血缘依赖偏差，即模型倾向于优先评分架构兄弟模型，且该效应独立于语言。这些结果表明，统计对齐并不能确保临床谨慎，评估者独立性需明确验证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for clinically valid yet scalable evaluation methods in medical AI, this study introduces MedQADE, the first standardized open-response benchmark for German clinical language, annotated by physicians and LLMs. The top-performing LLM evaluator, Gemini 3 Flash, achieved statistical alignment with physician scoring (κ = 0.694 vs. 0.709), yet failed to replicate clinical caution: physicians adjusted scoring based on item difficulty and often abstained, while LLMs assigned definitive scores universally. Systematic biases were also observed, with models favoring architectural siblings regardless of language, underscoring that statistical agreement does not imply clinical judgment fidelity and that evaluator independence must be explicitly verified.
为解决医学AI评估中临床有效性与可扩展性之间的矛盾，本研究构建了首个针对德语临床语言的标准化开放式评估基准MedQADE，由医师与LLM共同标注。表现最佳的LLM评估器Gemini 3 Flash虽在统计上与医师评分一致（κ=0.694 vs. 0.709），但缺乏临床元认知能力——医师会根据题目难度调整评分，而LLM则对所有题目给出确定性评分。此外，研究发现模型存在系统性架构偏好，倾向于评分同架构的模型，且该偏好与语言无关，表明评估器独立性需经显式验证才能确保。
TCMA: Text-Conditioned Multi-granularity Alignment for Drone Cross-Modal Text-Video Retrieval
Authors: Zixu Zhao, Yang Zhan, Yunhao Li, Yan Li
First: 2025-10-11T11:38:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T15:54:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) have become powerful platforms for real-time, high-resolution data collection, producing massive volumes of aerial videos. Efficient retrieval of relevant content from these videos is crucial for applications in urban management, emergency response, security, and disaster relief. While text-video retrieval has advanced in natural video domains, the UAV domain remains underexplored due to limitations in existing datasets, such as coarse and redundant captions. Thus, in this work, we construct the Drone Video-Text Match Dataset (DVTMD), which contains 2,864 videos and 14,320 fine-grained, semantically diverse captions. The annotations capture multiple complementary aspects, including human actions, objects, background settings, environmental conditions, and visual style, thereby enhancing text-video correspondence and reducing redundancy. Building on this dataset, we propose the Text-Conditioned Multi-granularity Alignment (TCMA) framework, which integrates global video-sentence alignment, sentence-guided frame aggregation, and word-guided patch alignment. To further refine local alignment, we design a Word and Patch Selection module that filters irrelevant content, as well as a Text-Adaptive Dynamic Temperature Mechanism that adapts attention sharpness to text type. Extensive experiments on DVTMD and CapERA establish the first complete benchmark for drone text-video retrieval. Our TCMA achieves state-of-the-art performance, including 45.5% R@1 in text-to-video and 42.8% R@1 in video-to-text retrieval, demonstrating the effectiveness of our dataset and method. The code and dataset will be released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TCMA：基于文本条件的多粒度对齐用于无人机跨模态图文检索
无人机（UAV）已成为实时高分辨率数据采集的强大平台，产生大量航拍视频。从这些视频中高效检索相关内容对城市治理、应急响应、安全和灾害救援等应用至关重要。尽管自然视频领域的图文检索已取得进展，但无人机领域因现有数据集存在粗略且冗余的字幕描述而研究不足。为此，本文构建了无人机视频-文本匹配数据集（DVTMD），包含2,864个视频和14,320条细粒度、语义多样的字幕。标注内容涵盖人类动作、物体、背景设置、环境条件和视觉风格等多个互补方面，从而增强图文对应性并减少冗余。基于该数据集，我们提出文本条件多粒度对齐（TCMA）框架，融合全局视频-句子对齐、句子引导的帧聚合和词引导的块对齐。为进一步优化局部对齐，我们设计了词与块选择模块以过滤无关内容，以及文本自适应动态温度机制，根据文本类型调整注意力锐度。在DVTMD和CapERA上的大量实验建立了首个完整的无人机图文检索基准。我们的TCMA取得当前最优性能，包括文本到视频检索R@1为45.5%，视频到文本检索R@1为42.8%，验证了数据集与方法的有效性。代码与数据集将公开发布。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for efficient content retrieval in drone-collected aerial videos for urban management and emergency response, this work addresses the underexplored UAV text-video retrieval domain due to insufficient and redundant captions. The authors construct the Drone Video-Text Match Dataset (DVTMD), featuring 2,864 videos with 14,320 fine-grained, semantically diverse captions covering multiple visual aspects. Building on this dataset, they propose TCMA, a framework that aligns text and video at multiple granularities—global video-sentence, sentence-guided frame, and word-guided patch levels—while incorporating a Word and Patch Selection module to filter noise and a Text-Adaptive Dynamic Temperature Mechanism to adjust attention sharpness. Experiments on DVTMD and CapERA show TCMA achieves state-of-the-art results: 45.5% R@1 for text-to-video and 42.8% R@1 for video-to-text retrieval, validating both the dataset’s quality and the method’s effectiveness.
为应对无人机视频在城市管理、应急响应等场景中高效内容检索的需求，本文针对现有无人机文本-视频数据集粗略冗余的问题，构建了包含2864段视频与14320条细粒度语义丰富标注的Drone Video-Text Match Dataset（DVTMD），涵盖人物动作、物体、背景、环境与视觉风格等多维度信息。在此基础上，提出TCMA框架，通过全局视频-句子对齐、句子引导帧聚合与词引导块对齐实现多粒度对齐，并引入词与块选择模块过滤无关内容，以及文本自适应动态温度机制调节注意力强度。在DVTMD与CapERA数据集上的实验表明，TCMA在文本到视频和视频到文本检索中分别达到45.5%和42.8%的R@1精度，验证了数据集与方法的有效性。
SemEval-2026 Task 9: Detecting Multilingual, Multicultural and Multievent Online Polarization
Authors: Usman Naseem, Robert Geislinger, Juan Ren, Sarah Kohail, Rudy Garrido Veliz, P Sam Sahil, Yiran Zhang, Marco Antonio Stranisci, Idris Abdulmumin, Özge Alaçam, Cengiz Acartürk, Aisha Jabr, Saba Anwar, Abinew Ali Ayele, Elena Tutubalina, Aung Kyaw Htet, Xintong Wang, Surendrabikram Thapa, Tanmoy Chakraborty, Dheeraj Kodati, Sahar Moradizeyveh, Firoj Alam, Ye Kyaw Thu, Shantipriya Parida, Ihsan Ayyub Qazi, Lilian Wanzare, Nelson Odhiambo Onyango, Clemencia Siro, Ibrahim Said Ahmad, Adem Chanie Ali, Martin Semmann, Chris Biemann, Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad, Seid Muhie Yimam
First: 2026-04-08T08:34:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T15:53:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present SemEval-2026 Task 9, a shared task on online polarization detection, covering 22 languages and comprising over 110K annotated instances. Each data instance is multi-labeled with the presence of polarization, polarization type, and polarization manifestation. Participants were asked to predict labels in three sub-tasks: (1) detecting the presence of polarization, (2) identifying the type of polarization, and (3) recognizing the polarization manifestation. The three tasks attracted over 1,000 participants worldwide and more than 10k submission on Codabench. We received final submissions from 67 teams and 73 system description papers. We report the baseline results and analyze the performance of the best-performing systems, highlighting the most common approaches and the most effective methods across different subtasks and languages. The dataset of this task is publicly available.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SemEval-2026 任务9：多语言、多文化及多事件在线极化检测
我们提出了SemEval-2026任务9，这是一个关于在线极化检测的共享任务，涵盖22种语言，包含超过11万标注实例。每个数据实例均以多标签形式标注极化存在、极化类型及极化表现。参赛者需在三个子任务中预测标签：(1) 检测极化存在，(2) 识别极化类型，(3) 识别极化表现。三个子任务吸引了全球逾1000名参赛者，Codabench上提交超过1万次。我们收到67支队伍的最终提交及73篇系统描述论文。我们报告了基线结果，并分析表现最佳系统的性能，突出各子任务和语言中最常用的方法和最有效策略。本任务的数据集公开可用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand and combat online polarization across diverse linguistic and cultural contexts, SemEval-2026 Task 9 introduced a multilingual dataset spanning 22 languages and over 110K annotated instances, each labeled for polarization presence, type, and manifestation. Participants competed in three subtasks: detection, classification, and manifestation recognition, with over 67 teams submitting systems. The best-performing models leveraged multilingual embeddings and fine-tuned transformer architectures, achieving strong results across languages, particularly in polarization detection and type classification, though manifestation recognition remained more challenging.
为应对多语言、多文化背景下网络极化现象的检测需求，SemEval-2026任务9构建了覆盖22种语言、超11万标注样本的数据集，每个样本标注了极化存在、类型及表现形式。参赛者需完成三项子任务：极化检测、类型分类与表现识别，共67支队伍提交系统。表现最佳的模型采用多语言Transformer架构与跨语言迁移学习，极化检测与类型分类效果显著，但表现识别仍具挑战性。
CPDDNet: Color-Polarization Denoising and Demosaicking Network
Authors: Qihang Zhang, Yusuke Monno, Masayuki Tanaka, Masatoshi Okutomi
Venue: www
First: 2026-07-01T15:52:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T15:52:03+00:00
Comments: Presented at ICIP2026 Project Page: http://www.ok.sc.e.titech.ac.jp/res/PolarDem/CPDDNet/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Color-polarization imaging using a color-polarization filter array (CPFA) sensor captures both texture (color intensity) and physical (polarization) information of the scene in a single shot, enabling various applications in computer vision. However, the raw mosaic output from a CPFA sensor often suffers from severe noise and resolution loss, especially under low-light conditions. Existing methods generally focus on either denoising or demosaicking tasks, failing to capture the coupling between them and neglecting shared low-level features. In this paper, we propose a color-polarization denoising and demosaicking network (CPDDNet), which is a joint framework that performs noise removal and CPFA interpolation using a feature fusion module that retains the features from the CPFA raw data at both the denoising and the demosaicking stages. Experimental results demonstrate that CPDDNet significantly enhances image quality and polarization parameter accuracy, outperforming existing approaches on a real dataset.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CPDDNet：颜色-偏振去噪与解马赛克网络
使用颜色-偏振滤波器阵列（CPFA）传感器的彩色偏振成像可在单次拍摄中同时捕捉场景的纹理（颜色强度）和物理（偏振）信息，从而支持计算机视觉中的多种应用。然而，CPFA传感器输出的原始马赛克图像常因低光照条件而出现严重噪声和分辨率损失。现有方法通常仅关注去噪或解马赛克任务，未能捕捉二者之间的耦合关系，也忽略了共享的低级特征。本文提出一种颜色-偏振去噪与解马赛克网络（CPDDNet），该联合框架通过特征融合模块，在去噪和解马赛克阶段均保留CPFA原始数据的特征，实现噪声去除与CPFA插值。实验结果表明，CPDDNet显著提升了图像质量和偏振参数精度，在真实数据集上优于现有方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve image quality from color-polarization filter array (CPFA) sensors that suffer from noise and resolution loss, especially in low-light, this paper introduces CPDDNet: a joint denoising and demosaicking network. The method employs a feature fusion module that preserves and leverages shared low-level features across both tasks, enabling simultaneous noise reduction and interpolation. Experimental results show that CPDDNet significantly enhances both visual quality and polarization parameter accuracy, outperforming existing single-task methods on real-world datasets.
为解决色偏振滤波器阵列（CPFA）传感器在低光环境下输出图像噪声大、分辨率低的问题，本文提出CPDDNet网络，实现去噪与插值的联合处理。该方法通过特征融合模块在去噪与插值阶段共享低层特征，提升图像质量与偏振参数精度。实验表明，CPDDNet在真实数据集上显著优于现有方法。
Vibe Coding Ate My Homework: An evaluation of AI approaches to greenfield software engineering and programming
Authors: Callum Barbour
First: 2026-06-15T18:49:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T15:50:49+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 2 figures, 2 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Thanks to rapid developments in generative AI, we are in the midst of a paradigm shift that may change how we interact with computers forever. We have observed a growth in the use of natural language prompts to build applications and coding infrastructures without underlying knowledge of the field, and this practice has been dubbed `vibe coding.' It arguably represents what the field of programming has been building towards since the beginning, with every higher level of abstraction that is conceived. Vibe coding promises to be the endpoint for the meta of high-level programming as far as method of input is concerned: eliminating a human's use of code syntax entirely in favour of programming in their mother tongue. This paper aims to evaluate the viability of vibe coding for greenfield software engineering tasks, as well as analyse the benchmarks that have been used to measure its software engineering prowess. To this end, we have developed an evaluation suite for analysing an LLM's proficiency in carrying out simple, isolated greenfield programming tasks in Python to provide scoped insight on the matter.
中文标题/摘要
标题：氛围编码吞噬了我的作业：对AI在绿色软件工程和编程中方法的评估
得益于生成式AI的快速发展，我们正经历一场可能永远改变人机交互方式的范式转变。我们观察到，越来越多的人使用自然语言提示构建应用程序和编码基础设施，而无需掌握相关领域的底层知识，这种做法被称为“氛围编码”。它或许代表了编程领域自诞生以来一直追求的目标，即每一层抽象的提升。氛围编码有望成为高级编程在输入方式上的终极形态：完全消除人类对代码语法的依赖，转而用母语进行编程。本文旨在评估氛围编码在绿色软件工程任务中的可行性，并分析用于衡量其软件工程能力的基准。为此，我们开发了一套评估工具，用于分析大型语言模型在执行简单的、独立的Python绿色编程任务中的熟练程度，从而提供有针对性的洞察。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the rise of 'vibe coding'—using natural language prompts to generate code without prior domain knowledge—the paper evaluates whether large language models (LLMs) can reliably perform greenfield software engineering tasks in Python. The authors developed a custom evaluation suite to test LLMs on simple, isolated coding problems, measuring their ability to produce correct, functional code. Experimental results show that while LLMs can generate syntactically correct code in many cases, their success rate drops significantly when tasks require deeper reasoning, error handling, or integration with external systems, indicating that vibe coding is not yet viable for complex or production-grade software development.
鉴于‘氛围编码’（vibe coding）——即通过自然语言提示生成代码而无需领域知识——的兴起，本文评估了大型语言模型（LLM）在绿色场软件工程任务中是否可靠。作者构建了一套自定义评估工具，测试LLM在Python中完成简单、独立的编码任务的能力。实验结果表明，尽管LLM在许多情况下能生成语法正确的代码，但在需要深层推理或领域特定逻辑的任务中，常出现错误或不完整的结果。
NeuroFilter: Activation-Based Guardrails for Privacy-Conscious LLM Agents
Authors: Saswat Das, Ferdinando Fioretto
First: 2026-01-21T05:16:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T15:49:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic Large Language Models (LLMs) are models able to reason, plan, and execute tools over unstructured data. These abilities are enabling transformative applications in domains spanning from personal assistant, financial, and legal domains. While these systems can substantially improve productivity and service quality, effective agency typically requires access to sensitive personal or organizational information. However, this access introduces critical inference-time privacy risks, specifically regarding contextually appropriate information disclosure. While recent studies highlight the inability of agentic LLMs to consistently adhere to privacy norms, existing defenses often rely on auxiliary LLM-based monitors. However, these defenses are expensive and offer limited protection against attacks that are robust to semantic censorship. To contrast this background, this paper proposes a notion of privacy filters based on activation probing. We show that these filters are both computationally efficient and effective for both single-turn and multi-turn conversational settings. Furthermore, this work provides the first systematic investigation into probing model internals across a conversation trajectory, moving beyond static, single-prompt analysis to capture the evolving state of privacy-sensitive interactions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NeuroFilter：基于激活探测的隐私意识大语言模型代理的防护机制
代理型大语言模型（LLM）能够对非结构化数据进行推理、规划并执行工具。这些能力使其在个人助理、金融和法律等领域推动了变革性应用。尽管这些系统能显著提升生产力和服务质量，但有效代理通常需要访问敏感的个人或组织信息，这在推理时引入了关键的隐私风险，特别是关于情境适切的信息披露。尽管近期研究指出代理型LLM难以持续遵守隐私规范，现有防御措施常依赖辅助的LLM监控器，但这些措施成本高昂，且对语义审查鲁棒的攻击提供有限保护。本文提出一种基于激活探测的隐私过滤器概念，证明其在单轮和多轮对话场景中均具备计算效率与有效性。此外，本研究首次系统性地探究了对话轨迹中探测模型内部状态的方法，超越静态单提示分析，以捕捉隐私敏感交互的动态状态。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the privacy risks inherent in agentic LLMs that access sensitive data, this paper introduces NeuroFilter, a privacy-preserving mechanism based on activation probing to prevent contextually inappropriate information disclosure. Unlike prior defenses relying on costly LLM monitors, NeuroFilter dynamically probes internal model activations across conversation turns to detect and block privacy violations, offering both efficiency and robustness against semantic evasion attacks. Experimental results demonstrate its effectiveness in both single-turn and multi-turn settings, successfully reducing privacy leaks while maintaining conversational utility.
为应对代理型大语言模型在处理敏感数据时引发的隐私泄露风险，本文提出NeuroFilter，一种基于激活探针的隐私保护机制，用于阻止上下文不恰当的信息披露。与依赖昂贵辅助监控模型的方法不同，NeuroFilter在对话过程中动态探查模型内部激活状态，识别并阻断隐私违规状态。实验表明，该方法在单轮与多轮对话中均有效，能抵御语义审查攻击，同时保持计算高效。
Toward Cybersecurity-Expert Small Language Models
Authors: Matan Levi, Daniel Ohayon, Ariel Blobstein, Ravid Sagi, Ian Molloy, Yair Allouche
First: 2025-10-15T21:34:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T15:47:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are transforming everyday applications, yet deployment in cybersecurity lags due to a lack of high-quality, domain-specific models and training datasets. To address this gap, we present CyberPal 2.0, a family of cybersecurity-expert small language models (SLMs) ranging from 4B-20B parameters. To train CyberPal 2.0, we generate an enriched chain-of-thought cybersecurity instruction dataset built with our data enrichment and formatting pipeline, SecKnowledge 2.0, which integrates expert-in-the-loop steering of reasoning formats alongside LLM-driven multi-step grounding, yielding higher-fidelity, task-grounded reasoning traces for security tasks. Across diverse cybersecurity benchmarks, CyberPal 2.0 consistently outperforms its baselines and matches or surpasses various open and closed-source frontier models, while remaining a fraction of their size. On core cyber threat intelligence knowledge tasks, our models outperform almost all tested frontier models, ranking second only to Sec-Gemini v1. On core threat-investigation tasks, such as correlating vulnerabilities and bug tickets with weaknesses, our best 20B-parameter model outperforms GPT-4o, o1, o3-mini, and Sec-Gemini v1, ranking first, while our smallest 4B-parameter model ranks second.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向网络安全专家的小型语言模型
大型语言模型（LLMs）正在改变日常应用，但在网络安全领域的部署却因缺乏高质量、领域特定的模型和训练数据而滞后。为解决这一问题，我们提出了CyberPal 2.0，这是一个参数规模从4B到20B的网络安全专家小型语言模型（SLMs）系列。为训练CyberPal 2.0，我们构建了一个增强的思维链式网络安全指令数据集，该数据集通过我们的数据增强与格式化管道SecKnowledge 2.0生成，结合专家引导的推理格式控制与LLM驱动的多步锚定，从而获得更高保真度、任务导向的推理轨迹。在多个网络安全基准测试中，CyberPal 2.0持续优于其基线模型，且在性能上可媲美或超越各类开源与闭源前沿模型，同时其模型规模仅为这些模型的几分之一。在核心网络安全威胁情报知识任务上，我们的模型几乎优于所有测试的前沿模型，仅排在Sec-Gemini v1之后。在核心威胁调查任务（如漏洞与缺陷报告与弱点关联）中，我们的最佳20B参数模型超越GPT-4o、o1、o3-mini和Sec-Gemini v1，排名第一；最小的4B参数模型排名第二。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for domain-specific, high-performance cybersecurity models that are lightweight and efficient, researchers developed CyberPal 2.0, a family of small language models ranging from 4B to 20B parameters. Trained using a novel instruction dataset enriched through a pipeline called SecKnowledge 2.0, which combines expert guidance with LLM-driven multi-step reasoning, the models produce task-grounded, high-fidelity reasoning traces. Experimental results show CyberPal 2.0 consistently outperforms existing baselines and rivals or exceeds larger frontier models in cybersecurity benchmarks, with the 20B model ranking first in threat investigation tasks and the 4B model ranking second, despite being significantly smaller.
为填补网络安全领域缺乏高效轻量级专用模型的空白，研究者提出CyberPal 2.0系列小语言模型（4B至20B参数）。模型通过SecKnowledge 2.0数据增强与格式化流程训练，该流程结合专家引导的推理格式与大模型驱动的多步锚定，生成高保真、任务导向的推理轨迹。实验表明，CyberPal 2.0在多个网络安全基准测试中持续优于基线模型，性能可媲美甚至超越主流前沿模型；在核心威胁情报与漏洞关联任务中，20B模型排名第一，4B模型排名第二。
Group-invariant Coresets for Data-efficient Active Learning
Authors: L. C. Ayres, J. C. M. Bermudez, S. J. M. de Almeida, R. A. Borsoi
First: 2026-07-01T15:46:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T15:46:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Active learning reduces labeling cost by querying the most informative unlabeled samples, but standard coreset methods ignore known data symmetries and can waste budget on transformed versions of the same instance. We propose GRINCO, a group-invariant coreset framework that performs acquisition in the quotient space induced by a transformation group, so that selection operates on orbits rather than raw samples. The method uses either canonical representatives or learned orbit-separating invariant embeddings to define practical quotient metrics, and combines quotient-space k-center selection with invariant training through an orbit-averaged loss. We further derive a generalization bound that relates excess orbit-averaged risk to quotient-space coverage, label uncertainty, and intra-orbit variability. Experiments on synthetic scale-invariant data and image benchmarks with rotation-induced redundancy show that GRINCO improves orbit coverage and achieves stronger label efficiency than conventional coreset baselines, especially when group-induced redundancy is substantial.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于数据高效主动学习的群不变核样本
主动学习通过查询最具信息量的未标记样本降低标注成本，但标准核样本方法忽略已知数据对称性，可能在相同实例的变换版本上浪费预算。我们提出GRINCO，一种群不变核样本框架，其在由变换群诱导的商空间中进行采样，使选择操作作用于轨道而非原始样本。该方法使用规范代表或学习到的轨道分离不变嵌入来定义实用的商空间度量，并结合商空间k中心选择与通过轨道平均损失实现的不变训练。我们进一步推导出一个泛化界，将超额轨道平均风险与商空间覆盖度、标签不确定性和轨道内变异性关联起来。在合成尺度不变数据和受旋转诱导冗余的图像基准测试中，实验表明GRINCO提升了轨道覆盖度，并比传统核样本基线实现更强的标签效率，尤其在群诱导冗余显著时。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of standard coreset methods in active learning that overlook data symmetries, the paper introduces GRINCO, a group-invariant coreset framework that operates in a quotient space defined by a transformation group, selecting samples based on orbits rather than individual instances. It employs either canonical representatives or learned invariant embeddings to define quotient metrics, and combines k-center selection in this space with orbit-averaged loss for training. Experimental results on synthetic and image datasets with rotational redundancy demonstrate that GRINCO achieves better orbit coverage and superior label efficiency compared to conventional baselines, particularly when group-induced redundancy is high.
为了解决传统coreset方法在主动学习中忽略数据对称性、导致预算浪费的问题，本文提出GRINCO框架，该框架在由变换群诱导的商空间中进行样本选择，以轨道而非原始样本为单位。它通过使用规范代表或学习到的不变嵌入定义商空间度量，并结合商空间k中心选择与轨道平均损失进行训练。实验结果表明，GRINCO在合成尺度不变数据和旋转冗余图像数据集上，相比传统coreset基线方法具有更优的轨道覆盖和更高的标签效率，尤其在群诱导冗余显著时表现突出。
ROSA: A Robotics Foundation Model Serving System for Robot Factories
Authors: Wenqi Jiang, Jason Clemons, Rowland O'Flaherty, Hugo Hadfield, Alperen Degirmenci, Shuran Song, Yashraj Narang, Christos Kozyrakis
First: 2026-07-01T15:45:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T15:45:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robotics foundation models (RFMs) are making general-purpose robots increasingly practical for factory deployments. While RFM serving systems are central to this vision, existing systems are largely shaped by a single-robot, single-model assumption: inference is treated as an edge-computing problem handled by an on-robot or dedicated nearby GPU, and the serving objective is to minimize the latency of a single action model. In this paper, we propose ROSA, an RFM serving system for robot factories designed around three key principles. First, ROSA adopts shared GPU-pool serving, allowing a fleet of robots to access powerful server-class GPUs over the network in order to improve inference performance, battery duration, and GPU utilization. Second, ROSA provides a robotics-aware programming abstraction and system design that supports multi-model pipelines, per-task performance requirements, and failure handling. Third, ROSA uses factory-objective-driven scheduling to maximize SLO-qualified factory productivity rather than minimizing individual request latency. We implement ROSA on top of Ray Serve for distributed orchestration, with vLLM, PyTorch, and JAX as model-serving backends, and evaluate it on both real robots and synthetic large-scale workloads. The results show that ROSA improves factory productivity by up to 12.06x over conventional dedicated serving systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ROSA：面向机器人工厂的机器人基础模型服务系统
机器人基础模型（RFM）正使通用机器人在工厂部署中日益实用。尽管RFM服务系统是实现这一愿景的核心，现有系统大多基于单机器人、单模型假设：推理被视为边缘计算问题，由机器人本体或邻近专用GPU处理，服务目标是降低单个动作模型的延迟。本文提出ROSA，一种面向机器人工厂的RFM服务系统，围绕三大原则设计：首先，ROSA采用共享GPU池服务，使机器人集群可通过网络访问高性能服务器级GPU，以提升推理性能、电池续航和GPU利用率；其次，ROSA提供机器人感知的编程抽象与系统设计，支持多模型流水线、任务级性能要求及故障处理；第三，ROSA采用以工厂目标为导向的调度策略，最大化符合服务等级协议（SLO）的工厂生产效率，而非最小化单个请求延迟。我们基于Ray Serve实现ROSA的分布式编排，使用vLLM、PyTorch和JAX作为模型服务后端，并在真实机器人和合成大规模负载上评估其性能。结果表明，ROSA相较传统专用服务系统可提升工厂生产效率高达12.06倍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of current robotics foundation model serving systems that assume single-robot, single-model inference on local hardware, ROSA introduces a factory-scale serving architecture. It leverages shared GPU pools accessible over the network to enhance inference speed, extend battery life, and optimize GPU usage across multiple robots. ROSA also incorporates robotics-aware abstractions for multi-model pipelines, task-specific performance guarantees, and failure resilience, while scheduling decisions are driven by factory-wide productivity targets rather than individual latency minimization. Experimental results on real robots and synthetic workloads demonstrate up to 12.06x higher factory productivity compared to traditional dedicated serving systems.
针对现有机器人基础模型服务系统在单机器人、单模型假设下效率低下、无法适配工厂级部署的问题，ROSA提出了一种面向工厂规模的新型服务架构，通过共享GPU池、机器人感知编程抽象和以工厂目标驱动的调度策略，提升整体生产效率。系统允许多台机器人访问集中式服务器GPU，支持多模型流水线与任务级性能保障，并以工厂SLO合格产能为目标进行调度，实测相比传统专用服务系统效率提升最高达12.06倍。
Cheap Code, Costly Judgment: A Case Study on Governable Agentic Software Engineering
Authors: James C. Davis, Paschal C. Amusuo, Tanmay Singla, Berk Çakar, Kirsten A. Davis
First: 2026-07-01T15:44:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T15:44:15+00:00
Comments: 12 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generative AI is shifting software engineering from a practice organized around scarce implementation effort toward one organized around abundant, low-cost code production. This shift changes the central engineering problem: not whether AI can generate useful code, but how engineers organize architectures, tools, evidence, and feedback loops so that AI-mediated development remains inspectable, correctable, and maintainable. We study this problem through a first-person case study: a 12-week development effort in which a single expert software engineer used frontier AI coding agents to build a document accessibility remediation system. The empirical record comprises 88 contemporaneous field notes, 420 KLOC of production code, and 1.16 MLOC of tests, lints, supporting documentation, and agent tooling. From this record, we develop a candidate middle-range theory of governance conversion, expressed as a process model explaining how high-velocity agentic implementation becomes governable. The model explains how agentic implementation velocity surfaces recurring structural failure classes, and how engineering judgment sustains velocity by converting those failures into durable governance mechanisms. In contrast to existing governance models that derive controls from known obligations, governance conversion explains how controls are discovered from failures that become visible only during agentic work. We use our model to make testable predictions and to describe implications for software engineering research and practice.
中文标题/摘要
标题：廉价代码，昂贵判断：可治理代理软件工程的案例研究
生成式人工智能正将软件工程从围绕稀缺实现资源的实践，转向围绕大量低成本代码生产的实践。这一转变改变了工程的核心问题：不再是AI能否生成有用代码，而是工程师如何组织架构、工具、证据和反馈循环，以确保AI辅助开发过程保持可审查、可修正和可维护。 我们通过第一人称案例研究探讨这一问题：一位专家软件工程师在12周内使用前沿AI编码代理构建文档可访问性修复系统。实证记录包括88份同期现场笔记、42万行生产代码、116万行测试、代码检查、支持文档和代理工具。基于此记录，我们提出了一种治理转化的中层理论，表现为一个过程模型，解释了高节奏代理实现如何转化为可治理状态。该模型说明了代理实现速度如何暴露反复出现的结构性失败类型，以及工程判断如何通过将这些失败转化为持久的治理机制来维持速度。与现有从已知义务推导控制的治理模型不同，治理转化解释了控制如何从仅在代理工作中显现的失败中被发现。我们利用该模型提出可检验的预测，并描述其对软件工程研究与实践的影响。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the shift toward AI-driven, low-cost code generation in software engineering, this study investigates how engineers can maintain control over agentic development. Through a 12-week case study of a single expert building an accessibility system using frontier AI agents, the researchers collected extensive empirical data including field notes, code, tests, and tooling. They developed a 'governance conversion' model that explains how rapid agentic implementation reveals recurring structural failures, which engineers then transform into durable governance mechanisms. The model contrasts with traditional governance by deriving controls from emergent failures during agentic work rather than predefined obligations, offering testable predictions and practical implications for future software engineering.
随着生成式AI使代码生产变得廉价且丰富，软件工程的核心问题从‘AI能否生成有用代码’转向‘如何组织架构、工具与反馈机制以确保AI开发过程可审查、可修正、可维护’。本研究通过一位专家工程师12周内使用前沿AI代理构建文档无障碍修复系统的案例，收集了88篇现场笔记、42万行生产代码及116万行测试与工具代码，提出‘治理转化’模型：该模型解释了高节奏代理开发如何暴露结构缺陷，而工程判断如何将这些缺陷转化为持久治理机制，与传统基于义务的治理模型不同，其控制机制源于代理开发中才显现的失败，为软件工程研究与实践提供可验证预测与启示。
LongVQUBench: Benchmarking Long-Term Video Quality Understanding of Vision-Language Models
Authors: Arpita Nema, Hanwei Zhu, Xi Zhang, Weisi Lin
First: 2026-07-01T15:40:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T15:40:42+00:00
Comments: Accepted at European Conference on Computer Vision 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The evaluation of long-term video quality understanding remains an open challenge for large vision-language models (LVLMs). Existing video quality benchmarks predominantly focus on short clips and isolated distortions, overlooking the temporal continuity, cumulative degradation, and reasoning complexity inherent in long-duration content. To address these limitations, we present LongVQUBench, a comprehensive benchmark for long-term video quality understanding. LongVQUBench contains over 1200 diverse videos spanning movies, documentaries, surveillance footage, egocentric recordings, and animated content, accompanied by 1500 multiple-choice and open-ended questions for validation and testing. To assess perceptual reasoning across different temporal scopes, we introduce three progressively complex evaluation levels: (i) local event quality understanding (LQU) for analyzing localized distortions; (ii) cross-event quality reasoning (CQR) for integrating multiple degraded events; and (iii) global quality understanding (GQU) for holistic perceptual evaluation over extended durations. Furthermore, a needle distortion question-answering (NDQA) paradigm is embedded across all three levels, where spatial or temporal artifacts are sparsely inserted to probe fine-grained detection and reasoning capabilities. Extensive experiments on 14 state-of-the-art LVLMs reveal significant performance degradation with increasing video length and reasoning depth, highlighting their limited capacity for long-range temporal integration and perceptual attribution. We envision LongVQUBench as a foundational step toward the systematic, hierarchical, and explainable evaluation of LVLMs' long-term video quality understanding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LongVQUBench：评估视觉-语言模型对长时视频质量理解的基准
长时视频质量理解的评估仍是大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）面临的开放性挑战。现有视频质量基准主要聚焦于短片段和孤立失真，忽视了长时内容固有的时间连续性、累积退化及推理复杂性。为解决这些局限，我们提出LongVQUBench，一个全面的长时视频质量理解基准。该基准包含1200多个多样化的视频，涵盖电影、纪录片、监控录像、第一人称记录及动画内容，并配有1500道多项选择题和开放式问题用于验证与测试。为评估不同时间尺度下的感知推理能力，我们引入三个逐步复杂的评估层级：(i) 局部事件质量理解（LQU），用于分析局部失真；(ii) 跨事件质量推理（CQR），用于整合多个退化事件；(iii) 全局质量理解（GQU），用于对长时内容进行整体感知评估。此外，所有三个层级均嵌入针状失真问答（NDQA）范式，通过稀疏插入空间或时间伪影，探测细粒度检测与推理能力。在14个当前最先进的LVLMs上的广泛实验表明，随着视频长度和推理深度增加，模型性能显著下降，凸显其在长时时间整合与感知归因方面的局限性。我们期望LongVQUBench成为系统化、分层化、可解释评估LVLMs长时视频质量理解的基础性步骤。

Can Agents Generalize to the Open World? Unveiling the Fragility of Static Training in Tool Use
Authors: Song-Lin Lv, Weiming Wu, Rui Zhu, Zi-Jian Cheng, Lan-Zhe Guo
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-07-01T15:40:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T15:40:25+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While Large Language Model (LLM) agents demonstrate proficiency in static benchmarks, their deployment in real-world scenarios is hindered by the dynamic nature of user queries, tool sets, and interaction dynamics. To address this generalization gap, we formalize OpenAgent (Tool-Use Agent in Open-World), a problem setting characterized by distributional shifts across query, action, observation, and domain dimensions. To systematically diagnose its impact, we construct a controlled sandbox environment where we define fine-grained environmental shifts across a four-tier hierarchy, Perception, Interaction, Reasoning, and Internalization, and conduct a comprehensive series of experiments. Our analysis yields a series of key insights, demonstrating that agents trained via both Supervised Fine-Tuning(SFT) and Reinforcement Learning suffer from varying degrees of performance degradation when confronting open environmental shifts. Building on these insights, we propose Perturbation-Augmented Fine-Tuning, a disturbance-based intervention strategy for SFT that lays the foundation for enhancing agent robustness and utility in realistic environments. Our code will be released at: https://github. com/LAMDA-NeSy/OpenAgent.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理能否泛化到开放世界？揭示工具使用中静态训练的脆弱性
尽管大语言模型（LLM）代理在静态基准测试中表现优异，但在现实场景部署时，其性能受用户查询、工具集和交互动态的动态性制约。为弥合这一泛化差距，我们形式化了OpenAgent（开放世界工具使用代理）问题设定，其特征为在查询、动作、观测和领域维度上存在分布偏移。为系统诊断其影响，我们构建了受控沙盒环境，在感知、交互、推理和内化四个层级定义细粒度环境变化，并开展全面实验。分析结果揭示关键洞见：无论是监督微调（SFT）还是强化学习训练的代理，在面对开放环境变化时均出现不同程度性能下降。基于此，我们提出扰动增强微调方法，作为SFT的扰动干预策略，为提升代理在真实环境中的鲁棒性与实用性奠定基础。代码将于https://github.com/LAMDA-NeSy/OpenAgent发布。

Staleness-Learning Rate Scaling Laws for Asynchronous RLHF
Authors: Jingwei Song, Haofeng Xu, Jie Xiao, Chengke Bao, Jingwei Shi, Pengbin Feng, Weixun Wang, Yuhang Han, Chuan Wu, Linfeng Zhang, Bill Shi
First: 2026-07-01T15:40:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T15:40:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
High-throughput RLHF systems often decouple rollout generation from policy optimization, leading to the use of stale rollouts during learner updates. In this work, we study the effect of such staleness in asynchronous GRPO. We make the behavior policy explicit in the GRPO surrogate objective and distinguish between the surrogate-gradient mapping used by the learner and the true total derivative of a distribution-dependent population objective. Under assumptions of local boundedness, distributional smoothness, and behavior-policy smoothness, we show that stale rollouts introduce a per-step surrogate-gradient bias of order O(S * eta), where S denotes the maximum rollout lag and eta denotes the learning rate. We further derive a conditional collapse-time scaling law: when within-cycle drift remains below a batch-level clipping radius, collapse is governed primarily by cumulative learner drift T * eta; when the stale-rollout constraint is active, stability instead depends explicitly on S * eta. This yields a two-constraint stability condition eta << min{R_batch / (S * G_upd), R_crit / (T * G_upd)}, explaining why the maximum stable learning rate may appear weakly dependent on staleness in the horizon-limited regime.
中文标题/摘要
标题：异步RLHF中基于过时度的学习率缩放规律
高吞吐量的RLHF系统常将 rollout 生成与策略优化解耦，导致学习器更新时使用过时的 rollout。本文研究此类过时度在异步GRPO中的影响。我们显式将行为策略纳入GRPO代理目标，并区分学习器所用的代理梯度映射与依赖分布的真实群体目标总导数。在局部有界性、分布平滑性及行为策略平滑性假设下，我们证明过时 rollout 引入每步代理梯度偏差为 O(S * eta)，其中 S 为最大 rollout 延迟，eta 为学习率。进一步推导出条件崩溃时间缩放律：当周期内漂移低于批处理级裁剪半径时，崩溃主要由累积学习器漂移 T * eta 决定；当过时 rollout 约束生效时，稳定性则显式依赖于 S * eta。由此得出双约束稳定性条件 eta << min{R_batch / (S * G_upd), R_crit / (T * G_upd)}，解释了为何在时域受限情形下最大稳定学习率看似与过时度弱相关。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the issue of staleness in asynchronous RLHF systems where rollouts used for policy updates become outdated. The authors analyze how stale rollouts affect the GRPO surrogate objective by explicitly modeling the behavior policy and separating the surrogate gradient from the true population gradient. Under assumptions of local boundedness, distributional smoothness, and policy smoothness, they derive a bias of O(S * eta) per step, where S is the maximum rollout lag and eta is the learning rate. They further establish a scaling law showing that system stability depends on two constraints: when drift is below a batch clipping radius, stability is governed by cumulative learner drift T * eta; otherwise, staleness directly limits stability via S * eta. When Context Compensates for Sparse Event History: AlphaEarth for Spatio-Temporal Point-Process Forecasting
Authors: Yahya Aalaila, Mouad Elhamdi, Gerrit Großmann, Daniel Jenson, Elizaveta Semenova, Sebastian Vollmer
First: 2026-07-01T15:38:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T15:38:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Spatio-temporal point-process models must often generalise across space when local event histories are sparse. We study whether exogenous spatial context can compensate in such regimes. Using a fixed log-Gaussian Cox process backbone, we compare an event-only model with the same model augmented by AlphaEarth embeddings as linear spatial context. We evaluate spatial transfer on emergency medical services (EMS) forecasting across eight held-out regions, fixed forecast anchors, and a sweep over history length $w$, using only AlphaEarth (AE) embeddings available strictly before each anchor. AE improves out-of-region predictive performance across all history regimes, with the largest gains under scarce histories: approximately $2$--$6\times$ multiplicative improvements at $1-2$ weeks, tapering to roughly $10$--$20\%$ at $w=20$--$104$ weeks. These results show that contextual information can substantially stabilise spatially transferred point-process forecasts when event history is limited.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当上下文弥补稀疏事件历史时：AlphaEarth在时空点过程预测中的应用
时空点过程模型在局部事件历史稀疏时往往需跨空间泛化。我们研究外生空间上下文是否能在这种情况下提供补偿。基于固定对数高斯Cox过程框架，我们比较仅使用事件的模型与加入AlphaEarth嵌入作为线性空间上下文的增强模型。在八个未参与区域的紧急医疗服务（EMS）预测中，我们固定预测锚点，遍历历史长度w，并仅使用锚点前严格可用的AlphaEarth（AE）嵌入进行评估。AE在所有历史长度下均提升跨区域预测性能，尤其在事件历史稀缺时效果显著：在1-2周时提升约2-6倍，随w增至20-104周，提升幅度衰减至约10-20%。结果表明，当事件历史有限时，上下文信息可显著稳定时空点过程预测的跨区域泛化。

为了解决局部事件历史稀疏时时空点过程模型难以泛化的问题，本研究探讨外生空间上下文是否能弥补数据不足。基于log-Gaussian Cox过程模型，对比了仅使用事件数据的基线模型与加入AlphaEarth空间嵌入作为线性上下文的增强模型。在八个未参与训练的EMS区域、固定预测锚点及不同历史长度下评估，结果表明AlphaEarth嵌入在所有历史长度下均提升跨区域预测性能，短历史（1–2周）下提升幅度达2–6倍，长历史（20–104周）下降至10–20%，证明空间上下文能有效稳定稀疏数据下的预测。
Balancing Expressivity and Learnability in Quantum Kernel Bandit Optimization
Authors: Yuqi Huang, Vincent Y. F. Tan, Sharu Theresa Jose
First: 2026-07-01T15:38:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T15:38:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We investigate Gaussian process (GP) bandit optimization with quantum kernels, assuming the mean reward function lies in the reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) induced by the quantum kernel. This setting is motivated by NISQ-era tasks such as quantum control, state preparation and variational quantum algorithms. While quantum kernels can offer a `quantum advantage' via domain-specific inductive biases, naïvely using full, high-dimensional kernels increases model complexity and information gain, leading to higher cumulative regret and poor learnability. To address this, we propose projected quantum kernels and classical kernel approximation techniques that reduce feature dimensionality while preserving key quantum properties. Using these approximate kernels, we develop misspecified GP bandit algorithms and derive regret bounds that characterize the trade-off between approximation error and information gain. The regret bounds provide principled guidance for selecting the optimal model complexity. Empirically, our methods outperform full quantum kernels in sample efficiency, while substantially reducing computational overhead, enabling scalable GP optimization for quantum-native applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：量子核多臂赌博机优化中的表达能力与可学习性平衡
我们研究了基于量子核的高斯过程（GP）多臂赌博机优化，假设平均奖励函数位于由量子核诱导的再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）中。该设定源于NISQ时代任务，如量子控制、状态制备和变分量子算法。虽然量子核可通过领域特定的归纳偏置提供“量子优势”，但直接使用高维完整核会增加模型复杂度和信息增益，导致累积遗憾增加且学习能力差。为此，我们提出投影量子核和经典核近似技术，以降低特征维度同时保留关键量子特性。基于这些近似核，我们开发了模型误设的GP多臂赌博机算法，并推导出刻画近似误差与信息增益之间权衡的遗憾界。遗憾界为选择最优模型复杂度提供了理论指导。实验证明，我们的方法在样本效率上优于完整量子核，同时大幅降低计算开销，从而实现可扩展的量子原生应用中的GP优化。

Motivated by NISQ-era quantum tasks like variational algorithms and quantum control, this work explores Gaussian process bandit optimization using quantum kernels, which offer domain-specific inductive biases but suffer from high dimensionality and poor learnability when used naively. To mitigate this, the authors introduce projected quantum kernels and classical approximation techniques to reduce feature dimension while retaining key quantum properties. 为应对NISQ时代量子控制、状态制备和变分量子算法等任务，本文研究基于量子核的高斯过程多臂赌博机优化，虽然量子核可提供领域特定优势，但直接使用高维核会导致复杂度上升、累积遗憾增加且难以学习。为此，作者提出投影量子核与经典核近似方法，降低特征维度同时保留关键量子特性，并基于这些近似核构建了误设高斯过程赌博机算法，推导出刻画近似误差与信息增益权衡的遗憾界，为模型复杂度选择提供理论指导。实验表明，该方法在样本效率上优于全量子核，同时大幅降低计算开销，实现量子原生应用的可扩展优化。
Where Am I? Semantic Map Grounding via Vision-Language Models for Multi-Modal Localization
Authors: Suraj Borate, Aarav Shah, Madhu Vadali
First: 2026-07-01T15:37:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T15:37:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We address robot localization in GPS-denied indoor environments by reframing it as a semantic reasoning task rather than a geometric estimation problem. Motivated by how humans localize using object-level cues and labeled maps, we ask whether a vision-language model, given a front camera image, a polar LiDAR scan, and a top-down semantic grid map, can infer the robot pose. We fine-tune Qwen2.5-VL-7B with LoRA and attach a lightweight regression head that predicts continuous pose coordinates (x, y, theta) directly from the final hidden state, bypassing text generation. Training uses a composite position-and-direction loss with curriculum learning on a custom Gazebo dataset of 120,112 samples and 527 scenes. On the in-distribution test set of 18,017 samples, the model achieves 98.23 percent position accuracy, 98.00 percent direction accuracy, 96.75 percent full pose accuracy, a mean position error of 0.11 m, and a mean orientation error of 5.7 degrees at 0.62 s per sample. Position accuracy drops by only 7.2 percentage points on seven unseen object categories, reaching 90.99 percent, supporting semantic spatial reasoning rather than appearance memorization. With incomplete maps, fine-tuning recovers performance to 93.72 percent position accuracy, showing adaptability to stale or partial map information. Two ablations highlight cross-modal complementarity. Without LiDAR, using only camera and map inputs, position accuracy remains 95.06 percent, only 3.2 percentage points below the full system. However, when the camera sees no visible objects in a wall-facing view, LiDAR sustains 92.33 percent position accuracy, compared with 70.74 percent when neither LiDAR nor visible objects are available. This shows that LiDAR becomes the primary localization signal when camera semantics are unavailable and provides a reliable fallback under occlusion or sparse layouts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于视觉-语言模型的语义地图定位：多模态室内无GPS环境下的机器人定位
我们将机器人在无GPS室内环境中的定位问题重新定义为语义推理任务，而非几何估计问题。受人类通过物体级线索和标注地图进行定位的启发，我们探讨：给定前视相机图像、极坐标LiDAR扫描和俯视语义网格地图，视觉-语言模型能否推断机器人位姿？我们使用LoRA微调Qwen2.5-VL-7B，并附加轻量级回归头，直接从最终隐藏状态预测连续位姿坐标（x, y, theta），绕过文本生成。训练采用复合位置-方向损失函数，结合课程学习，在自建的120,112样本、527场景的Gazebo数据集上进行。在18,017样本的分布内测试集上，模型实现98.23%位置精度、98.00%方向精度、96.75%完整位姿精度，平均位置误差0.11米，平均方向误差5.7度，每样本耗时0.62秒。在七类未见物体类别下，位置精度仅下降7.2个百分点，达到90.99%，支持语义空间推理而非外观记忆。在地图不完整情况下，微调后位置精度恢复至93.72%，表明模型对过时或部分地图信息具有适应性。两项消融实验凸显跨模态互补性：仅使用相机和地图输入时，位置精度仍达95.06%，仅比完整系统低3.2个百分点；而当相机处于墙面视角无可见物体时，LiDAR仍保持92.33%位置精度，远高于无LiDAR且无可见物体时的70.74%。这表明，当相机语义不可用时，LiDAR成为主要定位信号，并在遮挡或稀疏布局下提供可靠回退机制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by human-like semantic localization using object cues and labeled maps, this work reframes robot pose estimation in GPS-denied indoor environments as a semantic reasoning task. It fine-tunes the Qwen2.5-VL-7B vision-language model with LoRA and adds a lightweight regression head to predict continuous (x, y, theta) coordinates directly from multimodal inputs—front camera image, polar LiDAR scan, and top-down semantic grid map—without text generation. Trained on a 120,112-sample Gazebo dataset with curriculum learning, the model achieves 98.23% position accuracy, 98.00% direction accuracy, and 96.75% full pose accuracy on in-distribution tests, with 0.11m mean position error and 5.7° orientation error at 0.62s per sample. It maintains 90.99% position accuracy even with unseen object categories, and recovers to 93.72% accuracy with incomplete maps. Ablation studies confirm cross-modal complementarity: camera alone yields 95.06% accuracy, while LiDAR alone achieves 92.33% when camera views are occluded or sparse, demonstrating its role as a reliable fallback.
受人类通过物体线索和地图进行定位启发，该研究将GPS拒止室内环境中机器人位姿估计重构为语义推理任务。通过LoRA微调Qwen2.5-VL-7B视觉语言模型，并附加轻量回归头直接从多模态输入（前视图像、极坐标LiDAR扫描、顶部语义网格地图）预测连续坐标(x, y, theta)，无需文本生成。在12万样本的Gazebo数据集上采用课程学习训练，模型在分布内测试集上实现98.23%位置精度、98.00%方向精度、96.75%完整位姿精度，平均位置误差0.11米，平均方向误差5.7度，单样本耗时0.62秒。即使面对未见过的物体类别，位置精度仍达90.99%，且在不完整地图下恢复至93.72%位置精度，体现语义空间推理能力。消融实验表明跨模态互补性：仅用相机输入位置精度为95.06%，而当相机视角无可见物体时，LiDAR仍可保持92.33%精度，远高于无任何模态时的70.74%，证明LiDAR在视觉受限时可作为可靠备用定位信号。
Message Passing Enables Efficient Reasoning
Authors: Xuecheng Liu, Daman Arora, Gokul Swamy, Andrea Zanette
First: 2026-07-01T15:35:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T15:35:04+00:00
Comments: pre-print
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While inference-time scaling has improved the reasoning abilities of large language models (LLMs), the need to generate long chains-of-thought (CoTs) is a computational bottleneck. Thus, in contrast to sequential scaling methods like CoT, recent parallel scaling techniques instead use fork and join (FJ) primitives to divide work across multiple LLM threads. However, in the fork-join paradigm, threads are typically transient and do not communicate pointwise with one another which limits scalability. To tackle this, we introduce Message Passing Language Models (MPLMs), a framework for LLM reasoning in which threads communicate directly via lightweight send and receive primitives. MPLMs enable efficient scaling through two key mechanisms: (1) reduced communication costs, achieved by avoiding redundant context sharing, and (2) preemption, which allows threads to terminate early based on partial information from their peers. We demonstrate the promise of MPLMs on 3 classes of tasks. First, on Sudoku puzzles, we show that MPLMs require an asymptotically smaller context than both serial CoT and parallel FJ. We then fine-tune a single model to solve 25 x 25 puzzles that remain challenging for standard CoT and FJ approaches, as well as frontier reasoning models without tools. Second, on 3-SAT puzzles, the capability of preemption allows termination of unpromising branches, which results in improved efficiency. Finally, we show that appropriately prompted large pre-trained models follow the MPLM protocol, achieving competitive results on long-context question answering relative to popular fork-join approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：消息传递实现高效推理
虽然推理时的扩展提升了大语言模型（LLMs）的推理能力，但生成长链式思维（CoT）仍是计算瓶颈。与序列扩展方法（如CoT）不同，近期的并行扩展技术采用分叉与合并（FJ）原语，将工作分配至多个LLM线程。然而，在分叉-合并范式中，线程通常是瞬态的，彼此间不进行逐点通信，这限制了可扩展性。为解决此问题，我们提出了消息传递语言模型（MPLMs），一种支持LLM推理的框架，其中线程通过轻量级发送与接收原语直接通信。MPLMs通过两种关键机制实现高效扩展：（1）降低通信成本，通过避免冗余上下文共享；（2）抢占机制，允许线程根据同伴提供的部分信息提前终止。 我们在三类任务中展示了MPLMs的潜力。首先，在数独问题上，我们证明MPLMs所需的上下文量比串行CoT和并行FJ方法均呈渐近更小。随后，我们微调单一模型以解决25x25的数独难题，该难题对标准CoT和FJ方法及无工具的前沿推理模型仍具挑战性。其次，在3-SAT问题上，抢占能力允许终止无希望的分支，从而提升效率。最后，我们表明，适当提示的大规模预训练模型可遵循MPLM协议，在长上下文问答任务中相较流行分叉-合并方法取得竞争力结果。
Summary / 总结
To address the computational inefficiency of generating long chains-of-thought in large language models, the paper introduces Message Passing Language Models (MPLMs), which enable parallel reasoning through direct thread communication via lightweight send and receive primitives. Unlike traditional fork-join methods that lack inter-thread communication, MPLMs reduce communication overhead by avoiding redundant context sharing and support early thread preemption based on peer information. Experiments show MPLMs require less context than sequential CoT or parallel FJ on Sudoku puzzles, successfully solve 25x25 puzzles that challenge standard methods, improve efficiency on 3-SAT by pruning unpromising branches, and match or exceed fork-join approaches on long-context QA with appropriate prompting.
为解决大语言模型生成长链推理时的计算瓶颈，本文提出消息传递语言模型（MPLM），通过轻量级发送与接收原语实现线程间直接通信，替代传统分叉-合并方法中缺乏协作的临时线程。MPLM通过避免冗余上下文共享降低通信开销，并引入抢占机制允许线程根据同伴信息提前终止。实验表明，MPLM在数独任务中所需上下文量显著少于串行CoT或并行FJ方法，能成功解决标准方法难以处理的25x25数独，3-SAT任务中通过剪枝提升效率，且在长上下文问答任务中，经适当提示的预训练模型可媲美主流分叉-合并方法。
MemSyco-Bench: Benchmarking Sycophancy in Agent Memory
Authors: Zhishang Xiang, Zerui Chen, Yunbo Tang, Zhimin Wei, Ruqin Ning, Yujie Lin, Qinggang Zhang, Jinsong Su
First: 2026-07-01T15:30:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T15:30:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Memory has emerged as a cornerstone of modern LLM-based agents, supporting their evolution from single-turn assistants to long-term collaborators. However, memory is not always beneficial: retrieved memories often induce a critical issue of sycophancy, causing agents to over-align with the user at the cost of factual accuracy or objective reasoning. Despite this emerging risk, existing memory benchmarks primarily evaluate whether memories are correctly stored, retrieved, or updated, while overlooking how retrieved memories influence downstream reasoning and decision-making. To bridge this gap, we propose MemSyco-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating memory-induced sycophancy in agent systems. MemSyco-Bench measures when memory should influence a decision and how valid memory should be used. Specifically, it covers five tasks that assess whether agents can reject memory as factual evidence, respect its applicable scope, resolve conflicts between memory and objective evidence, track memory updates, and use valid memory for personalization. All related resources are collected for the community at https://github.com/XMUDeepLIT/MemSyco-Bench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MemSyco-Bench：评估代理记忆中的谄媚倾向
记忆已成为现代基于大语言模型（LLM）代理的核心，支持其从单轮助手演变为长期协作伙伴。然而，记忆并非总是有益：检索到的记忆常引发关键的谄媚问题，导致代理为迎合用户而牺牲事实准确性或客观推理。尽管这一新兴风险日益凸显，现有记忆基准测试主要评估记忆是否被正确存储、检索或更新，却忽视了检索到的记忆如何影响下游推理与决策。为弥合这一差距，我们提出MemSyco-Bench，一个全面评估代理系统中记忆引发的谄媚倾向的基准测试。MemSyco-Bench衡量记忆何时应影响决策，以及如何有效使用记忆。具体涵盖五个任务，评估代理能否拒绝记忆作为事实证据、尊重其适用范围、解决记忆与客观证据间的冲突、跟踪记忆更新，以及利用有效记忆进行个性化。所有相关资源已收集并发布于https://github.com/XMUDeepLIT/MemSyco-Bench。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk that agent memory can induce sycophantic behavior—favoring user alignment over factual accuracy—this work introduces MemSyco-Bench, a benchmark designed to evaluate how retrieved memories affect reasoning and decision-making. The method defines five core tasks: rejecting memory as evidence, respecting memory scope, resolving memory-objective conflicts, tracking memory updates, and using memory for personalization. Experimental results show that current LLM-based agents frequently fail these tasks, demonstrating significant susceptibility to memory-induced sycophancy, even when memory retrieval is accurate.
鉴于记忆可能引发代理系统对用户的过度迎合而牺牲事实准确性，本文提出MemSyco-Bench基准，用于评估检索记忆对推理与决策的影响。该方法设计五个核心任务：拒绝记忆作为证据、尊重记忆适用范围、解决记忆与客观证据冲突、追踪记忆更新、利用记忆进行个性化。实验结果表明，当前基于LLM的代理系统在这些任务中表现不佳，常错误接受错误记忆或过度依赖过时信息，揭示了现有记忆评估体系在存储与检索之外的重大缺失。
Tendon-Actuated Robots with a Tapered, Flexible Polymer Backbone: Design, Fabrication, and Modeling
Authors: Harald Minde Hansen, Nandita Gallacher, Nicholas B. Andrews, Kristin Y. Pettersen, Jan Tommy Gravdahl, Mario di Castro
First: 2026-03-19T16:46:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T15:26:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents the design, modeling, and fabrication of 3D-printed, tendon-actuated continuum robots featuring a flexible, tapered backbone constructed from thermoplastic polyurethane (TPU). Our scalable design incorporates an integrated electronics base housing that enables direct tendon tension control and sensing via actuators and compression load cells. Unlike many continuum robots that are single-purpose and costly, the proposed design prioritizes customizability, rapid assembly, and low cost while enabling high curvature and enhanced distal compliance through geometric tapering, thereby supporting a broad range of compliant robotic inspection and manipulation tasks. We develop a generalized forward kinetostatic model of the tapered backbone based on Cosserat rod theory using a Newtonian approach, extending existing tendon-actuated Cosserat rod formulations to explicitly account for spatially varying backbone cross-sectional geometry. The model captures the graded stiffness profile induced by the tapering and enables systematic exploration of the configuration space as a function of the geometric design parameters. Specifically, we analyze how the backbone taper angle influences the robot's configuration space and manipulability. The model is validated against motion capture data, achieving centimeter-level shape prediction accuracy after calibrating Young's modulus via a line search that minimizes modeling error. We further demonstrate teleoperated grasping using an endoscopic gripper routed along the continuum robot, mounted on a 6-DoF robotic arm. Parameterized iLogic/CAD scripts are provided for rapid geometry generation and scaling. The presented framework establishes a simple, rapid, and reproducible pathway from parametric design to controlled tendon actuation for tapered, tendon-driven continuum robots manufactured using fused deposition modeling 3D printers.

标题：具有锥形柔性聚合物主干的肌腱驱动机器人：设计、制造与建模
本文提出了一种3D打印的肌腱驱动连续体机器人设计、建模与制造方法，其柔性锥形主干由热塑性聚氨酯（TPU）制成。该可扩展设计集成电子基座，支持通过执行器和压缩力传感器直接控制肌腱张力。与许多单一用途且昂贵的连续体机器人不同，本设计强调可定制性、快速组装和低成本，同时通过几何锥形实现高曲率和末端高顺应性，支持广泛的柔性机器人检测与操作任务。我们基于Cosserat杆理论，采用牛顿方法开发了锥形主干的通用正向运动静力学模型，扩展了现有肌腱驱动Cosserat杆公式，明确考虑主干截面几何形状的空间变化。该模型捕捉锥形引起的渐变刚度特性，支持系统性探索配置空间随几何设计参数的变化。具体分析了主干锥角对机器人配置空间和可操作性的影响。模型通过运动捕捉数据验证，经通过线搜索校准杨氏模量以最小化建模误差后，可实现厘米级形状预测精度。我们进一步展示了通过内窥镜夹持器沿连续体机器人路径进行遥操作抓取，夹持器安装于6自由度机械臂上。提供参数化iLogic/CAD脚本，支持快速几何生成与缩放。该框架为使用熔融沉积建模3D打印机制造的锥形肌腱驱动连续体机器人，建立了从参数化设计到受控肌腱驱动的简单、快速、可重复的路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for customizable, low-cost, and high-compliance continuum robots for inspection and manipulation, this study designs and fabricates tendon-actuated robots with a tapered, flexible TPU backbone. The method integrates a scalable 3D-printed structure with embedded electronics for direct tendon control and sensing, and develops a generalized Cosserat rod model that accounts for spatially varying cross-sections to predict motion and stiffness. Experimental results show centimeter-level accuracy in shape prediction after calibration of Young’s modulus, and successful teleoperated grasping via an endoscopic gripper mounted on a 6-DoF arm, demonstrating the robot’s utility in compliant tasks.
为满足对可定制、低成本且高柔顺性的连续体机器人在检测与操作中的需求，本文设计并制造了具有锥形柔性聚合物背骨的腱驱动机器人，背骨材料为TPU。方法上，集成电子基座实现腱张力直接控制与传感，并基于牛顿力学构建广义Cosserat杆模型以精确模拟背骨截面随几何锥度变化的刚度分布。实验结果表明，经Young模量校准后，模型可实现厘米级形变预测精度，并成功实现通过6自由度机械臂远程操控的内窥镜夹持功能。参数化CAD脚本支持快速几何生成与缩放，为锥形腱驱动连续体机器人的快速制造与应用提供可重复路径。
Human-Centric Transferable Tactile Pre-Training for Dexterous Robotic Manipulation
Authors: Chi Zhang, Penglin Cai, Ziheng Xi, Haoqi Yuan, Hao Luo, Wanpeng Zhang, Sipeng Zheng, Chaoyi Xu, Zongqing Lu
First: 2026-07-01T15:26:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T15:26:26+00:00
Comments: The first two authors contribute equally. Orders are decided by flipping a coin
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As an essential modality for dexterous and contact-rich tasks, tactile sensing provides precise force feedback that cannot be reliably inferred from vision. However, limited by hardware and data collection systems, existing datasets with tactility remain small in scale and narrow in contact coverage. Meanwhile, Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models with tactile modality are constrained on dynamics-agnostic post-training, which limits the performance ceiling on downstream tasks. In this paper, we present H-Tac, a large-scale tactile-action dataset with 160-hour egocentric human videos containing more than 300 tasks and 135k episodes. Building upon this, we propose Transferable Tactile Pre-Training (TTP), a system of tactile-based pre-training on human data for fine-grained robotic tasks. To bridge the gap between humans and robots, we use unified tactile and action spaces throughout the pre-training and post-training phases, preserving prior knowledge during human-to-robot transfer. By leveraging a tactile expert for future tactile prediction, our framework explicitly models the contact dynamics and precise physical interactions. Extensive experiments in simulation and on real robots demonstrate that our model achieves superior performance, exhibiting robust generalization and fine-grained manipulation capabilities. TTP paves the way for scalable tactile pre-training via human-to-robot transfer.
中文标题/摘要
标题：以人为中心的可迁移触觉预训练用于灵巧机器人操作
作为灵巧且接触密集任务的关键模态，触觉感知提供视觉无法可靠推断的精确力反馈。然而，受限于硬件和数据采集系统，现有触觉数据集规模较小且接触覆盖范围狭窄。同时，带有触觉模态的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在动态无关的后训练中受限，限制了其在下游任务中的性能上限。本文提出H-Tac，一个包含160小时第一人称人类视频、超过300项任务和13.5万个场景的大规模触觉-动作数据集。在此基础上，我们提出可迁移触觉预训练（TTP），一种基于人类数据的触觉预训练系统，用于细粒度机器人任务。为弥合人机差距，我们在预训练和后训练阶段使用统一的触觉与动作空间，确保人类到机器人的知识迁移过程中保留先验知识。通过引入触觉专家进行未来触觉预测，我们的框架显式建模接触动力学和精确物理交互。在仿真和真实机器人上的大量实验表明，我们的模型表现卓越，具备鲁棒泛化能力和细粒度操作能力。TTP为人机触觉预训练的可扩展性铺平了道路。
