Motivated by the need for tactile data to enable dexterous robotic manipulation and the limitations of existing small-scale tactile datasets, this work introduces H-Tac, a large-scale tactile-action dataset derived from 160 hours of egocentric human videos covering over 300 tasks and 135k episodes. The authors propose Transferable Tactile Pre-Training (TTP), a method that pre-trains robotic models on human tactile-action data using unified tactile and action spaces to preserve knowledge during human-to-robot transfer. By incorporating a tactile expert for future tactile prediction, TTP explicitly models contact dynamics and physical interactions. Experiments in simulation and on real robots show that TTP achieves superior performance with robust generalization and fine-grained manipulation capabilities, demonstrating its effectiveness for scalable tactile pre-training.
为解决机器人精细操作中触觉反馈缺失及触觉数据稀缺的问题，本文提出H-Tac数据集，包含160小时第一人称人类视频，覆盖300余项任务和13.5万次操作。作者设计了可迁移触觉预训练框架TTP，通过统一触觉与动作空间，在人类数据上预训练并保留先验知识，实现人机迁移。引入触觉专家预测未来触觉，显式建模接触动力学。实验表明，TTP在仿真与真实机器人上均表现优异，具备强泛化能力与精细操作能力，验证了其通过人类数据实现可扩展触觉预训练的有效性。
GSRQ: Gain-Shape Residual Quantization for Sub-1-bit KV Cache
Authors: Soosung Kim, Minjae Park, Eui-Young Chung, Jaeyong Chung
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-07-01T15:25:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T15:25:21+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The deployment of Large Language Models (LLMs) with extended context windows is increasingly constrained by the linear growth of Key-Value (KV) cache memory. Vector Quantization (VQ), particularly Residual Quantization (RQ), is a promising approach for pushing KV cache storage toward the sub-1-bit regime by progressively encoding residuals with small codebooks. However, most VQ methods still rely on standard $\ell_2$ $K$-means as the core codebook-learning primitive. We identify a subtle high-dimensional issue of this primitive: Euclidean centroid averaging can induce centroid shrinkage, which weakens the angular alignment term in the $\ell_2$ distortion and makes directional preservation harder. To address this issue, we propose Gain-Shape $K$-means (GSKM), a drop-in replacement for $K$-means that improves directional fidelity while matching, and in some regimes improving, $\ell_2$ distortion. We then build Gain-Shape Residual Quantization (GSRQ) by incorporating a weighted extension of GSKM into an RQ pipeline. On LLaMA-3-8B, GSRQ substantially improves over strong KV cache quantization baselines across bit rates. At 1-bit, it improves the average accuracy across LongBench tasks from 11.34 to 33.54, a gain of 22.20 percentage points over VQLLM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GSRQ：用于亚1比特KV缓存的增益-形状残差量化
大型语言模型（LLM）在扩展上下文窗口部署时，其键值（KV）缓存内存呈线性增长，日益成为瓶颈。向量量化（VQ），特别是残差量化（RQ），通过逐步用小型码本编码残差，有望将KV缓存存储推向亚1比特范围。然而，大多数VQ方法仍依赖标准ℓ₂ K-均值作为核心码本学习原语。我们发现该原语在高维空间中存在一个微妙问题：欧氏质心平均会导致质心收缩，削弱ℓ₂失真中的角度对齐项，使方向保持更困难。为解决此问题，我们提出增益-形状K-均值（GSKM），作为K-均值的即插即用替代方案，可提升方向保真度，同时匹配甚至在某些情况下优于ℓ₂失真。随后，我们通过将GSKM的加权扩展整合进RQ流程，构建了增益-形状残差量化（GSRQ）。在LLaMA-3-8B上，GSRQ在各类比特率下均显著优于现有强KV缓存量化基线。在1比特时，其在LongBench任务上的平均准确率从11.34提升至33.54，相较VQLLM提升了22.20个百分点。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the memory bottleneck in KV cache for large language models with extended context windows, this work introduces GSRQ, a quantization method that leverages Gain-Shape K-means (GSKM) to improve directional fidelity in residual quantization. GSKM replaces standard K-means by avoiding centroid shrinkage in high dimensions, thereby preserving angular alignment and enhancing directional preservation without sacrificing $\ell_2$ distortion. When integrated into a residual quantization pipeline, GSRQ achieves superior performance over existing baselines: on LLaMA-3-8B at 1-bit, it boosts average LongBench accuracy from 11.34 to 33.54, a 22.20 percentage point improvement over VQLLM.
为缓解大语言模型在长上下文场景下KV缓存内存线性增长的问题，本文提出GSRQ方法，通过改进残差量化提升压缩效率。研究发现标准K-means在高维空间中存在因欧氏平均导致的聚类中心收缩问题，削弱方向保持能力。为此，提出Gain-Shape K-means（GSKM），在保持或提升ℓ₂失真同时增强方向保真度。GSRQ将GSKM集成到残差量化流程中，在LLaMA-3-8B上1比特下将LongBench平均准确率从11.34提升至33.54，较VQLLM提升22.20个百分点。
The Quadruped Soft Tail: Compliant Grasping and Swabbing for Contamination Surveys in Harsh Environments
Authors: Harald Minde Hansen, Nandita Gallacher, Kristin Y. Pettersen, Jan Tommy Gravdahl, Mario di Castro
First: 2026-06-29T20:41:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T15:24:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Beryllium contamination surveys in radioactive areas are challenging for robots in environments cluttered with cables and electronics. To address this problem, we have developed a novel quadruped system augmentation: A lightweight, soft, and compliant tendon-actuated robotic tail mounted on a quadruped robot. The tail features a hollow, flexible backbone and a tendon-actuated soft gripper that enables the robot to pick up sampling tissues, swab contaminated surfaces, and release the tissues at designated collection locations for subsequent beryllium analysis. To enable intuitive teleoperation, a closed-form kinematic model and a singularity-robust task-space controller are developed. Experimental results demonstrate that gripper actuation has a negligible effect on robot shape, while common-mode tendon actuation provides an effective mechanism for stiffness modulation and preload control. Furthermore, experimental validation indicates that the proposed kinematic model provides a suitable basis for real-time task-space control. The proposed system combines the agility of legged locomotion with the compliance of soft robotic manipulation, enabling the complete contamination-survey procedure to be performed without human exposure. While motivated by beryllium contamination surveys at CERN, the proposed quadruped soft-tail concept is broadly applicable to legged robots operating in cluttered, confined, or hazardous environments where conventional rigid-link manipulators are undesirable.
中文标题/摘要
标题：四足软尾系统：用于恶劣环境下污染检测的柔性抓取与擦拭
在放射性区域进行铍污染检测时，机器人在布满电缆和电子设备的环境中面临挑战。为解决此问题，我们开发了一种新型四足机器人增强系统：一种轻量、柔软且顺应性的肌腱驱动软尾。该尾部具有中空柔性脊柱和肌腱驱动的柔性夹爪，使机器人能够拾取采样材料、擦拭污染表面，并在指定收集点释放材料以供后续铍分析。为实现直观的遥操作，我们开发了闭式运动学模型和抗奇异性的任务空间控制器。实验结果表明，夹爪驱动对机器人形态影响极小，而共模肌腱驱动提供了一种有效的刚度调节和预加载控制机制。此外，实验验证表明，所提出的运动学模型可作为实时任务空间控制的良好基础。该系统结合了腿式移动的敏捷性与软体机器人操作的顺应性，使整个污染检测流程可在无人暴露的情况下完成。虽然该四足软尾概念源于CERN的铍污染检测需求，但其广泛适用于在复杂、狭窄或危险环境中运行的腿式机器人，这些环境不适合使用传统刚性连杆机械臂。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to perform beryllium contamination surveys in radioactive, cluttered environments without exposing humans, this work introduces a quadruped robot augmented with a lightweight, soft, tendon-actuated tail. The tail’s compliant gripper and flexible backbone allow it to grasp and swab surfaces while maintaining robot stability, and a closed-form kinematic model with a singularity-robust controller enables intuitive teleoperation. Experiments show that gripper actuation does not distort robot shape, common-mode tendon control effectively modulates stiffness and preload, and the kinematic model supports real-time task-space control. The system successfully completes contamination sampling tasks autonomously, combining legged mobility with soft manipulation for safe operation in hazardous settings.
为应对放射性环境中电缆和电子设备密集区域的铍污染采样难题，该研究提出一种四足机器人附加的轻质柔性尾部装置，通过肌腱驱动实现抓取与擦拭功能。尾部采用中空柔性骨架与软性夹爪，可在不干扰机器人步态的前提下完成采样操作；结合闭式运动学模型与奇异性鲁棒的任务空间控制器，实现直观遥操作。实验表明，夹爪动作对机器人形态影响极小，肌腱共模驱动可有效调节刚度与预载力，运动学模型支持实时控制，使机器人可独立完成污染检测全流程，无需人员暴露，该方案适用于各类复杂、受限或危险环境中的四足机器人作业。
Agentic generation of verifiable rules for deterministic, self-expanding reaction classification
Authors: Daniel Armstrong, Maarten Dobbelaere, Valentas Olikauskas, Helena Avila, Octavian Susanu, Jérôme Waser, Philippe Schwaller
First: 2026-07-01T15:24:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-01T15:24:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Computer-assisted synthesis planning breaks target molecules into accessible precursors using large libraries of reaction rules that assign each transformation a deterministic, interpretable label. But chemistry is long-tailed, making manual encoding intractable, and existing tools rely on fixed rulesets that cannot adapt to new chemistries. Here we present a fully automated pipeline in which a multi-agent framework of large language models (LLMs) classifies reactions and writes the rules themselves across 665,901 US patent reactions, generating each rule under a verification loop that tests it against the corpus. It expands a standard taxonomy from 68 to 14,073 classes without human curation. With a lightweight fingerprint classifier, it classifies 97.7\% of unseen reactions, matching a leading proprietary classifier while resolving chemistry more finely and extending on demand to chemistry outside its training distribution. The result is a living reactivity database and a general route to turning generative models into reliable, self-expanding symbolic systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于代理的可验证规则生成，用于确定性自扩展反应分类
计算机辅助合成规划通过大型反应规则库将目标分子分解为可获取的前体，每条规则赋予每个转化一个确定且可解释的标签。但化学领域长尾分布，使得人工编码不可行，现有工具依赖固定规则集，无法适应新化学。本文提出全自动流程，采用多代理框架的大语言模型（LLMs）对665,901个美国专利反应进行分类并自动生成规则，每条规则在验证循环中通过与语料库测试。该方法将标准分类体系从68类扩展至14,073类，无需人工校准。结合轻量指纹分类器，可对97.7%未见反应进行分类，性能媲美主流专有分类器，化学分辨率更精细，且可按需扩展至训练分布外的化学领域。结果是一个动态反应数据库，以及将生成模型转化为可靠自扩展符号系统的通用路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate reaction classification in chemistry and overcome the limitations of fixed, manually encoded rule systems, this work introduces a multi-agent framework using large language models to autonomously classify and generate verifiable reaction rules across a large corpus of US patent reactions. The system iteratively expands a reaction taxonomy from 68 to 14,073 classes through self-generated rules validated against the dataset, and employs a lightweight fingerprint classifier to achieve 97.7% accuracy on unseen reactions, outperforming existing tools in both precision and adaptability to novel chemistries.
为解决化学反应分类中人工编码不可持续、现有工具无法适应新化学的问题，该研究提出一种多智能体大语言模型框架，自动在66.5万条专利反应数据上生成并验证反应规则。系统在无需人工干预下将标准分类体系从68类扩展至14,073类，并通过轻量指纹分类器实现对未知反应97.7%的准确率，匹配主流专有工具，同时提供更精细的化学解析和对训练分布外化学的扩展能力。
